import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell'
import { useConversations } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
import { useHarness } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
import { useIdentity } from '@/hooks/useIdentity'
import { useRoute } from '@/lib/router'
import { ConversationPage } from '@/pages/ConversationPage'
import { HomePage } from '@/pages/HomePage'
import { ReplayPage } from '@/pages/ReplayPage'
import { RunPage } from '@/pages/RunPage'

/**
 * Routes (path based, no router dependency — see src/lib/router.ts):
 *   /                        new run (composer + scenarios)
 *   /conversations/:id       a conversation loaded from the SQLite store via REST; ?run=<id> picks a run
 *   /runs/:id                a run outside a conversation (harness without the store)
 *   /replay/:demoId          bundled recorded run
 */
export default function App() {
  const route = useRoute()
  const identity = useIdentity()
  const harness = useHarness()
  const conversations = useConversations(harness.client)

  let page: React.ReactNode
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'conversation':
      page = (
        <ConversationPage
          key={route.id}
          harness={harness}
          conversations={conversations}
          conversationId={route.id}
          runId={route.runId ?? null}
        />
      )
      break
    case 'run':
      page = <RunPage key={route.runId} harness={harness} runId={route.runId} />
      break
    case 'replay':
      page = <ReplayPage key={route.demoId} harness={harness} demoId={route.demoId} />
      break
    default:
      page = <HomePage harness={harness} conversations={conversations} />
  }

  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <AppShell harness={harness} conversations={conversations} route={route} identity={identity}>
        {page}
      </AppShell>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )
}
import { initTheme } from '@/lib/theme'
import { StrictMode } from 'react'
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
import './index.css'
import App from './App.tsx'

initTheme()
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
)
export { cn } from "cn"
/**
 * Path-based routes with no router dependency.
 *
 *   /                         home
 *   /conversations/:id[?run=] a persisted conversation, optionally focused on one of its runs
 *   /runs/:id                 a bare run (no conversation context — pre-Store harness)
 *   /replay/:demoId           a bundled replay, no backend
 *
 * The pre-Store query forms (`/?run=<id>`, `/?demo=<id>`, `/?c=<id>`) still parse so old links
 * keep working; hrefFor() always emits the path form. serve.py answers unknown extension-less
 * paths with index.html, so deep links survive a refresh.
 *
 * Navigation is pushState/replaceState plus a custom event, so every useRoute() instance and any
 * plain listener re-reads the location; the browser's own popstate (back/forward) is honoured too.
 */

import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'

import { getLogger } from './log'

const log = getLogger('web')

export type Route =
  | { kind: 'home' }
  | { kind: 'conversation'; id: string; runId?: string | null }
  | { kind: 'run'; runId: string }
  | { kind: 'replay'; demoId: string }

/** Dispatched on `window` after every in-app navigation (in addition to the native `popstate`). */
export const ROUTE_EVENT = 'faultline:route'

const HOME: Route = { kind: 'home' }

function decode(s: string): string {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(s)
  } catch {
    return s
  }
}

function segments(pathname: string): string[] {
  return pathname.split('/').filter((s) => s.length > 0).map(decode)
}

function params(search: string): URLSearchParams {
  return new URLSearchParams(search.startsWith('?') ? search.slice(1) : search)
}

export function parseRoute(pathname: string, search = ''): Route {
  const segs = segments(pathname || '/')
  const q = params(search)

  if (segs.length === 0) {
    // Legacy query-param routing.
    const c = q.get('c')
    const run = q.get('run')
    const demo = q.get('demo')
    if (c) return { kind: 'conversation', id: c, runId: run || null }
    if (run) return { kind: 'run', runId: run }
    if (demo) return { kind: 'replay', demoId: demo }
    return HOME
  }

  if (segs.length === 2) {
    const [head, id] = segs as [string, string]
    if (id.length === 0) return HOME
    if (head === 'conversations') return { kind: 'conversation', id, runId: q.get('run') || null }
    if (head === 'runs') return { kind: 'run', runId: id }
    if (head === 'replay') return { kind: 'replay', demoId: id }
  }

  return HOME
}

export function hrefFor(route: Route): string {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return '/'
    case 'conversation': {
      const base = `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(route.id)}`
      return route.runId ? `${base}?run=${encodeURIComponent(route.runId)}` : base
    }
    case 'run':
      return `/runs/${encodeURIComponent(route.runId)}`
    case 'replay':
      return `/replay/${encodeURIComponent(route.demoId)}`
  }
}

/** Structural equality, so callers can skip no-op navigations. */
export function sameRoute(a: Route, b: Route): boolean {
  return hrefFor(a) === hrefFor(b)
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- window binding

function locationKey(): string {
  return `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}`
}

/** The route the address bar currently shows. */
export function currentRoute(): Route {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return HOME
  return parseRoute(window.location.pathname, window.location.search)
}

export interface NavigateOptions {
  /** Replace the current history entry instead of pushing a new one. */
  replace?: boolean
}

export function navigate(route: Route, opts: NavigateOptions = {}): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
  const href = hrefFor(route)
  // Pushing an entry identical to the current one only makes Back a no-op; replace instead.
  const replace = opts.replace === true || href === locationKey()
  try {
    if (replace) window.history.replaceState({}, '', href)
    else window.history.pushState({}, '', href)
  } catch (err) {
    // history can throw in sandboxed/srcdoc frames; the in-memory route still updates below.
    log.warn('route.history_error', 'history update failed', { href, error: String(err) })
  }
  log.info('route.change', replace ? 'replaced' : 'navigated', { href, kind: route.kind })
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent<Route>(ROUTE_EVENT, { detail: route }))
}

/** Fires on back/forward and after navigate(). */
export function subscribeRoute(fn: () => void): () => void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {}
  window.addEventListener('popstate', fn)
  window.addEventListener(ROUTE_EVENT, fn)
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('popstate', fn)
    window.removeEventListener(ROUTE_EVENT, fn)
  }
}

// Snapshot must be referentially stable for an unchanged location, or useSyncExternalStore
// would re-render forever.
let cache: { key: string; route: Route } | null = null

function getSnapshot(): Route {
  const key = locationKey()
  if (cache && cache.key === key) return cache.route
  cache = { key, route: parseRoute(window.location.pathname, window.location.search) }
  return cache.route
}

function getServerSnapshot(): Route {
  return HOME
}

/** The current Route; re-renders on navigate() and on the browser's back/forward. */
export function useRoute(): Route {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribeRoute, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot)
}
# apps/web — Faultline browser UI

A chat-style viewer for Faultline runs: a Claude agent executes real shell commands in a Modal
Sandbox while the environment injects faults at the tool boundary. The UI shows every model turn,
every tool call with its output, the injected fault and whether the agent recovered, the workspace
diff, and the graded score — grouped into **conversations that belong to this browser**.

Static SPA, served by the Modal app `faultline-web` (Server `site`); talks to the harness API over
CORS. See `../../ARCHITECTURE.md` for the system design and `../../PLAN.md` §2.9–§2.10, §3.3 for
the plan.

## Stack

- Vite 8 · React 19 · TypeScript 6 · Tailwind v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`)
- shadcn (v4 CLI, style `base-nova` on Base UI) — primitives + the **sidebar block** (collapsible to icons)
- [Beautiful UI](https://www.beautifului.dev) (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine) — AI-native composites,
  copied and adapted into `src/components/bui/` (see its README/LICENSE there)
- vitest + Testing Library (jsdom)

**Node ≥ 22.12 is required** (Vite 8 / vitest 5). `.nvmrc` pins 22; the lockfile is pnpm v9
(`packageManager: pnpm@9.15.4`).

```bash
source ~/.nvm/nvm.sh && nvm use        # Node 22 from .nvmrc
corepack pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm dev                               # http://localhost:5173, harness URL from public/config.json
pnpm test                              # vitest run
pnpm build                             # tsc -b && vite build → dist/
```

## Routes (path based, `src/lib/router.ts`, no router dependency)

| Path | What |
|---|---|
| `/` | new run: composer (`/` picks a scenario, model + seed chips) and scenario cards |
| `/conversations/:id` | a conversation loaded from the SQLite store via `GET /conversations/{id}`; `?run=<id>` selects a run |
| `/runs/:id` | a run outside a conversation (harness build without the store); upgrades itself when the record carries `conversation_id` |
| `/replay/:demoId` | bundled recorded run from `public/demo/*.json`, no backend |

`serve.py` answers extension-less paths with `index.html`, so deep links work on Modal.

## Data flow

- **Identity** — `src/lib/identity.ts` mints `u_<uuid4>` once per browser (localStorage, cookie
  mirror) and every request carries `X-Faultline-User`. Conversations are scoped to it. Scoping,
  not auth.
- **Conversations** — `GET /conversations` fills the sidebar; `GET /conversations/{id}` returns
  `{conversation, runs, messages}`; finished runs render from the persisted `messages/blocks`
  projection (the top-bar chip says `sqlite`). If the harness has no such routes yet (404) the UI
  says "history unavailable" and runs still work through `POST /runs`.
- **Live runs** — seed from `GET /runs/{id}`, then tail `GET /runs/{id}/events` (SSE, resumes with
  `Last-Event-ID`, ~110 s rotations, polling fallback). `src/lib/reducer.ts` folds events into the
  view state; `src/lib/transcript.ts` maps either source onto one transcript model.
- **Config** — harness URL precedence: `window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__` → `/config.json` (written by
  `serve.py` from `$HARNESS_URL` at container start) → `VITE_HARNESS_URL` → built-in default.

## Layout

```
src/
  App.tsx                       route switch inside the sidebar shell
  components/app-sidebar.tsx    conversations (grouped by day) · replays · identity menu
  components/layout/            AppShell (SidebarProvider), TopBar (breadcrumb + status chips), Link
  components/transcript/        TranscriptView: task bubble → turns (AssistantText, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips) → ScoreRows
  components/workspace/         Files / Diffs / Timeline / Logs column (sheet on narrow screens)
  components/bui/               Beautiful UI ports (AssistantText, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips, CodeBlock, ScoreRows, LoadingState, FileStatusTable, Composer)
  components/ui/                shadcn primitives + sidebar block
  pages/                        HomePage, ConversationPage, RunPage, ReplayPage
  hooks/                        useHarness, useIdentity, useConversations, useConversation, useRunView, useStartRun
  lib/                          api, config, identity, router, reducer, transcript, replay, format, log, types
public/demo/                    bundled replays (export with ../../scripts/export_demo.py)
modal_app.py · serve.py         Modal Server hosting (prebuilt dist/ or in-image Node 22 build)
```

## Deploy

```bash
pnpm build
HARNESS_URL=https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run \
  modal deploy -e local apps/web/modal_app.py
# → https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.modal.run
```

      (function () {
        try {
          var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('theme');
          var t = (q === 'light' || q === 'dark' || q === 'system') ? q : localStorage.getItem('faultline.theme');
          if (t !== 'light' && t !== 'dark') t = 'system';
          var dark = t === 'dark' || (t === 'system' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
          var root = document.documentElement;
          root.classList.toggle('dark', dark);
          root.style.colorScheme = dark ? 'dark' : 'light';
          root.dataset.theme = t;
        } catch (e) {}
      })();

mkdir -p /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/522d0edc-be20-407e-a1e7-3a9776da8b50/scratchpad && cat > /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/522d0edc-be20-407e-a1e7-3a9776da8b50/scratchpad/graph.py <<'EOF'
import os,re,sys
root='/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web/src'
files=[]
for d,_,fs in os.walk(root):
    for f in fs:
        if f.endswith(('.ts','.tsx')): files.append(os.path.join(d,f))
imp=re.compile(r"""(?:from|import)\s*\(?\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]""")
def resolve(src,spec):
    if spec.startswith('@/'): p=os.path.join(root,spec[2:])
    elif spec.startswith('.'): p=os.path.normpath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(src),spec))
    else: return None
    for c in [p,p+'.ts',p+'.tsx',os.path.join(p,'index.ts'),os.path.join(p,'index.tsx')]:
        if os.path.isfile(c): return c
    return 'UNRESOLVED:'+spec
users={f:[] for f in files}
ext={}
for f in files:
    s=open(f).read()
    for m in imp.findall(s):
        r=resolve(f,m)
        if r is None: ext.setdefault(m,[]).append(os.path.relpath(f,root))
        elif r.startswith('UNRESOLVED'): print('UNRESOLVED',f,m)
        else: users.setdefault(r,[]).append(os.path.relpath(f,root))
for f in sorted(files):
    u=users[f]; nt=[x for x in u if '.test.' not in x]
    print(f"{os.path.relpath(f,root):45s} prod={len(nt)} test={len(u)-len(nt)} :: {', '.join(u)}")
print('\n--- external imports')
for k in sorted(ext): print(k, '<-', len(ext[k]), ext[k][:4])
EOF

cd /Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web && source ~/.nvm/nvm.sh && nvm use 22 >/dev/null; rtk proxy pnpm exec tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit; echo "tsc exit=$?"; rtk proxy pnpm exec oxlint 2>&1 | grep -E "Found|warnings|errors" | tail -3; rtk proxy grep -rnE "ui/(card|collapsible|scroll-area|select)['\"]" src; echo ---; cat > /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/522d0edc-be20-407e-a1e7-3a9776da8b50/scratchpad/exports.py <<'EOF'
import os,re
root='/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web/src'
files=[]
for d,_,fs in os.walk(root):
    for f in fs:
        if f.endswith(('.ts','.tsx')): files.append(os.path.join(d,f))
src={f:open(f).read() for f in files}
ex=re.compile(r"^export\s+(?:default\s+)?(?:async\s+)?(?:function|const|let|type|interface|class|enum)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)",re.M)
ex2=re.compile(r"^export\s*\{([^}]*)\}",re.M)
for f in sorted(files):
    if '/ui/' in f: continue
    names=ex.findall(src[f])
    for m in ex2.findall(src[f]):
        if 'from' in m: continue
        names += [n.strip().split(' as ')[-1].strip() for n in m.split(',') if n.strip()]
    for n in names:
        w=re.compile(r"\b"+re.escape(n)+r"\b")
        prod=[o for o in files if o!=f and '.test.' not in o and 'test-fixtures' not in o and w.search(src[o]) and not o.endswith('index.ts')]
        test=[o for o in files if o!=f and ('.test.' in o or 'test-fixtures' in o) and w.search(src[o])]
        own=len(w.findall(src[f]))
        if not prod:
            print(f"{os.path.relpath(f,root)}: {n}  own_refs={own} tests={[os.path.basename(t) for t in test]}")
EOF

     1	/**
     2	 * Wire contracts mirrored from packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py.
     3	 *
     4	 * Keep field names identical to the pydantic models — the harness serialises those models
     5	 * straight onto the wire. If a field is missing here it is simply not rendered; if a field is
     6	 * added here it MUST be optional so an older harness still type-checks at runtime.
     7	 */
     8	
     9	export type FaultKind = 'missing_file' | 'denied_write' | 'ack_lost'
    10	export type FaultMode = 'sticky' | 'transient'
    11	
    12	export interface FaultSpec {
    13	  kind: FaultKind
    14	  path: string
    15	  mode: FaultMode
    16	  hits: number | null
    17	  delay_ms: number
    18	}
    19	
    20	export type HarnessFaultKind = 'worker_crash' | 'transport_abort'
    21	
    22	/**
    23	 * A REAL interruption the harness inflicts on itself at a chosen call (chaos trigger).
    24	 *   worker_crash    : the run_episode process exits hard after dispatching the call; the side
    25	 *                     effect completes at sandbox-env and a fresh worker resumes the run.
    26	 *   transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side; the server still
    27	 *                     completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
    28	 */
    29	export interface HarnessFault {
    30	  kind: HarnessFaultKind
    31	  tool: ToolName
    32	  path: string
    33	  nth: number
    34	  after_ms: number
    35	}
    36	
    37	export interface CheckSpec {
    38	  id: string
    39	  description: string
    40	  weight: number
    41	}
    42	
    43	/** A fault class in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing. */
    44	export interface FaultPublic {
    45	  kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
    46	  origin: ErrorOrigin
    47	  layer: ErrorLayer
    48	  description: string
    49	}
    50	
    51	export interface Scenario {
    52	  id: string
    53	  title: string
    54	  description: string
    55	  task_prompt: string
    56	  max_steps: number
    57	  fault_kinds: FaultKind[]
    58	  /** Planned REAL interruptions (the UI may say "includes a real harness interruption"). Older harness: absent. */
    59	  harness_faults?: HarnessFault[]
    60	  /** What the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight). Older harness: absent. */
    61	  checks?: CheckSpec[]
    62	  faults_public?: FaultPublic[]
    63	}
    64	
    65	export type ToolName = 'run_command' | 'read_file' | 'write_file' | 'list_dir'
    66	
    67	/**
    68	 * OS/HTTP-style codes the agent sees (identical whether injected or real) plus the real-failure
    69	 * codes only the layer that really failed produces. Mirrors `ErrorCode` in schemas.py.
    70	 */
    71	export type ErrorCode =
    72	  | 'ENOENT' // no such file or directory
    73	  | 'EACCES' // permission denied
    74	  | 'ETIMEDOUT' // the response never arrived (ack lost, or a real client timeout)
    75	  | 'EINVAL' // bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
    76	  | 'ESANDBOX' // the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable
    77	  | 'ETRANSPORT' // harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure
    78	  | 'EHARNESS' // the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
    79	  | 'EMODEL' // Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
    80	  | 'EGYM' // gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
    81	  | 'EINTERNAL' // unexpected exception inside sandbox-env
    82	  | 'ENOEPISODE' // missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
    83	
    84	export const ERROR_CODES: readonly ErrorCode[] = [
    85	  'ENOENT',
    86	  'EACCES',
    87	  'ETIMEDOUT',
    88	  'EINVAL',
    89	  'ESANDBOX',
    90	  'ETRANSPORT',
    91	  'EHARNESS',
    92	  'EMODEL',
    93	  'EGYM',
    94	  'EINTERNAL',
    95	  'ENOEPISODE',
    96	]
    97	
    98	/** WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do. */
    99	export type ErrorOrigin =
   100	  | 'injected' // intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
   101	  | 'staged' // the scenario really changed the world at reset; the error is a real OS error
   102	  | 'real' // unplanned failure of a real component
   103	
   104	export const ERROR_ORIGINS: readonly ErrorOrigin[] = ['injected', 'staged', 'real']
   105	
   106	/** WHICH layer really failed. */
   107	export type ErrorLayer = 'filesystem' | 'sandbox' | 'transport' | 'harness' | 'model' | 'gym' | 'boundary'
   108	
   109	export const ERROR_LAYERS: readonly ErrorLayer[] = [
   110	  'filesystem',
   111	  'sandbox',
   112	  'transport',
   113	  'harness',
   114	  'model',
   115	  'gym',
   116	  'boundary',
   117	]
   118	
   119	/**
   120	 * Differentiated classification of one failure (docs/error-taxonomy.md). `label` is a fixed
   121	 * string the UI renders verbatim.
   122	 */
   123	export interface ErrorClass {
   124	  origin: ErrorOrigin
   125	  layer: ErrorLayer
   126	  code: ErrorCode
   127	  /** injected/staged only: which fault kind */
   128	  kind?: FaultKind | null
   129	  label: string
   130	  /** false when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption) */
   131	  outcome_known: boolean
   132	  /** ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run? */
   133	  side_effect_applied?: boolean | null
   134	  detail?: string | null
   135	}

   141	  detail?: string | null
   142	}

   144	export interface FaultFired {
   145	  step: number
   146	  kind: FaultKind
   147	  path: string
   148	  mode: FaultMode
   149	  /** injected (intercepted) or staged (sticky missing_file hit for real). Schema default: injected. */
   150	  origin?: ErrorOrigin
   151	  /** boundary for injected, filesystem for staged. Schema default: boundary. */
   152	  layer?: ErrorLayer
   153	  /** One plain-language sentence for the UI. */
   154	  description?: string
   155	}

   251	export type RunStatus =
   252	  | 'queued'
   253	  | 'running'
   254	  | 'ok' // agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
   255	  | 'truncated' // step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
   256	  | 'unevaluated' // loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
   257	  | 'interrupted' // a real interruption ended the run without resume
   258	  | 'error' // the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
   259	
   260	/** A real interruption observed by the harness (origin is always real). */
   261	export interface Interruption {
   262	  step?: number | null
   263	  layer: ErrorLayer
   264	  code: ErrorCode
   265	  label: string
   266	  /** the in-flight tool call, if any */
   267	  tool_use_id?: string | null
   268	  tool?: ToolName | null
   269	  path?: string | null
   270	  outcome_known: boolean
   271	  /** true when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure) */
   272	  planned: boolean
   273	  /** a new worker picked the run up afterwards */
   274	  resumed: boolean
   275	  /** which worker observed it */
   276	  worker_generation: number
   277	  at: string
   278	  detail?: string | null
   279	}
   280	
   281	/** How a tool call actually ended, as the harness reports it on `tool.result.data.outcome`. */
   282	export type ToolOutcome =
   283	  | 'executed' // the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
   284	  | 'failed' // the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
   285	  | 'not_executed' // refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it
   286	  | 'unknown' // may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:17.022Z
     1	"""Shared wire contracts for Faultline (pydantic v2).
     2	
     3	Three consumers: services/sandbox-env (gym + MCP), services/agent-harness (model loop + API),
     4	scripts/ (smoke + evidence). apps/web mirrors these in src/lib/types.ts — keep both in sync.
     5	
     6	Sections: faults & scenarios · MCP tool I/O · ledger · gym REST · harness runs/events · persistence · health.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	from typing import Any, Literal
    12	
    13	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    14	
    15	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- faults & scenarios
    16	
    17	FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
    18	FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]
    19	
    20	
    21	class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
    22	    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).
    23	
    24	    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
    25	    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
    26	    """
    27	
    28	    kind: FaultKind
    29	    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
    30	    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
    31	    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
    32	    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")
    33	
    34	
    35	class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
    36	    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
    37	
    38	
    39	HarnessFaultKind = Literal["worker_crash", "transport_abort"]
    40	
    41	
    42	class Scenario(BaseModel):
    43	    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""
    44	
    45	    id: str
    46	    title: str
    47	    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
    48	    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
    49	    max_steps: int = 20
    50	    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")
    51	    harness_faults: list["HarnessFault"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="REAL interruptions the harness inflicts on itself (see HarnessFault)")
    52	    checks: list["CheckSpec"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="what the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight)")
    53	    faults_public: list["FaultPublic"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="fault classes in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing")
    54	
    55	
    56	class CheckSpec(BaseModel):
    57	    id: str
    58	    description: str
    59	    weight: float = 1.0
    60	
    61	
    62	class FaultPublic(BaseModel):
    63	    kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
    64	    origin: ErrorOrigin
    65	    layer: ErrorLayer
    66	    description: str
    67	
    68	
    69	class HarnessFault(BaseModel):
    70	    """A real interruption the harness inflicts on ITSELF at a chosen call (chaos trigger, real failure mode).
    71	
    72	    worker_crash    : after dispatching the matching tool call and waiting `after_ms`, the run_episode
    73	                      process exits hard (os._exit) — the request is already at sandbox-env, so the
    74	                      side effect completes; a fresh worker resumes the run from the persisted events.
    75	    transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server
    76	                      still completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
    77	    """
    78	
    79	    kind: HarnessFaultKind
    80	    tool: ToolName = "write_file"
    81	    path: str
    82	    nth: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, description="fire on the nth matching call")
    83	    after_ms: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, description="delay after dispatch before interrupting (lets the request reach the server)")
    84	
    85	
    86	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O
    87	
    88	ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
    89	ErrorCode = Literal[
    90	    # OS/HTTP-style codes the AGENT sees (the same text whether injected or real):
    91	    "ENOENT",      # no such file or directory
    92	    "EACCES",      # permission denied
    93	    "ETIMEDOUT",   # the response never arrived (ack lost); the operation MAY have completed
    94	    "EINVAL",      # bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
    95	    # Real-failure codes (never injected; only the layer that really failed produces them):
    96	    "ESANDBOX",    # the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable (terminated, not found, exec failed)
    97	    "ETRANSPORT",  # harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure (connection reset, 5xx, client abort)
    98	    "EHARNESS",    # the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
    99	    "EMODEL",      # Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
   100	    "EGYM",        # gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
   101	    "EINTERNAL",   # unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail)
   102	    "ENOEPISODE",  # missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
   103	]
   104	
   105	# Provenance: WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do.
   106	ErrorOrigin = Literal[
   107	    "injected",  # intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
   108	    "staged",    # the scenario really changed the world at reset (e.g. deleted a file); the error is a real OS error
   109	    "real",      # unplanned failure of a real component (sandbox, transport, harness, model, gym, filesystem)
   110	]
   111	ErrorLayer = Literal["filesystem", "sandbox", "transport", "harness", "model", "gym", "boundary"]
   112	
   113	
   114	class ErrorClass(BaseModel):
   115	    """Differentiated classification of one failure, for humans and the UI (never shown to the agent).
   116	
   117	    Fixed `label` vocabulary (apps/web renders these verbatim; see docs/error-taxonomy.md):
   118	      injected/boundary  "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)"
   119	                         "simulated: write denied (nothing written)"
   120	                         "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)"
   121	      staged/filesystem  "staged: file absent since reset"
   122	      real/filesystem    "real: OS error in sandbox"
   123	      real/sandbox       "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable"
   124	      real/transport     "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)"
   125	      real/harness       "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)"
   126	      real/model         "real: model API error"
   127	      real/gym           "real: gym control-plane failure"
   128	      real/boundary      "real: internal error in sandbox-env"
   129	    """
   130	
   131	    origin: ErrorOrigin
   132	    layer: ErrorLayer
   133	    code: ErrorCode
   134	    kind: FaultKind | None = Field(default=None, description="injected/staged only: which fault kind")
   135	    label: str
   136	    outcome_known: bool = Field(description="False when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption)")
   137	    side_effect_applied: bool | None = Field(default=None, description="ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run?")
   138	    detail: str | None = None
   139	
   140	
   141	STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
   142	CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file
   143

   300	
   301	ToolOutcome = Literal[
   302	    "executed",      # the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
   303	    "failed",        # the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
   304	    "not_executed",  # refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it (injected EACCES/ENOENT, EINVAL, ESANDBOX)
   305	    "unknown",       # may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)
   306	]
   307	
   308	EventType = Literal[
   309	    "run.started",
   310	    "episode.reset",
   311	    "turn.text",
   312	    "tool.call",
   313	    "tool.result",
   314	    "fault.fired",
   315	    "workspace.diff",
   316	    "episode.evaluated",
   317	    "run.finished",
   318	    "log",
   319	    "llm.call",
   320	    "turn.thinking",
   321	    "interruption",
   322	    "run.resumed",
   323	    "episode.sandbox",
   324	]

   326	
   327	class Event(BaseModel):
   328	    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
   329	    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.
   330	
   331	    data shapes by type:
   332	      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
   333	      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt, sandbox_id (short), attempt (1 = first provisioning)}
   334	      turn.text         {text}
   335	      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
   336	      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired,
   337	                         outcome: executed|failed|not_executed|unknown, error_class?: ErrorClass (iff is_error),
   338	                         error_code?: str (compat), attempts: int (harness retries of this call; 1 = none),
   339	                         sandbox: {id: short, alive: bool}}
   340	      fault.fired       FaultFired
   341	      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
   342	      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
   343	      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str,
   344	                         evaluation_status: ok|failed|skipped, evaluation_error?: str, steps, score?,
   345	                         error_class?: ErrorClass (present whenever status is unevaluated|interrupted|error)}
   346	      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
   347	      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
   348	      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
   349	      interruption      Interruption   (a REAL interruption: origin is always "real")
   350	      run.resumed       {worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}
   351	      episode.sandbox   {sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated|replaced, reason, step}   (the environment noticed the worker changed)
   352	    Every tool.result with is_error carries data.error_class: ErrorClass; every fault.fired carries origin.
   353	    """
   354	
   355	    id: int
   356	    ts: str
   357	    run_id: str
   358	    type: EventType
   359	    step: int | None = None
   360	    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
   361	
   362	
   363	RunStatus = Literal[
   364	    "queued",
   365	    "running",
   366	    "ok",           # agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
   367	    "truncated",    # step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
   368	    "unevaluated",  # loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
   369	    "interrupted",  # a real interruption ended the run without resume (sandbox gone, worker died, ...)
   370	    "error",        # the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
   371	]
   372

   383	    path: str | None = None
   384	    outcome_known: bool = False
   385	    planned: bool = Field(default=False, description="True when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure)")
   386	    resumed: bool = Field(default=False, description="a new worker picked the run up afterwards")
   387	    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="which worker observed it")
   388	    at: str
   389	    detail: str | None = None

services/agent-harness/harness/loop.py:355:            "episode.reset",
     1	/**
     2	 * Typed harness client: plain cross-origin JSON fetches plus a resilient run subscription.
     3	 *
     4	 * Reliability model (this is the interesting part):
     5	 *  - The harness closes every SSE stream at ~110 s to stay under Modal's 150 s request cap, so a
     6	 *    clean close mid-run is NORMAL and must not be treated as a failure. The server announces it
     7	 *    with `event: done` + `{reason:"window"}`; an older harness just closes, and we distinguish
     8	 *    that from a real failure by whether the stream delivered anything first.
     9	 *  - EventSource cannot set headers, so the resume point is passed both natively (the browser
    10	 *    sends `Last-Event-ID` on its own reconnect) and as a `last_event_id` query param on ours.
    11	 *    For the same reason the SSE request carries no `X-Faultline-User`; ownership is enforced on
    12	 *    the JSON routes, and a run id is unguessable.
    13	 *  - After two consecutive failed opens we stop trusting SSE and fall back to polling
    14	 *    GET /runs/{id} every 1.5 s, which is strictly less efficient but works through anything.
    15	 *  - No credentials are ever sent: the web origin and the harness origin are different modal.run
    16	 *    subdomains and these are ordinary CORS requests. Identity travels as a header instead
    17	 *    (ARCHITECTURE.md §6).
    18	 */
    19

    64	export class HarnessError extends Error {
    65	  constructor(
    66	    message: string,
    67	    readonly status?: number,
    68	    readonly url?: string,
    69	  ) {
    70	    super(message)
    71	    this.name = 'HarnessError'
    72	  }
    73	}
    74	
    75	/** True for a HarnessError carrying HTTP 404 — the harness's "not yours / not there / not shipped yet". */
    76	export function isNotFound(err: unknown): boolean {
    77	  return err instanceof HarnessError && err.status === 404
    78	}
    79	
    80	type HeaderMap = Record<string, string>
    81	
    82	function toHeaderMap(h: HeadersInit | undefined): HeaderMap {
    83	  if (!h) return {}
    84	  if (Array.isArray(h)) return Object.fromEntries(h)
    85	  if (typeof Headers !== 'undefined' && h instanceof Headers) {
    86	    const out: HeaderMap = {}
    87	    h.forEach((v, k) => {
    88	      out[k] = v
    89	    })
    90	    return out
    91	  }
    92	  return { ...(h as HeaderMap) }
    93	}
    94	
    95	async function jsonFetch<T>(
    96	  url: string,
    97	  init: RequestInit = {},
    98	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
    99	  headers: HeaderMap = {},
   100	): Promise<T> {
   101	  const t0 = Date.now()
   102	  let res: Response
   103	  try {
   104	    res = await fetchImpl(url, {
   105	      ...init,
   106	      credentials: 'omit',
   107	      headers: { accept: 'application/json', ...headers, ...toHeaderMap(init.headers) },
   108	    })
   109	  } catch (err) {
   110	    log.error('http.error', 'request failed', { url, error: String(err) })
   111	    throw new HarnessError(`network error contacting ${url}: ${String(err)}`, undefined, url)
   112	  }
   113	  const durMs = Date.now() - t0
   114	  if (!res.ok) {
   115	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => '')
   116	    // 404 is an expected answer for routes a harness build may not have yet (/me, /conversations).
   117	    log[res.status === 404 ? 'warn' : 'error']('http.response', 'non-2xx', {
   118	      url,
   119	      status: res.status,
   120	      dur_ms: durMs,
   121	    })
   122	    throw new HarnessError(
   123	      `${res.status} ${res.statusText} from ${url}${body ? `: ${body.slice(0, 400)}` : ''}`,
   124	      res.status,
   125	      url,
   126	    )
   127	  }
   128	  log.debug('http.response', 'ok', { url, status: res.status, dur_ms: durMs })
   129	  return (await res.json()) as T
   130	}
   131	
   132	/**
   133	 * PLAN.md 2.3 specifies `GET /scenarios -> [Scenario]`, but the deployed harness answers
   134	 * `{"scenarios": [...]}`. Rather than break on either, accept both shapes here — this is the one
   135	 * place in the app that has to know, and a client that tolerates an envelope costs nothing.
   136	 * Logged as a contract deviation.
   137	 */
   138	export function normaliseScenarios(body: unknown): Scenario[] {
   139	  if (Array.isArray(body)) return body as Scenario[]
   140	  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {

   143	      log.debug('scenarios.envelope', 'harness returned {scenarios: [...]}, unwrapping', {

   155	/** Same tolerance as normaliseScenarios, for any list route: bare array or `{<key>: [...]}`. */
   156	function normaliseList<T>(body: unknown, key: string): T[] {
   157	  if (Array.isArray(body)) return body as T[]
   158	  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
   159	    const inner = (body as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
   160	    if (Array.isArray(inner)) {
   161	      log.debug(`${key}.envelope`, `harness returned {${key}: [...]}, unwrapping`, { count: inner.length })
   162	      return inner as T[]
   163	    }
   164	  }
   165	  log.warn(`${key}.shape`, `unrecognised /${key} payload; treating as empty`, { got: typeof body })
   166	  return []
   167	}

   169	export interface HarnessClientOptions {
   170	  /** Anonymous browser id (identity.ts). Sent as `X-Faultline-User` on every request when set. */
   171	  userId?: string | null
   172	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
   173	}
   174	
   175	export class HarnessClient {
   176	  readonly base: string
   177	  readonly userId: string | null
   178	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch
   179	
   180	  /**
   181	   * `new HarnessClient(base, { userId, fetchImpl })`. The older positional form
   182	   * `new HarnessClient(base, fetchImpl)` is still accepted.
   183	   */
   184	  constructor(base: string, opts: HarnessClientOptions | typeof fetch = {}) {
   185	    this.base = base
   186	    if (typeof opts === 'function') {
   187	      this.fetchImpl = opts
   188	      this.userId = null
   189	    } else {
   190	      this.fetchImpl = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch
   191	      this.userId = opts.userId ?? null
   192	    }
   193	  }
   194	
   195	  /** Headers every JSON request carries (identity). Also used by the polling fallback. */
   196	  headers(): HeaderMap {
   197	    return this.userId ? { [USER_HEADER]: this.userId } : {}
   198	  }
   199	
   200	  private get<T>(path: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
   201	    return jsonFetch<T>(`${this.base}${path}`, { signal }, this.fetchImpl, this.headers())
   202	  }
   203	
   204	  private send<T>(method: 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE', path: string, body?: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
   205	    return jsonFetch<T>(
   206	      `${this.base}${path}`,
   207	      {
   208	        method,
   209	        ...(body === undefined
   210	          ? {}
   211	          : { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
   212	        signal,
   213	      },
   214	      this.fetchImpl,
   215	      this.headers(),
   216	    )
   217	  }
   218	
   219	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- health / catalogue
   220	
   221	  health(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Health> {
   222	    return this.get<Health>('/health', signal)
   223	  }
   224	
   225	  async scenarios(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Scenario[]> {
   226	    const body = await this.get<Scenario[] | { scenarios?: Scenario[] }>('/scenarios', signal)
   227	    return normaliseScenarios(body)
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- runs
   231	
   232	  createRun(req: RunRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<CreateRunResponse> {
   233	    log.info('run.create', 'POST /runs', {
   234	      scenario_id: req.scenario_id,
   235	      model: req.model ?? null,
   236	    })
   237	    return this.send<CreateRunResponse>('POST', '/runs', req, signal)
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  getRun(runId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RunRecord> {
   241	    return this.get<RunRecord>(`/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`, signal)
   242	  }
   243	
   244	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- identity / conversations
   245	  // These routes ship with the harness Store. Until then they 404; callers use isNotFound() to
   246	  // treat that as "history unavailable" rather than an error.
   247	
   248	  me(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<MeResponse> {
   249	    return this.get<MeResponse>('/me', signal)
   250	  }
   251	
   252	  async listConversations(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ConversationSummary[]> {
   253	    const body = await this.get<ConversationSummary[] | { conversations?: ConversationSummary[] }>(
   254	      '/conversations',
   255	      signal,
   256	    )
   257	    return normaliseList(body, 'conversations')
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  getConversation(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ConversationDetail> {
   261	    return this.get<ConversationDetail>(`/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, signal)
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  createConversation(req: CreateConversationRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Conversation> {
   265	    log.info('conversation.create', 'POST /conversations', { scenario_id: req.scenario_id })
   266	    return this.send<Conversation>('POST', '/conversations', req, signal)
   267	  }
   268	
   269	  createConversationRun(
   270	    id: string,
   271	    req: ConversationRunRequest,
   272	    signal?: AbortSignal,
   273	  ): Promise<ConversationRunResponse> {
   274	    log.info('conversation.run', 'POST /conversations/{id}/runs', {
   275	      conversation_id: id,
   276	      model: req.model ?? null,
   277	    })
   278	    return this.send<ConversationRunResponse>(
   279	      'POST',
   280	      `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/runs`,
   281	      req,
   282	      signal,
   283	    )
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  updateConversation(
   287	    id: string,
   288	    patch: UpdateConversationRequest,
   289	    signal?: AbortSignal,
   290	  ): Promise<Conversation> {
   291	    return this.send<Conversation>('PATCH', `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, patch, signal)
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  /** Soft archive (the harness never hard-deletes). */
   295	  archiveConversation(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ArchiveResponse> {
   296	    log.info('conversation.archive', 'DELETE /conversations/{id}', { conversation_id: id })
   297	    return this.send<ArchiveResponse>('DELETE', `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, undefined, signal)
   298	  }

   301	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ run subscription
   302	
   303	export type TransportMode = 'sse' | 'poll' | 'closed'
   304	
   305	export interface TransportState {
   306	  mode: TransportMode
   307	  /** Consecutive failed SSE opens. Reset whenever a stream delivers an event. */
   308	  errors: number
   309	  detail?: string
   310	}
   311	
   312	export interface SubscribeOptions {
   313	  base: string
   314	  runId: string
   315	  /** Resume point; -1 means "from the beginning". */
   316	  lastEventId?: number
   317	  onEvents: (events: Event[]) => void
   318	  onRecord?: (record: RunRecord) => void
   319	  onTransport?: (state: TransportState) => void
   320	  onDone?: () => void
   321	  /** Extra headers for the polling fetch (identity). EventSource cannot carry them. */
   322	  headers?: Record<string, string>
   323	  /** Injection points for tests. */
   324	  eventSourceImpl?: typeof EventSource | null
   325	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
   326	  pollIntervalMs?: number
   327	  reconnectDelayMs?: number
   328	  maxSseErrors?: number
   329	  setTimeoutImpl?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown
   330	  clearTimeoutImpl?: (handle: unknown) => void
   331	}
   332	
   333	export interface Subscription {
   334	  close: () => void
   335	  /** Test/debug introspection. */
   336	  state: () => TransportState
   337	}
   338	
   339	function parseEvent(raw: string): Event | null {
   340	  try {
   341	    const v = JSON.parse(raw) as Event
   342	    return typeof v?.id === 'number' && typeof v?.type === 'string' ? v : null
   343	  } catch {
   344	    return null
   345	  }
   346	}
   347	
   348	/** Payload of the server's `event: done` frame. Older harness builds send no data at all. */
   349	export interface DonePayload {
   350	  reason?: 'finished' | 'window' | string
   351	  status?: RunStatus | string | null
   352	  last_event_id?: number
   353	  run_id?: string
   354	}
   355	
   356	export function parseDone(raw: unknown): DonePayload | null {
   357	  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) return null
   358	  try {
   359	    const v = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown
   360	    return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as DonePayload) : null
   361	  } catch {
   362	    return null
   363	  }
   364	}
   365	
   366	/**
   367	 * Does this `done` frame end the run, or merely the current ~110 s window?
   368	 * Only an explicit `reason: "window"` with a non-terminal status means "reconnect"; anything
   369	 * ambiguous (no data, unparseable, unknown reason, terminal status) is treated as finished, which
   370	 * is the safe default — a wrongly-ended subscription can be re-opened, a wrongly-kept one spins.
   371	 */
   372	export function doneMeansReconnect(payload: DonePayload | null): boolean {
   373	  if (!payload) return false
   374	  if (payload.reason !== 'window') return false
   375	  return !isTerminal((payload.status ?? null) as RunStatus | null)
   376	}
   377	
   378	/**
   379	 * Tail a run. Returns immediately; every update arrives through the callbacks.
   380	 * Always call `close()` — it tears down the EventSource and any pending timer.
   381	 */
   382	export function subscribeRun(opts: SubscribeOptions): Subscription {
   383	  const {
   384	    base,
   385	    runId,
   386	    onEvents,
   387	    onRecord,
   388	    onTransport,
   389	    onDone,
   390	    headers = {},
   391	    fetchImpl = typeof fetch !== 'undefined' ? fetch : undefined,
   392	    pollIntervalMs = 1500,
   393	    reconnectDelayMs = 1000,
   394	    maxSseErrors = 2,
   395	    setTimeoutImpl = (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms),
   396	    clearTimeoutImpl = (h) => clearTimeout(h as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
   397	  } = opts
   398	
   399	  const ESImpl =
   400	    opts.eventSourceImpl !== undefined
   401	      ? opts.eventSourceImpl
   402	      : typeof EventSource !== 'undefined'
   403	        ? EventSource
   404	        : null
   405	
   406	  let lastEventId = opts.lastEventId ?? -1
   407	  let closed = false
   408	  let es: EventSource | null = null
   409	  let timer: unknown = null
   410	  let transport: TransportState = { mode: 'sse', errors: 0 }
   411	
   412	  const setTransport = (patch: Partial<TransportState>) => {
   413	    transport = { ...transport, ...patch }
   414	    onTransport?.(transport)
   415	  }
   416	
   417	  const clearTimer = () => {
   418	    if (timer !== null) {
   419	      clearTimeoutImpl(timer)
   420	      timer = null
   421	    }
   422	  }
   423	
   424	  const emit = (events: Event[]) => {
   425	    const fresh = events.filter((e) => e.id > lastEventId)
   426	    if (fresh.length === 0) return
   427	    lastEventId = Math.max(lastEventId, ...fresh.map((e) => e.id))
   428	    onEvents(fresh)
   429	  }
   430	
   431	  const finish = (reason: string) => {
   432	    if (closed) return
   433	    log.info('stream.done', reason, { run_id: runId, last_event_id: lastEventId })
   434	    closed = true
   435	    es?.close()
   436	    es = null
   437	    clearTimer()
   438	    setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: reason })
   439	    onDone?.()
   440	  }
   441	
   442	  // ------------------------------------------------ polling fallback
   443	
   444	  const pollOnce = async () => {
   445	    if (closed || !fetchImpl) return
   446	    try {
   447	      const rec = await jsonFetch<RunRecord>(
   448	        `${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`,
   449	        {},
   450	        fetchImpl,
   451	        headers,
   452	      )
   453	      onRecord?.(rec)
   454	      emit(rec.events ?? [])
   455	      if (isTerminal(rec.status)) {
   456	        finish('run reached a terminal status (poll)')
   457	        return
   458	      }
   459	    } catch (err) {
   460	      log.warn('poll.error', 'GET /runs failed', { run_id: runId, error: String(err) })
   461	      setTransport({ detail: String(err) })
   462	    }
   463	    if (!closed) timer = setTimeoutImpl(() => void pollOnce(), pollIntervalMs)
   464	  }
   465	
   466	  const startPolling = (why: string) => {
   467	    if (closed) return
   468	    es?.close()
   469	    es = null
   470	    clearTimer()
   471	    log.warn('stream.fallback', `switching to polling: ${why}`, {
   472	      run_id: runId,
   473	      interval_ms: pollIntervalMs,
   474	    })
   475	    setTransport({ mode: 'poll', detail: why })
   476	    void pollOnce()
   477	  }
   478	
   479	  // ------------------------------------------------ SSE
   480	
   481	  const openSse = () => {
   482	    if (closed || !ESImpl) return
   483	    const url = new URL(`${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}/events`)
   484	    if (lastEventId >= 0) url.searchParams.set('last_event_id', String(lastEventId))
   485	    let deliveredThisStream = 0
   486	
   487	    log.info('stream.open', 'opening SSE', { run_id: runId, url: url.toString() })
   488	    const source = new ESImpl(url.toString())
   489	    es = source
   490	
   491	    /** Drop this stream and open a fresh one at the resume point; the error counter stays 0. */
   492	    const rotate = (why: string) => {
   493	      source.close()
   494	      if (es === source) es = null
   495	      log.info('stream.rotate', why, {
   496	        run_id: runId,
   497	        delivered: deliveredThisStream,
   498	        last_event_id: lastEventId,
   499	      })
   500	      setTransport({ errors: 0 })
   501	      timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, 0)
   502	    }
   503	
   504	    const handle = (e: MessageEvent) => {
   505	      const ev = parseEvent(e.data as string)
   506	      if (!ev) {
   507	        log.warn('stream.parse_error', 'unparseable SSE frame', { run_id: runId })
   508	        return
   509	      }
   510	      deliveredThisStream += 1
   511	      if (transport.errors !== 0) setTransport({ errors: 0, detail: undefined })
   512	      emit([ev])
   513	      if (ev.type === 'run.finished') {
   514	        // Let the server send `done`; if it does not, the close handler below stops us.
   515	        log.info('stream.run_finished', 'run.finished seen', { run_id: runId })
   516	      }
   517	    }
   518	
   519	    source.addEventListener('message', handle as EventListener)
   520	    for (const t of EVENT_TYPES) source.addEventListener(t, handle as EventListener)
   521	    source.addEventListener('done', ((e: MessageEvent) => {
   522	      if (closed) return
   523	      const payload = parseDone(e?.data)
   524	      if (doneMeansReconnect(payload)) {
   525	        rotate('server closed its ~110 s window (`done` reason=window); reconnecting')
   526	        return
   527	      }
   528	      finish(
   529	        payload?.reason
   530	          ? `server sent \`event: done\` (reason=${payload.reason}, status=${String(payload.status ?? '?')})`
   531	          : 'server sent `event: done`',
   532	      )
   533	    }) as EventListener)
   534	
   535	    source.addEventListener('open', () => {
   536	      log.info('stream.connected', 'SSE open', { run_id: runId })
   537	      setTransport({ mode: 'sse' })
   538	    })
   539	
   540	    source.addEventListener('error', () => {
   541	      if (closed) return
   542	      if (es !== source) return // already rotated away from this stream
   543	      if (deliveredThisStream > 0) {
   544	        // Expected on an older harness: the stream closes at ~110 s with no `done` frame.
   545	        rotate('stream closed after delivering events; reconnecting')
   546	        return
   547	      }
   548	      source.close()
   549	      es = null
   550	      const errors = transport.errors + 1
   551	      setTransport({ errors, detail: 'EventSource error' })
   552	      log.warn('stream.error', 'SSE failed before delivering anything', {
   553	        run_id: runId,
   554	        errors,
   555	      })
   556	      if (errors >= maxSseErrors) {
   557	        startPolling(`${errors} consecutive EventSource errors`)
   558	        return
   559	      }
   560	      timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, reconnectDelayMs)
   561	    })
   562	  }
   563	
   564	  if (ESImpl) {
   565	    openSse()
   566	  } else {
   567	    startPolling('EventSource unavailable in this environment')
   568	  }
   569	
   570	  return {
   571	    close: () => {
   572	      if (closed) return
   573	      closed = true
   574	      es?.close()
   575	      es = null
   576	      clearTimer()
   577	      setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: 'closed by caller' })
   578	      log.info('stream.close', 'subscription closed by caller', { run_id: runId })
   579	    },
   580	    state: () => transport,
   581	  }
   582	}

cd /Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web && rtk proxy cat -n src/hooks/useHarness.ts src/hooks/useConversations.ts src/hooks/useConversation.ts src/hooks/useStartRun.ts
Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:52.612Z
     1	/**
     2	 * Resolve the harness URL once, build the client for the current identity, then keep the two
     3	 * things Home needs from it: `/health` and `/scenarios`.
     4	 *
     5	 * Both are allowed to fail. A dead harness must still leave a usable page — the bundled replay
     6	 * is the whole reason demo mode exists — so failures land in `healthError` / `scenariosError`
     7	 * rather than throwing.
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
    11	
    12	import { useIdentity } from '@/hooks/useIdentity'
    13	import { HarnessClient } from '@/lib/api'
    14	import { resolveConfig, type ResolvedConfig } from '@/lib/config'
    15	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
    16	import type { Health, Scenario } from '@/lib/types'
    17	
    18	const log = getLogger('web')
    19	
    20	export interface HarnessState {
    21	  /** Resolved harness origin, or null while resolving. */
    22	  base: string | null
    23	  /** `/health` answered and reported ok — the only gate on starting a live run. */
    24	  reachable: boolean
    25	  /** Where the URL came from: window | config.json | build-env | default. */
    26	  source: ResolvedConfig['source'] | null
    27	  health: Health | null
    28	  healthError: string | null
    29	  scenarios: Scenario[]
    30	  scenariosError: string | null
    31	  loading: boolean
    32	  refresh: () => void
    33	  /** Bound to the current identity: every request carries `X-Faultline-User`. Null until the URL resolves. */
    34	  client: HarnessClient | null
    35	  /** The anonymous browser id the client sends. */
    36	  userId: string
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function useHarness(): HarnessState {
    40	  const { userId } = useIdentity()
    41	  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<ResolvedConfig | null>(null)
    42	  const [health, setHealth] = useState<Health | null>(null)
    43	  const [healthError, setHealthError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    44	  const [scenarios, setScenarios] = useState<Scenario[]>([])
    45	  const [scenariosError, setScenariosError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    46	  /** Which (client, refresh epoch) the last probe pair settled for; `loading` derives from it. */
    47	  const [settled, setSettled] = useState<{ client: HarnessClient; epoch: number } | null>(null)
    48	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0)
    49	
    50	  // 1. Resolve the base URL once.
    51	  useEffect(() => {
    52	    let cancelled = false
    53	    void resolveConfig().then((resolved) => {
    54	      if (cancelled) return
    55	      setCfg(resolved)
    56	      log.info('config.resolved', 'harness URL resolved', {
    57	        harness_url: resolved.harnessUrl,
    58	        source: resolved.source,
    59	      })
    60	    })
    61	    return () => {
    62	      cancelled = true
    63	    }
    64	  }, [])
    65	
    66	  // 2. One client per (base, identity). A reset() of the id rebuilds it.
    67	  const client = useMemo(
    68	    () => (cfg ? new HarnessClient(cfg.harnessUrl, { userId }) : null),
    69	    [cfg, userId],
    70	  )
    71	
    72	  // 3. Probe /health and /scenarios whenever the client changes or refresh() is called.
    73	  useEffect(() => {
    74	    if (!client) return
    75	    let cancelled = false
    76	
    77	    // Deliberately NOT `await Promise.all*` before updating: a cold harness answers /health in
    78	    // ~12 s while /scenarios comes back in 0.3 s (or the other way round). Coupling them would
    79	    // leave the whole page on a spinner for the slower of the two, so each settles on its own.
    80	    const probeHealth = client
    81	      .health()
    82	      .then((value) => {
    83	        if (cancelled) return
    84	        setHealth(value)
    85	        setHealthError(null)
    86	        log.info('health.ok', 'harness reachable', {
    87	          model_default: value.model_default ?? null,
    88	          has_provider_key: value.has_provider_key,
    89	        })
    90	      })
    91	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
    92	        if (cancelled) return
    93	        setHealth(null)
    94	        setHealthError(String(err))
    95	        log.warn('health.error', 'harness unreachable', { error: String(err) })
    96	      })
    97	
    98	    const probeScenarios = client
    99	      .scenarios()
   100	      .then((value) => {
   101	        if (cancelled) return
   102	        setScenarios(value)
   103	        setScenariosError(null)
   104	        log.info('scenarios.loaded', 'catalogue loaded', { count: value.length })
   105	      })
   106	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
   107	        if (cancelled) return
   108	        setScenarios([])
   109	        setScenariosError(String(err))
   110	        log.warn('scenarios.error', 'GET /scenarios failed', { error: String(err) })
   111	      })
   112	
   113	    void Promise.all([probeHealth, probeScenarios]).then(() => {
   114	      if (!cancelled) setSettled({ client, epoch: nonce })
   115	    })
   116	
   117	    return () => {
   118	      cancelled = true
   119	    }
   120	  }, [client, nonce])
   121	
   122	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), [])
   123	
   124	  // Loading until the URL resolves, and again whenever the client or the refresh epoch moves on.
   125	  const loading = !client || !(settled?.client === client && settled.epoch === nonce)
   126	
   127	  return {
   128	    base: cfg?.harnessUrl ?? null,
   129	    reachable: health?.ok === true,
   130	    source: cfg?.source ?? null,
   131	    health,
   132	    healthError,
   133	    scenarios,
   134	    scenariosError,
   135	    loading,
   136	    refresh,
   137	    client,
   138	    userId,
   139	  }
   140	}
     1	/**
     2	 * The conversation rail: GET /conversations for the current identity.
     3	 *
     4	 * A harness without the Store answers 404 on this route. That is not an error — the app still
     5	 * runs episodes through POST /runs — so it lands in `available: false` and the rail hides itself.
     6	 *
     7	 * State is the last *loaded* answer, keyed by the request that produced it (client identity +
     8	 * refresh epoch); `loading` is derived from whether that key is current. No setState runs
     9	 * synchronously inside the effect, so a refresh or an identity reset costs one render, not two.
    10	 */
    11	
    12	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
    13	
    14	import { HarnessClient, isNotFound } from '@/lib/api'
    15	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
    16	import type { ConversationSummary } from '@/lib/types'
    17	
    18	const log = getLogger('web')
    19	
    20	export interface ConversationsState {
    21	  items: ConversationSummary[]
    22	  loading: boolean
    23	  /** Transport / server error text; never set for a 404. */
    24	  error: string | null
    25	  /** null until the first answer; false when the harness has no /conversations routes. */
    26	  available: boolean | null
    27	  refresh: () => void
    28	}
    29	
    30	interface Loaded {
    31	  client: HarnessClient
    32	  epoch: number
    33	  items: ConversationSummary[]
    34	  available: boolean | null
    35	  error: string | null
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function useConversations(client: HarnessClient | null): ConversationsState {
    39	  const [epoch, setEpoch] = useState(0)
    40	  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null)
    41	
    42	  useEffect(() => {
    43	    if (!client) return
    44	    let cancelled = false
    45	
    46	    client
    47	      .listConversations()
    48	      .then((list) => {
    49	        if (cancelled) return
    50	        setLoaded({ client, epoch, items: list, available: true, error: null })
    51	        log.info('conversations.loaded', 'rail loaded', { count: list.length })
    52	      })
    53	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
    54	        if (cancelled) return
    55	        if (isNotFound(err)) {
    56	          setLoaded({ client, epoch, items: [], available: false, error: null })
    57	          log.info('conversations.unavailable', 'GET /conversations is 404: history not available on this harness')
    58	          return
    59	        }
    60	        // Keep whatever was on screen; just surface the error.
    61	        setLoaded((prev) => ({
    62	          client,
    63	          epoch,
    64	          items: prev?.items ?? [],
    65	          available: prev?.available ?? null,
    66	          error: String(err),
    67	        }))
    68	        log.warn('conversations.error', 'GET /conversations failed', { error: String(err) })
    69	      })
    70	
    71	    return () => {
    72	      cancelled = true
    73	    }
    74	  }, [client, epoch])
    75	
    76	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setEpoch((n) => n + 1), [])
    77	
    78	  return useMemo(() => {
    79	    const current = !!client && !!loaded && loaded.client === client && loaded.epoch === epoch
    80	    return {
    81	      items: loaded?.items ?? [],
    82	      loading: !!client && !current,
    83	      error: current ? loaded.error : null,
    84	      available: loaded?.available ?? null,
    85	      refresh,
    86	    }
    87	  }, [client, loaded, epoch, refresh])
    88	}
     1	/**
     2	 * One persisted conversation: GET /conversations/{id} → runs + messages.
     3	 *
     4	 * The harness answers 404 both for an unknown id and for an id that belongs to another browser
     5	 * (no existence oracle), so the two are reported identically as "not found".
     6	 *
     7	 * State is the last *loaded* answer, keyed by (client, id, refresh epoch). `detail` / `error`
     8	 * are only exposed while that key is current, so switching conversations never shows the
     9	 * previous one's transcript while the next loads — and no setState runs synchronously in the
    10	 * effect.
    11	 */
    12	
    13	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
    14	
    15	import { HarnessClient, isNotFound } from '@/lib/api'
    16	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
    17	import type { ConversationDetail } from '@/lib/types'
    18	
    19	const log = getLogger('web')
    20	
    21	export const NOT_FOUND = 'not found'
    22	
    23	export interface ConversationState {
    24	  detail: ConversationDetail | null
    25	  loading: boolean
    26	  /** `NOT_FOUND` for a 404, otherwise the transport / server error text. */
    27	  error: string | null
    28	  refresh: () => void
    29	}
    30	
    31	interface Loaded {
    32	  client: HarnessClient
    33	  id: string
    34	  epoch: number
    35	  detail: ConversationDetail | null
    36	  error: string | null
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function useConversation(client: HarnessClient | null, id: string | null): ConversationState {
    40	  const [epoch, setEpoch] = useState(0)
    41	  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null)
    42	
    43	  useEffect(() => {
    44	    if (!client || !id) return
    45	    let cancelled = false
    46	
    47	    client
    48	      .getConversation(id)
    49	      .then((value) => {
    50	        if (cancelled) return
    51	        setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: value, error: null })
    52	        log.info('conversation.loaded', 'GET /conversations/{id}', {
    53	          conversation_id: id,
    54	          runs: value.runs?.length ?? 0,
    55	          messages: value.messages?.length ?? 0,
    56	        })
    57	      })
    58	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
    59	        if (cancelled) return
    60	        if (isNotFound(err)) {
    61	          setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: null, error: NOT_FOUND })
    62	          log.warn('conversation.not_found', 'conversation is 404 for this identity', { conversation_id: id })
    63	          return
    64	        }
    65	        setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: null, error: String(err) })
    66	        log.warn('conversation.error', 'GET /conversations/{id} failed', { conversation_id: id, error: String(err) })
    67	      })
    68	
    69	    return () => {
    70	      cancelled = true
    71	    }
    72	  }, [client, id, epoch])
    73	
    74	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setEpoch((n) => n + 1), [])
    75	
    76	  return useMemo(() => {
    77	    const wanted = !!client && !!id
    78	    const current =
    79	      wanted && !!loaded && loaded.client === client && loaded.id === id && loaded.epoch === epoch
    80	    return {
    81	      detail: current ? loaded.detail : null,
    82	      loading: wanted && !current,
    83	      error: current ? loaded.error : null,
    84	      refresh,
    85	    }
    86	  }, [client, id, loaded, epoch, refresh])
    87	}
     1	import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'
     2	
     3	import type { ConversationsState } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
     4	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
     5	import { isNotFound } from '@/lib/api'
     6	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
     7	import { navigate } from '@/lib/router'
     8	
     9	const log = getLogger('web')
    10	
    11	export interface StartParams {
    12	  scenarioId: string
    13	  model: string
    14	  seed: number | null
    15	  /** Task prompt override; empty/null = the scenario default (server side). */
    16	  prompt: string | null
    17	  /** Start the run inside an existing conversation. */
    18	  conversationId?: string | null
    19	  title?: string
    20	}
    21	
    22	/**
    23	 * One place that knows how to start a run, in order of preference:
    24	 *   1. inside an existing conversation          → POST /conversations/{id}/runs
    25	 *   2. new conversation (history available)     → POST /conversations, then (1)
    26	 *   3. harness without the SQLite store (404)   → POST /runs, land on /runs/{id}
    27	 * and then navigates to the right page. Every path refreshes the conversation rail.
    28	 */
    29	export function useStartRun(harness: HarnessState, conversations: ConversationsState) {
    30	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
    31	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    32	
    33	  const start = useCallback(
    34	    async (p: StartParams): Promise<string | null> => {
    35	      const client = harness.client
    36	      if (!client) {
    37	        setError('harness URL not resolved yet')
    38	        return null
    39	      }
    40	      setBusy(true)
    41	      setError(null)
    42	      const req = {
    43	        model: p.model,
    44	        seed: p.seed,
    45	        task_prompt: p.prompt && p.prompt.trim() ? p.prompt : undefined,
    46	      }
    47	      try {
    48	        if (p.conversationId) {
    49	          const r = await client.createConversationRun(p.conversationId, req)
    50	          log.info('run.started', 'run accepted (existing conversation)', {
    51	            run_id: r.run_id,
    52	            conversation_id: p.conversationId,
    53	            scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
    54	            model: p.model,
    55	          })
    56	          navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: r.conversation_id ?? p.conversationId, runId: r.run_id })
    57	          conversations.refresh()
    58	          return r.run_id
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        if (conversations.available !== false) {
    62	          try {
    63	            const conv = await client.createConversation({ scenario_id: p.scenarioId, title: p.title })
    64	            const r = await client.createConversationRun(conv.id, req)
    65	            log.info('run.started', 'run accepted (new conversation)', {
    66	              run_id: r.run_id,
    67	              conversation_id: conv.id,
    68	              scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
    69	              model: p.model,
    70	            })
    71	            navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: conv.id, runId: r.run_id })
    72	            conversations.refresh()
    73	            return r.run_id
    74	          } catch (err) {
    75	            if (!isNotFound(err)) throw err
    76	            log.warn('conversations.unavailable', 'no /conversations routes on this harness; using POST /runs', {
    77	              error: String(err),
    78	            })
    79	          }
    80	        }
    81	
    82	        const r = await client.createRun({ scenario_id: p.scenarioId, model: p.model, seed: p.seed })
    83	        log.info('run.started', 'run accepted (POST /runs)', {
    84	          run_id: r.run_id,
    85	          conversation_id: r.conversation_id ?? null,
    86	          scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
    87	          model: p.model,
    88	        })
    89	        if (r.conversation_id) navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: r.conversation_id, runId: r.run_id })
    90	        else navigate({ kind: 'run', runId: r.run_id })
    91	        conversations.refresh()
    92	        return r.run_id
    93	      } catch (err) {
    94	        setError(String(err))
    95	        log.error('run.start_failed', 'could not start the run', {
    96	          scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
    97	          error: String(err),
    98	        })
    99	        return null
   100	      } finally {
   101	        setBusy(false)
   102	      }
   103	    },
   104	    [harness.client, conversations],
   105	  )
   106	
   107	  return { start, busy, error }
   108	}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
    @app.get("/health")
    async def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
        reachable: bool | None = None
        detail: dict[str, Any] = {}
        gym = make_gym()
        try:
            info = await asyncio.to_thread(gym.health)
            reachable = bool(info)
            detail["sandbox_env"] = {k: info.get(k) for k in ("svc", "ok", "version") if k in info}
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - unreachable is a normal, reportable state
            reachable = False
            detail["sandbox_env_error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
        finally:
            try:
                gym.close()
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
                pass
        detail["sandbox_env_reachable"] = reachable
        try:
            detail["store"] = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.health)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the Store being down is reportable, not fatal
            detail["store"] = {"ok": False, "last_checkpoint_at": None,
                               "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]}
        # The secret is attached to `run_episode` only; if this is ever true, the boundary leaked.
        has_key = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ
        if has_key:
            log.error("health.secret_boundary", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY visible to the API function")
        return {
            "svc": "harness",
            "ok": True,
            "version": VERSION,
            "has_provider_key": has_key,
            "model_default": config.model_default(),
            "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url(),
            "detail": detail,
        }

    async def scenarios() -> Any:

            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=502,
                content={"error": "sandbox-env unreachable", "detail": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:500]},
            )
        finally:

                gym.close()
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
                pass

    async def scenario_catalogue() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Live scenario list, or [] when the gym is unreachable (a blip must not 502 the Run button)."""
        gym = make_gym()
        try:
            return list(await asyncio.to_thread(gym.scenarios) or [])
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            log.warn("scenarios.unavailable", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
            return []
        finally:
            try:
                gym.close()
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
                pass

    @app.post("/conversations")

            return err

        if not scenario_id:

        title = body.get("title") or _scenario_title(catalogue, scenario_id)

        )

        if err is not None:

        detail = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
        if detail is None:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})

    @app.patch("/conversations/{conversation_id}")
    async def patch_conversation(request: Request, conversation_id: str, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        uid, err = need_user(request)
        if err is not None:
            return err
        title = body.get("title")
        archived = body.get("archived")
        conv = await asyncio.to_thread(
            runs.update_conversation, conversation_id, uid,
            title=str(title) if isinstance(title, str) else None,
            archived=bool(archived) if archived is not None else None,
        )
        if conv is None:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
        return conv

        if err is not None:
            return err
        out = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.delete_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
        if out is None:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
