    @app.post("/conversations/{conversation_id}/runs")
    async def start_conversation_run(request: Request, conversation_id: str,
                                     body: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Any:
        uid, err = need_user(request)
        if err is not None:
            return err
        detail = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
        if detail is None:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
        conversation = detail.get("conversation") or {}
        req = dict(body or {})
        out = await _start_run(
            scenario_id=str(conversation.get("scenario_id") or ""),
            body=req,
            user_id=uid,
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
        )
        if isinstance(out, JSONResponse):
            return out
        return JSONResponse(status_code=202, content=out)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
    async def _start_run(*, scenario_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], user_id: str | None,
                         conversation_id: str | None) -> Any:
        if not scenario_id:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": "scenario_id is required"})
        model = body.get("model") or config.model_default()
        if model not in config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={"error": f"model {model!r} is not allowed", "allowed": config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST},
            )
        seed = body.get("seed")
        max_steps = body.get("max_steps")
        task_prompt: str | None = body.get("task_prompt") or None

        # Validate the scenario against the live catalogue; if the gym is unreachable we let the run
        # through and let reset fail loudly inside the episode.
        catalogue = await scenario_catalogue()
        if catalogue:
            known = {s.get("id") for s in catalogue if isinstance(s, dict)}
            if scenario_id not in known:
                return JSONResponse(
                    status_code=400,
                    content={"error": f"unknown scenario {scenario_id!r}",
                             "known": sorted(k for k in known if k)},
                )
            match = next((s for s in catalogue if s.get("id") == scenario_id), {})
            if not max_steps and isinstance(match.get("max_steps"), int):
                max_steps = match["max_steps"]
            if not task_prompt and isinstance(match.get("task_prompt"), str):
                task_prompt = match["task_prompt"]

        max_steps = int(max_steps or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS)
        max_steps = max(1, min(max_steps, config.HARD_MAX_STEPS))
        owner = user_id or ANON_USER_ID

        if not conversation_id:
            conversation = await asyncio.to_thread(
                runs.create_conversation, owner, scenario_id, _scenario_title(catalogue, scenario_id)
            )
            conversation_id = str((conversation or {}).get("id") or "")

        run_id = new_run_id()
        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
        record = {
            "run_id": run_id,
            "status": "queued",
            "scenario_id": scenario_id,
            "model": model,
            "seed": seed,
            "max_steps": max_steps,
            "episode_id": None,
            "task_prompt": task_prompt,
            "created_at": now_iso(),
            "finished_at": None,
            "events": [],
            "evaluation": None,
            "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
            "error": None,
            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
            "user_id": owner,
        }
        await asyncio.to_thread(runs.create, record)

        req = {"scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "seed": seed, "max_steps": max_steps,
               "conversation_id": conversation_id, "user_id": owner}
        if task_prompt:
            req["task_prompt"] = task_prompt
        try:
            call_id = await asyncio.to_thread(spawn_fn, run_id, req)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the record exists, so fail it visibly
            log.error("spawn.failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=run_id)
            error = f"spawn failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            await asyncio.to_thread(runs.update, run_id, status="error", error=error,
                                    finished_at=now_iso())
            return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"error": error, "run_id": run_id})

        log.info("run.spawned", "run_episode spawned", run_id=run_id, scenario_id=scenario_id,
                 model=model, max_steps=max_steps, conversation_id=conversation_id, user_id=owner,
                 call_id=call_id if isinstance(call_id, str) else None)
        return {"run_id": run_id, "conversation_id": conversation_id, "status": "queued",
                "scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "max_steps": max_steps, "seed": seed}

    @app.post("/runs")
    async def create_run(request: Request, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        out = await _start_run(
            scenario_id=str(body.get("scenario_id") or "").strip(),
            body=body,
            user_id=user_of(request),
            conversation_id=None,
        )
        return out

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:19:07.064Z
     1	/**
     2	 * Owns one run's view state and its transport.
     3	 *
     4	 * Two sources, one reducer:
     5	 *   - live : GET /runs/{id} to seed (so a page refresh mid-run restores everything), then
     6	 *            subscribeRun() for the tail. The seed is best-effort — a run created a moment ago can
     7	 *            404 until the harness has written it — so a failed seed must NOT skip the tail.
     8	 *   - demo : a bundled RunRecord replayed at 400 ms per event, with no backend at all.
     9	 */
    10	
    11	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
    12	
    13	import { HarnessClient, subscribeRun, type Subscription, type TransportState } from '@/lib/api'
    14	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
    15	import { fromRunRecord, initialState, reduce, type ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
    16	import { DEMOS, loadDemo, playReplay, type ReplayHandle } from '@/lib/replay'
    17	import type { Event } from '@/lib/types'
    18	
    19	const log = getLogger('web')
    20	
    21	export type RunSource =
    22	  | { kind: 'none' }
    23	  | { kind: 'live'; runId: string }
    24	  | { kind: 'demo'; demoId: string }
    25	
    26	export interface RunViewState {
    27	  state: ViewState
    28	  transport: TransportState | null
    29	  loadError: string | null
    30	  loading: boolean
    31	  /** True while a bundled demo is still emitting events. */
    32	  replaying: boolean
    33	}
    34	
    35	export function useRunView(client: HarnessClient | null, source: RunSource): RunViewState {
    36	  const [state, setState] = useState<ViewState>(initialState)
    37	  const [transport, setTransport] = useState<TransportState | null>(null)
    38	  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    39	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(source.kind !== 'none')
    40	  const [replaying, setReplaying] = useState(false)
    41	
    42	  const subRef = useRef<Subscription | null>(null)
    43	  const replayRef = useRef<ReplayHandle | null>(null)
    44	
    45	  const push = useCallback((events: Event[]) => {
    46	    setState((prev) => events.reduce(reduce, prev))
    47	  }, [])
    48	
    49	  const key =
    50	    source.kind === 'live'
    51	      ? `live:${source.runId}`
    52	      : source.kind === 'demo'
    53	        ? `demo:${source.demoId}`
    54	        : 'none'
    55	
    56	  useEffect(() => {
    57	    let cancelled = false
    58	
    59	    const teardown = () => {
    60	      subRef.current?.close()
    61	      subRef.current = null
    62	      replayRef.current?.stop()
    63	      replayRef.current = null
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    teardown()
    67	    setState(initialState())
    68	    setTransport(null)
    69	    setLoadError(null)
    70	    setReplaying(false)
    71	
    72	    const stop = () => {
    73	      cancelled = true
    74	      teardown()
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    if (key === 'none') {
    78	      setLoading(false)
    79	      return stop
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    setLoading(true)
    83	
    84	    if (key.startsWith('demo:')) {
    85	      const demoId = key.slice('demo:'.length)
    86	      const meta = DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === demoId)
    87	      void (async () => {
    88	        try {
    89	          if (!meta) throw new Error(`no bundled demo named "${demoId}"`)
    90	          const rec = await loadDemo(meta.file)
    91	          if (cancelled) return
    92	          // Seed identity from the record, but let the replayed events drive status and score.
    93	          const seeded = fromRunRecord({ ...rec, events: [], evaluation: null, finished_at: null })
    94	          setState({ ...seeded, status: 'running', evaluation: null })
    95	          setLoading(false)
    96	          setReplaying(true)
    97	          log.info('demo.loaded', 'bundled demo loaded', { demo: demoId, events: rec.events.length })
    98	          replayRef.current = playReplay({
    99	            events: rec.events,
   100	            onEvents: push,
   101	            onDone: () => {
   102	              if (!cancelled) setReplaying(false)
   103	              log.info('demo.finished', 'replay complete', { demo: demoId })
   104	            },
   105	          })
   106	        } catch (err) {
   107	          if (cancelled) return
   108	          setLoading(false)
   109	          setLoadError(String(err))
   110	          log.error('demo.error', 'failed to load bundled run', { demo: demoId, error: String(err) })
   111	        }
   112	      })()
   113	      return stop
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    const runId = key.slice('live:'.length)
   117	    if (!client) {
   118	      setLoading(false)
   119	      setLoadError('harness URL not resolved yet')
   120	      return stop
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    void (async () => {
   124	      let from = -1
   125	      try {
   126	        const rec = await client.getRun(runId)
   127	        if (cancelled) return
   128	        setState(fromRunRecord(rec))
   129	        from = rec.events?.length ? Math.max(...rec.events.map((e) => e.id)) : -1
   130	        log.info('run.restored', 'seeded view from GET /runs/{id}', {
   131	          run_id: runId,
   132	          events: rec.events?.length ?? 0,
   133	          status: rec.status,
   134	        })
   135	      } catch (err) {
   136	        if (cancelled) return
   137	        setLoadError(String(err))
   138	        log.warn('run.load_error', 'GET /runs failed; relying on the stream', {
   139	          run_id: runId,
   140	          error: String(err),
   141	        })
   142	      }
   143	      if (cancelled) return
   144	      setLoading(false)
   145	      subRef.current = subscribeRun({
   146	        base: client.base,
   147	        runId,
   148	        lastEventId: from,
   149	        onEvents: push,
   150	        onRecord: (r) => {
   151	          // The polling fallback returns the whole record; take the summary fields from it so the
   152	          // header stays right even if a `run.finished` event was missed.
   153	          setState((prev) => ({
   154	            ...prev,
   155	            status: r.status ?? prev.status,
   156	            usage: r.usage ?? prev.usage,
   157	            evaluation: r.evaluation ?? prev.evaluation,
   158	            error: r.error ?? prev.error,
   159	            finishedAt: r.finished_at ?? prev.finishedAt,
   160	          }))
   161	        },
   162	        onTransport: (t) => {
   163	          if (!cancelled) setTransport(t)
   164	        },
   165	      })
   166	    })()
   167	
   168	    return stop
   169	  }, [key, client, push])
   170	
   171	  return { state, transport, loadError, loading, replaying }
   172	}

    17	/** A run outside a conversation (harness without the SQLite store). Upgrades itself to
    18	 *  /conversations/{id} when the run record turns out to carry a conversation_id. */
    19	export function RunPage({ harness, runId }: { harness: HarnessState; runId: string }) {
    20	  const source = useMemo(() => ({ kind: 'live', runId }) as const, [runId])
    21	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
    22	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
    23	  const live = rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued'
    24	
    25	  useEffect(() => {
    26	    const client = harness.client
    27	    if (!client) return
    28	    let cancelled = false
    29	    void client
    30	      .getRun(runId)
    31	      .then((rec) => {
    32	        if (cancelled || !rec.conversation_id) return
    33	        log.info('run.upgrade', 'run belongs to a conversation; redirecting', {
    34	          run_id: runId,
    35	          conversation_id: rec.conversation_id,
    36	        })
    37	        navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: rec.conversation_id, runId }, { replace: true })
    38	      })
    39	      .catch(() => undefined)
    40	    return () => {
    41	      cancelled = true
    42	    }
    43	  }, [harness.client, runId])
    44	
    45	  useEffect(() => {
    46	    document.title = `${rv.state.scenarioId ?? 'run'} · ${runId} — Faultline`
    47	  }, [rv.state.scenarioId, runId])
    48	
    49	  const transcript = useMemo(() => transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live), [rv.state, live])
    50	  const scenario = useMemo(
    51	    () => harness.scenarios.find((s) => s.id === rv.state.scenarioId) ?? null,
    52	    [harness.scenarios, rv.state.scenarioId],
    53	  )
    54	  const verdict = useMemo(() => buildVerdict(rv.state, scenario, { live }), [rv.state, scenario, live])
    55	  const src: TranscriptSource = live ? (rv.transport?.mode === 'poll' ? 'poll' : 'sse') : 'record'
    56	
    57	  return (
    58	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
    59	      <TopBar
    60	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Runs', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: rv.state.scenarioId ? `${rv.state.scenarioId} · ${runId}` : runId }]}
    61	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
    62	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
    63	      >
    64	        <RunStatusStrip state={rv.state} transport={rv.transport} source={src} sandbox={verdict.sandbox} />
    65	      </TopBar>
    66	      <RunLayout state={rv.state} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen}>
    67	          <TranscriptView
    68	            transcript={transcript}
    69	            diffs={rv.state.diffs}
    70	            maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps}
    71	            loading={rv.loading}
    72	            loadingLabel="Loading run…"
    73	            error={rv.loadError}
    74	            transport={rv.transport}
    75	            verdict={verdict}
    76	          />
    77	      </RunLayout>
    78	    </div>
    79	  )
    80	}

    55	            error={rv.loadError}

     1	/**
     2	 * Demo replay: play a bundled RunRecord's events back with fixed spacing so the UI is fully
     3	 * exercisable with no backend at all (a provider outage or a cold harness must not leave a reviewer
     4	 * looking at an empty page).
     5	 *
     6	 * `public/demo/<scenario>.json` holds a RunRecord; scripts/export_demo.py writes those from a real
     7	 * run under runs/. See public/demo/README.md.
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import { getLogger } from './log'
    11	import type { Event, RunRecord } from './types'
    12	
    13	const log = getLogger('web')
    14	
    15	export const DEMO_STEP_MS = 400
    16	
    17	export interface DemoMeta {
    18	  id: string
    19	  label: string
    20	  file: string
    21	}
    22	
    23	/** Demos shipped in public/demo. Adding a file here is all it takes to expose a new replay. */
    24	export const DEMOS: DemoMeta[] = [
    25	  // Real captures: claude-haiku-4-5 on the deployed stack (2026-09-12), exported with
    26	  // scripts/export_demo.py. Ids match scenario ids so the scenario table offers them via demoFor().
    27	  { id: 'lost-ack', label: 'lost-ack', file: '/demo/lost-ack.json' },
    28	  { id: 'locked-file', label: 'locked-file', file: '/demo/locked-file.json' },
    29	  { id: 'missing-config', label: 'missing-config', file: '/demo/missing-config.json' },
    30	  { id: 'gauntlet', label: 'gauntlet', file: '/demo/gauntlet.json' },
    31	]
    32	
    33	export function demoFor(scenarioId: string): DemoMeta | undefined {
    34	  return DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === scenarioId)
    35	}
    36	
    37	export async function loadDemo(
    38	  file: string,
    39	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
    40	): Promise<RunRecord> {
    41	  const res = await fetchImpl(file, { cache: 'no-store' })
    42	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`demo ${file} not found (${res.status})`)
    43	  const rec = (await res.json()) as RunRecord
    44	  if (!Array.isArray(rec?.events)) throw new Error(`demo ${file} has no events`)
    45	  return rec
    46	}
    47	
    48	export interface ReplayHandle {
    49	  stop: () => void
    50	}
    51	
    52	export interface ReplayOptions {
    53	  events: Event[]
    54	  onEvents: (events: Event[]) => void
    55	  onDone?: () => void
    56	  stepMs?: number
    57	  setTimeoutImpl?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown
    58	  clearTimeoutImpl?: (h: unknown) => void
    59	}
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * Emit `events` one at a time, `stepMs` apart. Consecutive events that belong to the same instant
    63	 * (a tool.call and its tool.result share a step) still get their own tick — the point of the demo
    64	 * is to show the timeline filling in, not to simulate real latency.
    65	 */
    66	export function playReplay(opts: ReplayOptions): ReplayHandle {
    67	  const {
    68	    events,
    69	    onEvents,
    70	    onDone,
    71	    stepMs = DEMO_STEP_MS,
    72	    setTimeoutImpl = (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms),
    73	    clearTimeoutImpl = (h) => clearTimeout(h as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
    74	  } = opts
    75	
    76	  let i = 0
    77	  let handle: unknown = null
    78	  let stopped = false
    79	
    80	  log.info('replay.start', 'playing bundled demo', { events: events.length, step_ms: stepMs })
    81	
    82	  const tick = () => {
    83	    if (stopped) return
    84	    const ev = events[i]
    85	    i += 1
    86	    if (!ev) {
    87	      log.info('replay.done', 'demo finished', { events: events.length })
    88	      onDone?.()
    89	      return
    90	    }
    91	    onEvents([ev])
    92	    handle = setTimeoutImpl(tick, stepMs)
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  // First event lands immediately so the view is never blank.
    96	  tick()
    97	
    98	  return {
    99	    stop: () => {
   100	      if (stopped) return
   101	      stopped = true
   102	      if (handle !== null) clearTimeoutImpl(handle)
   103	      log.info('replay.stop', 'demo stopped by caller', { delivered: i })
   104	    },
   105	  }
   106	}

    13	export const STATUS_TONE: Record<RunStatus, string> = {
    14	  queued: 'border-border text-muted-foreground',
    15	  running: 'border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300',
    16	  ok: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300',

    20	  unevaluated: 'border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-300',
    21	  // a real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it
    22	  interrupted: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
    23	}

    25	/** Where the transcript on screen is coming from. Shown so a reviewer can see the persistence path. */
    26	export type TranscriptSource = 'sse' | 'poll' | 'sqlite' | 'replay' | 'record'
    27	
    28	const SOURCE_LABEL: Record<TranscriptSource, { label: string; tip: string; icon: typeof Radio }> = {
    29	  sse: { label: 'live · sse', tip: 'Tailing GET /runs/{id}/events; the harness rotates the stream every ~110 s and we resume with Last-Event-ID.', icon: Radio },
    30	  poll: { label: 'live · poll', tip: 'SSE failed twice in a row — polling GET /runs/{id} every 1.5 s instead.', icon: Radio },
    31	  sqlite: { label: 'sqlite', tip: 'Transcript loaded from GET /conversations/{id}: the messages/blocks projection stored in SQLite on the Modal Volume.', icon: Database },
    32	  record: { label: 'record', tip: 'Loaded from GET /runs/{id} (the persisted run record).', icon: Database },
    33	  replay: { label: 'replay', tip: 'A bundled recorded run playing back in the browser; no backend involved.', icon: Rewind },
    34	}

    36	function Stat({ label, value, title }: { label: string; value: string; title?: string }) {
    37	  return (
    38	    <div className="hidden flex-col leading-tight lg:flex" title={title}>
    39	      <span className="text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">{label}</span>
    40	      <span className="font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums">{value}</span>
    41	    </div>
    42	  )
    43	}

    45	export function RunStatusStrip({
    46	  state,
    47	  transport,
    48	  source,
    49	  replaying,
    50	  sandbox,
    51	}: {
    52	  state: ViewState | null
    53	  transport: TransportState | null
    54	  source: TranscriptSource | null
    55	  replaying?: boolean
    56	  /** From the run's Verdict: which workspace answered and whether it survived. */
    57	  sandbox?: SandboxSummary | null
    58	}) {
    59	  const live = !!state && (state.status === 'running' || state.status === 'queued')
    60	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
    61	  useEffect(() => {
    62	    if (!live) return
    63	    const t = setInterval(() => setTick((n) => n + 1), 1000)
    64	    return () => clearInterval(t)
    65	  }, [live])
    66	
    67	  if (!state) return null
    68	  const faults = faultCount(state)
    69	  const recovered = recoveredCount(state)
    70	  const src = source ? SOURCE_LABEL[source] : null
    71	  const SrcIcon = src?.icon ?? Radio
    72	
    73	  return (
    74	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
    75	      <Badge variant="outline" className={`font-mono ${STATUS_TONE[state.status]}`}>
    76	        {replaying ? 'replay' : state.status}
    77	      </Badge>
    78	      {src ? (
    79	        <Tooltip>
    80	          <TooltipTrigger
    81	            render={
    82	              <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">
    83	                <SrcIcon aria-hidden /> {src.label}
    84	              </Badge>
    85	            }
    86	          />
    87	          <TooltipContent>{src.tip}{transport?.detail ? ` (${transport.detail})` : ''}</TooltipContent>
    88	        </Tooltip>
    89	      ) : null}
    90	      {sandbox ? <SandboxChip sandbox={sandbox} className="hidden md:inline-flex" /> : null}
    91	      <Stat label="step" value={`${state.step}/${state.maxSteps}`} />
    92	      <Stat
    93	        label="tokens"
    94	        value={`${fmtTokens(state.usage.input_tokens)} in · ${fmtTokens(state.usage.output_tokens)} out`}
    95	        title={`${state.usage.input_tokens} input / ${state.usage.output_tokens} output`}
    96	      />
    97	      <Stat label="elapsed" value={fmtDuration(elapsedMs(state))} />
    98	      <Stat label="faults" value={`${recovered}/${faults} recovered`} />
    99	      {state.evaluation ? <Stat label="score" value={`${Math.round(state.evaluation.score)}`} /> : null}
   100	    </div>
   101	  )
   102	}

    23	export function TopBar({
    24	  crumbs,
    25	  children,
    26	  onToggleWorkspace,
    27	  workspaceOpen,
    28	}: {
    29	  crumbs: Crumb[]
    30	  /** Right-aligned content (status chips). */
    31	  children?: ReactNode
    32	  onToggleWorkspace?: () => void
    33	  workspaceOpen?: boolean
    34	}) {
    35	  return (
    36	    <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-border bg-background/85 px-3 backdrop-blur supports-[backdrop-filter]:bg-background/70">
    37	      <SidebarTrigger className="-ml-1" />
    38	      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="mr-1 h-4" />
    39	      <Breadcrumb className="min-w-0">
    40	        <BreadcrumbList className="flex-nowrap">
    41	          {crumbs.map((c, i) => {
    42	            const last = i === crumbs.length - 1
    43	            return (
    44	              <Fragment key={`${c.label}-${i}`}>
    45	                <BreadcrumbItem className="min-w-0">
    46	                  {last || !c.route ? (
    47	                    <BreadcrumbPage className="truncate">{c.label}</BreadcrumbPage>
    48	                  ) : (
    49	                    <BreadcrumbLink className="truncate" {...linkProps(c.route)}>
    50	                      {c.label}
    51	                    </BreadcrumbLink>
    52	                  )}
    53	                </BreadcrumbItem>
    54	                {/* The separator is an <li> too, so it must be a sibling of the item, not a child. */}
    55	                {!last ? <BreadcrumbSeparator /> : null}
    56	              </Fragment>
    57	            )
    58	          })}
    59	        </BreadcrumbList>
    60	      </Breadcrumb>
    61	      <span className="flex-1" />
    62	      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto">{children}</div>
    63	      {onToggleWorkspace ? (
    64	        <Button
    65	          variant={workspaceOpen ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
    66	          size="sm"
    67	          className="ml-1 h-8 gap-1.5"
    68	          aria-pressed={workspaceOpen}
    69	          onClick={onToggleWorkspace}
    70	          title="Toggle the workspace panel (files, diffs, timeline, logs)"
    71	        >
    72	          <PanelRight aria-hidden />
    73	          <span className="hidden md:inline">Workspace</span>
    74	        </Button>
    75	      ) : null}
    76	    </header>
    77	  )
    78	}

     1	import type { MouseEvent } from 'react'
     2	
     3	import { hrefFor, navigate, type Route } from '@/lib/router'
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * Props for an `<a>` that navigates in-app on a plain left click and behaves like a normal link
     7	 * otherwise (middle click, cmd/ctrl click, right click → open in a new tab / copy link).
     8	 */
     9	export function linkProps(route: Route, opts: { replace?: boolean } = {}) {
    10	  return {
    11	    href: hrefFor(route),
    12	    onClick: (e: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
    13	      if (e.defaultPrevented || e.button !== 0) return
    14	      if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return
    15	      e.preventDefault()
    16	      navigate(route, opts)
    17	    },
    18	  }
    19	}
     1	import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
     2	import { AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
     3	
     4	import { Composer } from '@/components/bui'
     5	import { linkProps } from '@/components/layout/Link'
     6	import { RunStatusStrip, STATUS_TONE, type TranscriptSource } from '@/components/layout/RunStatusStrip'
     7	import { TopBar } from '@/components/layout/TopBar'
     8	import { TranscriptView } from '@/components/transcript/TranscriptView'
     9	import { RunLayout } from '@/components/workspace/RunLayout'
    10	import { useWorkspaceOpen } from '@/components/workspace/WorkspaceColumn'
    11	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'

    14	import type { ConversationsState } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
    15	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
    16	import { useRunView } from '@/hooks/useRunView'
    17	import { useStartRun } from '@/hooks/useStartRun'
    18	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
    19	import { isTerminal } from '@/lib/reducer'
    20	import { buildVerdict } from '@/lib/reliability'

    26	function RunSwitcher({
    27	  conversationId,
    28	  runs,
    29	  selectedId,
    30	}: {
    31	  conversationId: string
    32	  runs: RunSummary[]
    33	  selectedId: string | null
    34	}) {
    35	  if (runs.length <= 1) return null
    36	  return (
    37	    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5" aria-label="Runs in this conversation">
    38	      <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">runs</span>
    39	      {runs.map((r, i) => (
    40	        <a
    41	          key={r.id}
    42	          {...linkProps({ kind: 'conversation', id: conversationId, runId: r.id })}
    43	          aria-current={r.id === selectedId ? 'page' : undefined}
    44	          className="rounded-md outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50"
    45	        >
    46	          <Badge
    47	            variant="outline"
    48	            className={`font-mono ${STATUS_TONE[r.status]} ${r.id === selectedId ? 'ring-1 ring-ring/60' : 'opacity-80'}`}
    49	          >
    50	            #{i + 1} · {r.status}
    51	            {r.score !== null && r.score !== undefined ? ` · ${Math.round(r.score)}` : ''}
    52	          </Badge>
    53	        </a>
    54	      ))}
    55	    </div>
    56	  )
    57	}

    59	export function ConversationPage({
    60	  harness,
    61	  conversations,
    62	  conversationId,
    63	  runId,
    64	}: {
    65	  harness: HarnessState
    66	  conversations: ConversationsState
    67	  conversationId: string
    68	  runId: string | null
    69	}) {
    70	  const { detail, loading, error, refresh } = useConversation(harness.client, conversationId)
    71	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
    72	  const [model, setModel] = useState<string>(DEFAULT_MODEL)
    73	  const [seed, setSeed] = useState<number | null>(null)
    74	  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('')
    75	  const { start, busy, error: startError } = useStartRun(harness, conversations)
    76	
    77	  const runs = useMemo(
    78	    () => [...(detail?.runs ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at)),
    79	    [detail?.runs],
    80	  )
    81	  const selected: RunSummary | null = useMemo(() => {
    82	    if (runs.length === 0) return null
    83	    return (runId ? runs.find((r) => r.id === runId) : null) ?? runs[runs.length - 1] ?? null
    84	  }, [runs, runId])
    85	
    86	  const source = useMemo(
    87	    () => (selected ? ({ kind: 'live', runId: selected.id } as const) : ({ kind: 'none' } as const)),
    88	    [selected],
    89	  )
    90	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
    91	  const live = !!selected && (rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued')
    92	
    93	  // When the live run reaches a terminal state, reload the conversation from the store so the
    94	  // transcript switches to the persisted (SQLite) projection and the rail updates its status dot.
    95	  const refreshedFor = useRef<string | null>(null)
    96	  useEffect(() => {
    97	    if (!selected || !isTerminal(rv.state.status) || rv.state.runId !== selected.id) return
    98	    if (refreshedFor.current === selected.id) return
    99	    refreshedFor.current = selected.id
   100	    if (!isTerminal(selected.status)) {
   101	      log.info('conversation.refresh', 'run finished; reloading persisted transcript', {
   102	        conversation_id: conversationId,
   103	        run_id: selected.id,
   104	        status: rv.state.status,
   105	      })
   106	      refresh()
   107	      conversations.refresh()
   108	    }
   109	  }, [rv.state.status, rv.state.runId, selected, conversationId, refresh, conversations])
   110	
   111	  useEffect(() => {
   112	    if (harness.health?.model_default) setModel(harness.health.model_default)
   113	  }, [harness.health?.model_default])
   114	
   115	  const scenario = useMemo(
   116	    () => harness.scenarios.find((s) => s.id === detail?.conversation.scenario_id) ?? null,
   117	    [harness.scenarios, detail?.conversation.scenario_id],
   118	  )
   119	  useEffect(() => {
   120	    if (scenario && prompt === '') setPrompt(scenario.task_prompt)
   121	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   122	  }, [scenario?.id])
   123	
   124	  const verdict = useMemo(() => buildVerdict(rv.state, scenario, { live }), [rv.state, scenario, live])
   125	
   126	  useEffect(() => {
   127	    document.title = detail ? `${detail.conversation.title} — Faultline` : 'Faultline'
   128	  }, [detail])
   129	
   130	  const messagesForRun = useMemo(
   131	    () => (detail && selected ? detail.messages.filter((m) => m.run_id === selected.id) : []),
   132	    [detail, selected],
   133	  )
   134	  const persisted = !!detail && !!selected && isTerminal(selected.status) && messagesForRun.length > 0 && !live
   135	  const transcript = useMemo(
   136	    () =>
   137	      persisted && detail && selected
   138	        ? transcriptFromMessages(messagesForRun, selected)
   139	        : transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live),
   140	    [persisted, detail, selected, messagesForRun, rv.state, live],
   141	  )
   142	  const transcriptSource: TranscriptSource | null = persisted
   143	    ? 'sqlite'
   144	    : live
   145	      ? rv.transport?.mode === 'poll'
   146	        ? 'poll'
   147	        : 'sse'
   148	      : selected
   149	        ? 'record'
   150	        : null
   151	
   152	  const submit = () => {
   153	    if (!detail || busy) return
   154	    void start({
   155	      scenarioId: detail.conversation.scenario_id,
   156	      model,
   157	      seed,
   158	      prompt: scenario && prompt.trim() === scenario.task_prompt.trim() ? null : prompt,
   159	      conversationId: detail.conversation.id,
   160	    })
   161	  }
   162	
   163	  const title = detail?.conversation.title ?? (loading ? 'Loading…' : 'Conversation')
   164	
   165	  return (
   166	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
   167	      <TopBar
   168	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Conversations', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: title }]}
   169	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
   170	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
   171	      >
   172	        <RunStatusStrip
   173	          state={selected ? rv.state : null}
   174	          transport={rv.transport}
   175	          source={transcriptSource}
   176	          sandbox={selected ? verdict.sandbox : null}
   177	        />
   178	      </TopBar>
   179	
   180	      <RunLayout state={selected ? rv.state : null} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen}>
   181	          {loading && !detail ? (
   182	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-4 px-4 py-8">
   183	              <Skeleton className="ml-auto h-16 w-2/3" />
   184	              <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
   185	              <Skeleton className="h-32 w-full" />
   186	            </div>
   187	          ) : error && !detail ? (
   188	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-8">
   189	              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200">
   190	                <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
   191	                <div>
   192	                  <div className="font-medium">Conversation not available</div>
   193	                  <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-all opacity-80">{error}</div>
   194	                  <a className="mt-1 inline-block underline" {...linkProps({ kind: 'home' })}>
   195	                    Start a new run
   196	                  </a>
   197	                </div>
   198	              </div>
   199	            </div>
   200	          ) : (
   201	            <TranscriptView
   202	              transcript={transcript}
   203	              diffs={rv.state.diffs}
   204	              maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps || scenario?.max_steps || 20}
   205	              loading={!!selected && rv.loading}
   206	              loadingLabel="Loading run…"
   207	              error={rv.loadError}
   208	              transport={rv.transport}
   209	              verdict={selected ? verdict : null}
   210	              footer={
   211	                <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
   212	                  {detail ? (
   213	                    <RunSwitcher conversationId={detail.conversation.id} runs={runs} selectedId={selected?.id ?? null} />
   214	                  ) : null}
   215	                  {detail && runs.length === 0 ? (
   216	                    <p className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
   217	                      No runs in this conversation yet — send the task below to start one.
   218	                    </p>
   219	                  ) : null}
   220	                </div>
   221	              }
   222	            />
   223	          )}
   224	
   225	          {detail ? (
   226	            <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-border bg-background/90 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur md:px-6">
   227	              <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-2">
   228	                <Composer
   229	                  scenarios={scenario ? [scenario] : harness.scenarios}
   230	                  scenarioId={detail.conversation.scenario_id}
   231	                  onScenarioChange={() => undefined}
   232	                  models={MODEL_ALLOWLIST}
   233	                  model={model}
   234	                  onModelChange={setModel}
   235	                  seed={seed}
   236	                  onSeedChange={setSeed}
   237	                  prompt={prompt}
   238	                  onPromptChange={setPrompt}
   239	                  onSubmit={submit}
   240	                  disabled={!harness.reachable || live || busy}
   241	                  busy={busy}
   242	                  placeholder={live ? 'A run is in progress…' : 'Edit the task and press enter to run it again in this conversation'}
   243	                  hint={
   244	                    live
   245	                      ? 'wait for the current run to finish'
   246	                      : `${detail.conversation.scenario_id} · another run in this conversation`
   247	                  }
   248	                />
   249	                {startError ? (
   250	                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-destructive">
   251	                    <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
   252	                    <span className="break-all">Could not start the run: {startError}</span>
   253	                  </div>
   254	                ) : null}
   255	              </div>
   256	            </div>
   257	          ) : null}
   258	      </RunLayout>
   259	    </div>
   260	  )
   261	}

     2	 * Pure fold: Event[] -> ViewState.
     3	 *
     4	 * Everything the UI renders comes from here, so the same function drives a live SSE run and a
     5	 * bundled replay, and the interesting derivations (which call a fault hit, whether the agent
     6	 * recovered) are unit-testable without a browser.
     7	 *
     8	 * Derivations worth naming:
     9	 *   - `mutating` / `read` classification and argv-token path matching mirror
    10	 *     services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md so the badge in the UI and the grader's checks agree.
    11	 *   - `recovered`: a faulted call is recovered when a later call on the same path succeeds AND a
    12	 *     successful *read* of that path happened at or before that success. Retrying blind does not
    13	 *     count — that is precisely the behaviour the `lost-ack` scenario grades.

99	    99	}
100	   100	
101	   101	/** One `messages.create` round trip (`llm.call`). Retries share a step with `attempt > 1`. */
102	   102	export interface LlmCallView {
103	   103	  step: number
104	   104	  attempt: number
105	   105	  model: string
106	   106	  stopReason: string | null
107	   107	  usage: Usage
108	   108	  durationMs: number | null
109	   109	  requestId: string | null
110	   110	  error: string | null
111	   111	}
112	   112	
113	   113	export interface ViewState {
114	   114	  runId: string | null
115	   115	  status: RunStatus
116	   116	  scenarioId: string | null
117	   117	  model: string | null
118	   118	  seed: number | null
119	   119	  maxSteps: number
120	   120	  episodeId: string | null
121	   121	  taskPrompt: string | null
122	   122	  anthropicWorkspace: string | null
123	   123	  startedAt: string | null
124	   124	  finishedAt: string | null
125	   125	  /** Highest step seen so far. */
126	   126	  step: number
127	   127	  /**
128	   128	   * Token totals. Accumulated from `llm.call` while the run is in flight (last attempt per step,
129	   129	   * so a retried request is not double counted); `run.finished` and the RunRecord always win.
130	   130	   */
131	   131	  usage: Usage
132	   132	  /** Every LLM round trip seen, in event order. */
133	   133	  llmCalls: LlmCallView[]
134	   134	  steps: StepView[]
135	   135	  files: FileEntry[]
136	   136	  diffs: FileDiff[]
137	   137	  faults: FaultFired[]
138	   138	  evaluation: EvaluateResponse | null
139	   139	  logs: LogLine[]
140	   140	  error: string | null
141	   141	  durationMs: number | null
142	   142	  lastEventId: number
143	   143	  eventCount: number
144	   144	  /** Every real interruption (`interruption` events; the RunRecord's list wins on seed). */
145	   145	  interruptions: Interruption[]
146	   146	  /** Every `run.resumed` (a fresh worker picked the run up), in order. */
147	   147	  resumes: RunResumedData[]
148	   148	  /** 1 + number of resumes (highest worker_generation seen). */
149	   149	  workerGeneration: number
150	   150	  /** Run-level classification: `run.finished.data.error_class` or `RunRecord.error_class`. */
151	   151	  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
152	   152	  /** The public scenario, when `episode.reset` carried it. */
153	   153	  scenario: Scenario | null
154	   154	  /** `episode.sandbox` notices (sandbox alive / terminated / replaced), in order. */
155	   155	  sandboxEvents: EpisodeSandboxData[]
156	   156	  /** Short sandbox id last seen (episode.reset / episode.sandbox / tool.result.sandbox). */
157	   157	  sandboxId: string | null
158	   158	  /** Last reported liveness; null until something reports it. */
159	   159	  sandboxAlive: boolean | null
160	   160	  /** `episode.reset.data.attempt` — >1 when the harness re-provisioned the workspace. */
161	   161	  resetAttempt: number | null
162	   162	  /** `run.finished.data.evaluation_status` / `evaluation_error`. */
163	   163	  evaluationStatus: EvaluationStatus | null
164	   164	  evaluationError: string | null
165	   165	}
166	   166	
167	   167	export function initialState(): ViewState {
168	   168	  return {
169	   169	    runId: null,
170	   170	    status: 'queued',
171	   171	    scenarioId: null,
172	   172	    model: null,
173	   173	    seed: null,
174	   174	    maxSteps: 20,
175	   175	    episodeId: null,
176	   176	    taskPrompt: null,
177	   177	    anthropicWorkspace: null,
178	   178	    startedAt: null,
179	   179	    finishedAt: null,
180	   180	    step: 0,
181	   181	    usage: { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 },
182	   182	    llmCalls: [],
183	   183	    steps: [],
184	   184	    files: [],
185	   185	    diffs: [],
186	   186	    faults: [],
187	   187	    evaluation: null,
188	   188	    logs: [],
189	   189	    error: null,
190	   190	    durationMs: null,
191	   191	    lastEventId: -1,
192	   192	    eventCount: 0,
193	   193	    interruptions: [],
194	   194	    resumes: [],
195	   195	    workerGeneration: 1,
196	   196	    errorClass: null,
197	   197	    scenario: null,
198	   198	    sandboxEvents: [],
199	   199	    sandboxId: null,
200	   200	    sandboxAlive: null,
201	   201	    resetAttempt: null,
202	   202	    evaluationStatus: null,
203	   203	    evaluationError: null,
204	   204	  }
205	   205	}
206	   206	
207	   207	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- path / verb helpers
208	   208	
209	   209	/** `"./src/x.py"`, `'/workspace/src/x.py'`, `"src/x.py"` all normalise to `src/x.py`. */
210	   210	export function normalisePath(raw: string): string {
211	   211	  let s = raw.trim()
212	   212	  if (s.length >= 2 && ((s[0] === '"' && s.endsWith('"')) || (s[0] === "'" && s.endsWith("'")))) {
213	   213	    s = s.slice(1, -1)
214	   214	  }
215	   215	  s = s.replace(/^\/workspace\//, '')
216	   216	  s = s.replace(/^\.\//, '')
217	   217	  s = s.replace(/^\/+/, '')
218	   218	  // `ls src/ratelimiter/` and `read_file src/ratelimiter` name the same thing.
219	   219	  if (s.length > 1) s = s.replace(/\/+$/, '')
220	   220	  return s
221	   221	}
222	   222	
223	   223	/** Cheap argv split: whitespace, honouring single/double quotes. Good enough for token matching. */
224	   224	export function argvTokens(command: string): string[] {
225	   225	  const out: string[] = []
226	   226	  const re = /"([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|(\S+)/g
227	   227	  let m: RegExpExecArray | null
228	   228	  while ((m = re.exec(command)) !== null) {
229	   229	    out.push(m[1] ?? m[2] ?? m[3] ?? '')
230	   230	  }
231	   231	  return out
232	   232	}
233	   233	
234	   234	const MUTATING_RE =
235	   235	  /(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))/
236	   236	
237	   237	const PY_WRITE_RE = /python[0-9.]*\s+.*-c[\s\S]*open\([\s\S]*['"][wa]\+?['"]/
238	   238	
239	   239	const READ_VERB_RE =
240	   240	  /\b(cat|head|tail|grep|rg|wc|diff|less|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find|pytest|nl|od)\b|\bsed\s+-n\b|python[0-9.]*\s+-m\s+pytest/
241	   241	
242	   242	export function commandIsMutating(command: string): boolean {
243	   243	  return MUTATING_RE.test(command) || PY_WRITE_RE.test(command)
244	   244	}
245	   245	
246	   246	export function commandIsRead(command: string): boolean {
247	   247	  return !commandIsMutating(command) && READ_VERB_RE.test(command)
248	   248	}
249	   249	
250	   250	/** Does this call touch `path`? Direct for path tools; argv-token match for run_command. */
251	   251	export function callTouches(call: ToolCallView, path: string): boolean {
252	   252	  const target = normalisePath(path)
253	   253	  if (call.tool === 'run_command') {
254	   254	    if (!call.command) return false
255	   255	    return argvTokens(call.command).some((t) => {
256	   256	      const n = normalisePath(t)
257	   257	      return n === target || n.endsWith(`/${target}`)
258	   258	    })
259	   259	  }
260	   260	  return call.path !== null && call.path === target
261	   261	}
262	   262	
263	   263	function inputPath(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
264	   264	  const raw = input?.path
265	   265	  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.length > 0) return normalisePath(raw)
266	   266	  if (tool === 'list_dir') return '.'
267	   267	  return null
268	   268	}
269	   269	
270	   270	export interface CallClassification {
271	   271	  path: string | null
272	   272	  command: string | null
273	   273	  mutating: boolean
274	   274	  read: boolean
275	   275	}
276	   276	
277	   277	/**
278	   278	 * Path / verb classification of a tool call from its name and input. Exported so the persisted
279	   279	 * transcript path (transcript.ts) classifies exactly as the live fold does.
280	   280	 */
281	   281	export function classifyCall(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): CallClassification {
282	   282	  return classify(tool, input)
283	   283	}
284	   284	
285	   285	function classify(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): CallClassification {
286	   286	  if (tool === 'run_command') {
287	   287	    const command = typeof input?.command === 'string' ? (input.command as string) : null
288	   288	    return {
289	   289	      path: null,
290	   290	      command,
291	   291	      mutating: command ? commandIsMutating(command) : false,
292	   292	      read: command ? commandIsRead(command) : false,
293	   293	    }
294	   294	  }
295	   295	  if (tool === 'write_file') {
296	   296	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: true, read: false }
297	   297	  }
298	   298	  if (tool === 'read_file' || tool === 'list_dir') {
299	   299	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: true }
300	   300	  }
301	   301	  // `submit` and anything else the harness adds locally.
302	   302	  return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: false }
303	   303	}
304	   304	
305	   305	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- result parsing
306	   306	
307	   307	export function parseToolResult(
308	   308	  toolUseId: string,
309	   309	  output: string,
310	   310	  isError: boolean,
311	   311	  durationMs: number,
312	   312	): ToolResultView {
313	   313	  const base: ToolResultView = {
314	   314	    toolUseId,
315	   315	    output: output ?? '',
316	   316	    isError,
317	   317	    durationMs,
318	   318	    stdout: null,
319	   319	    stderr: null,
320	   320	    content: null,
321	   321	    exitCode: null,
322	   322	    errorText: null,
323	   323	    errorCode: null,
324	   324	    truncated: false,
325	   325	    bytesWritten: null,
326	   326	    entries: null,
327	   327	    errorClass: null,
328	   328	    outcome: null,
329	   329	    attempts: null,
330	   330	    sandbox: null,
331	   331	  }
332	   332	  let parsed: unknown
333	   333	  try {
334	   334	    parsed = JSON.parse(output)
335	   335	  } catch {
336	   336	    if (isError) base.errorText = output
337	   337	    return base
338	   338	  }
339	   339	  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return base
340	   340	  const o = parsed as Record<string, unknown>
341	   341	  if (typeof o.stdout === 'string') base.stdout = o.stdout
342	   342	  if (typeof o.stderr === 'string') base.stderr = o.stderr
343	   343	  if (typeof o.content === 'string') base.content = o.content
344	   344	  if (typeof o.exit_code === 'number') base.exitCode = o.exit_code
345	   345	  if (typeof o.truncated === 'boolean') base.truncated = o.truncated
346	   346	  if (typeof o.bytes_written === 'number') base.bytesWritten = o.bytes_written
347	   347	  if (typeof o.error === 'string') base.errorText = o.error
348	   348	  if (typeof o.code === 'string' && (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(o.code)) {
349	   349	    base.errorCode = o.code as ErrorCode
350	   350	  }
351	   351	  if (Array.isArray(o.entries)) {
352	   352	    base.entries = o.entries as ToolResultView['entries']
353	   353	  }
354	   354	  return base
355	   355	}
356	   356	
357	   357	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- recovery
358	   358	
359	   359	function flatten(steps: StepView[]): ToolCallView[] {
360	   360	  const all: ToolCallView[] = []
361	   361	  for (const s of steps) all.push(...s.calls)
362	   362	  all.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
363	   363	  return all
364	   364	}
365	   365	
366	   366	/**
367	   367	 * Recompute `recovered` for every faulted call. Cheap (a run is tens of calls) and keeps the flag
368	   368	 * correct as later events arrive.
369	   369	 */
370	   370	export function applyRecovery(steps: StepView[]): StepView[] {
371	   371	  const all = flatten(steps)
372	   372	  const recoveredIds = new Set<string>()
373	   373	
374	   374	  for (const c of all) {
375	   375	    if (!c.fault) continue
376	   376	    const path = normalisePath(c.fault.path || c.path || '')
377	   377	    if (!path) continue
378	   378	    const later = all.filter((x) => x.seq > c.seq)
379	   379	    const successes = later.filter((x) => x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path))
380	   380	    if (successes.length === 0) continue
381	   381	    const firstSuccessSeq = successes[0]!.seq
382	   382	    const verified = later.some(
383	   383	      (x) => x.seq <= firstSuccessSeq && x.read && x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path),
384	   384	    )
385	   385	    if (verified) recoveredIds.add(c.toolUseId)
386	   386	  }
387	   387	
388	   388	  if (recoveredIds.size === 0) {
389	   389	    return steps.map((s) =>
390	   390	      s.calls.some((c) => c.recovered) ? { ...s, calls: s.calls.map((c) => ({ ...c, recovered: false })) } : s,
391	   391	    )
392	   392	  }
393	   393	  return steps.map((s) => ({
394	   394	    ...s,
395	   395	    calls: s.calls.map((c) => {
396	   396	      const next = recoveredIds.has(c.toolUseId)
397	   397	      return c.recovered === next ? c : { ...c, recovered: next }
398	   398	    }),
399	   399	  }))
400	   400	}
401	   401	
402	   402	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the fold
403	   403	
404	   404	function upsertStep(steps: StepView[], step: number): { steps: StepView[]; idx: number } {
405	   405	  const idx = steps.findIndex((s) => s.step === step)
406	   406	  if (idx >= 0) return { steps, idx }
407	   407	  const next = [...steps, { step, thinking: null, texts: [], calls: [] }].sort((a, b) => a.step - b.step)
408	   408	  return { steps: next, idx: next.findIndex((s) => s.step === step) }
409	   409	}
410	   410	
411	   411	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- usage
412	   412	
413	   413	function asUsage(v: unknown, fallback: Usage = { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 }): Usage {
414	   414	  const o = asRecord(v)
415	   415	  const out: Usage = {
416	   416	    input_tokens: num(o.input_tokens, fallback.input_tokens),
417	   417	    output_tokens: num(o.output_tokens, fallback.output_tokens),
418	   418	  }
419	   419	  const cr = typeof o.cache_read_input_tokens === 'number' ? o.cache_read_input_tokens : fallback.cache_read_input_tokens
420	   420	  const cc =
421	   421	    typeof o.cache_creation_input_tokens === 'number'
422	   422	      ? o.cache_creation_input_tokens
423	   423	      : fallback.cache_creation_input_tokens
424	   424	  if (cr !== undefined) out.cache_read_input_tokens = cr
425	   425	  if (cc !== undefined) out.cache_creation_input_tokens = cc
426	   426	  return out
427	   427	}
428	   428	
429	   429	/**
430	   430	 * Sum token usage over LLM calls, counting only the LAST attempt of each step so a request that
431	   431	 * was retried after a 429/5xx is not billed twice in the header.
432	   432	 */
433	   433	export function sumLlmUsage(calls: LlmCallView[]): Usage {
434	   434	  const lastPerStep = new Map<number, LlmCallView>()
435	   435	  for (const c of calls) {
436	   436	    const prev = lastPerStep.get(c.step)
437	   437	    if (!prev || c.attempt >= prev.attempt) lastPerStep.set(c.step, c)
438	   438	  }
439	   439	  const out: Usage = { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 }
440	   440	  let cr = 0
441	   441	  let cc = 0
442	   442	  let sawCache = false
443	   443	  for (const c of lastPerStep.values()) {
444	   444	    out.input_tokens += c.usage.input_tokens
445	   445	    out.output_tokens += c.usage.output_tokens
446	   446	    if (typeof c.usage.cache_read_input_tokens === 'number') {
447	   447	      cr += c.usage.cache_read_input_tokens
448	   448	      sawCache = true
449	   449	    }
450	   450	    if (typeof c.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens === 'number') {
451	   451	      cc += c.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens
452	   452	      sawCache = true
453	   453	    }
454	   454	  }
455	   455	  if (sawCache) {
456	   456	    out.cache_read_input_tokens = cr
457	   457	    out.cache_creation_input_tokens = cc
458	   458	  }
459	   459	  return out
460	   460	}
461	   461	
462	   462	function usageIsEmpty(u: Usage | null | undefined): boolean {
463	   463	  return !u || ((u.input_tokens ?? 0) === 0 && (u.output_tokens ?? 0) === 0)
464	   464	}
465	   465	
466	   466	function asRecord(v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
467	   467	  return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
468	   468	}
469	   469	
470	   470	function num(v: unknown, fallback: number): number {
471	   471	  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fallback
472	   472	}
473	   473	
474	   474	function str(v: unknown): string | null {
475	   475	  return typeof v === 'string' ? v : null
476	   476	}
477	   477	
478	   478	function asOrigin(v: unknown): ErrorOrigin | null {
479	   479	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_ORIGINS as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorOrigin) : null
480	   480	}
481	   481	
482	   482	function asLayer(v: unknown): ErrorLayer | null {
483	   483	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_LAYERS as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorLayer) : null
484	   484	}
485	   485	
486	   486	function asCode(v: unknown): ErrorCode | null {
487	   487	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorCode) : null
488	   488	}
489	   489	
490	   490	function asFault(v: unknown): FaultFired | null {
491	   491	  const o = asRecord(v)
492	   492	  if (typeof o.kind !== 'string' || typeof o.path !== 'string') return null
493	   493	  const fault: FaultFired = {
494	   494	    step: num(o.step, 0),
495	   495	    kind: o.kind as FaultFired['kind'],
496	   496	    path: o.path,
497	   497	    mode: (o.mode as FaultFired['mode']) ?? 'transient',
498	   498	  }
499	   499	  const origin = asOrigin(o.origin)
500	   500	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
501	   501	  if (origin) fault.origin = origin
502	   502	  if (layer) fault.layer = layer
503	   503	  if (typeof o.description === 'string' && o.description.length > 0) fault.description = o.description
504	   504	  return fault
505	   505	}
506	   506	
507	   507	/** Structural check for an ErrorClass payload; anything short of (origin, layer, code, label) is ignored. */
508	   508	export function asErrorClass(v: unknown): ErrorClass | null {
509	   509	  const o = asRecord(v)
510	   510	  const origin = asOrigin(o.origin)
511	   511	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
512	   512	  const code = asCode(o.code)
513	   513	  if (!origin || !layer || !code || typeof o.label !== 'string') return null
514	   514	  return {
515	   515	    origin,
516	   516	    layer,
517	   517	    code,
518	   518	    kind: typeof o.kind === 'string' ? (o.kind as ErrorClass['kind']) : null,
519	   519	    label: o.label,
520	   520	    outcome_known: o.outcome_known !== false,
521	   521	    side_effect_applied: typeof o.side_effect_applied === 'boolean' ? o.side_effect_applied : null,
522	   522	    detail: str(o.detail),
523	   523	  }
524	   524	}
525	   525	
526	   526	function asInterruption(v: unknown, fallbackStep: number | null): Interruption | null {
527	   527	  const o = asRecord(v)
528	   528	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
529	   529	  const code = asCode(o.code)
530	   530	  if (!layer || !code) return null
531	   531	  return {
532	   532	    step: typeof o.step === 'number' ? o.step : fallbackStep,
533	   533	    layer,
534	   534	    code,
535	   535	    label: str(o.label) ?? '',
536	   536	    tool_use_id: str(o.tool_use_id),
537	   537	    tool: str(o.tool) as Interruption['tool'],
538	   538	    path: str(o.path),
539	   539	    outcome_known: o.outcome_known === true,
540	   540	    planned: o.planned === true,
541	   541	    resumed: o.resumed === true,
542	   542	    worker_generation: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(o.worker_generation, 1))),
543	   543	    at: str(o.at) ?? '',
544	   544	    detail: str(o.detail),
545	   545	  }
546	   546	}
547	   547	
548	   548	function asToolOutcome(v: unknown): ToolOutcome | null {
549	   549	  return v === 'executed' || v === 'failed' || v === 'not_executed' || v === 'unknown' ? v : null
550	   550	}
551	   551	
552	   552	function asSandboxRef(v: unknown): { id: string; alive: boolean } | null {
553	   553	  const o = asRecord(v)
554	   554	  if (typeof o.id !== 'string') return null
555	   555	  return { id: o.id, alive: o.alive !== false }
556	   556	}
557	   557	
558	   558	function asScenario(v: unknown): Scenario | null {
559	   559	  const o = asRecord(v)
560	   560	  if (typeof o.id !== 'string' || typeof o.title !== 'string') return null
561	   561	  return o as unknown as Scenario
562	   562	}
563	   563	
564	   564	function sameInterruption(a: Interruption, b: Interruption): boolean {
565	   565	  if (a.tool_use_id && b.tool_use_id) return a.tool_use_id === b.tool_use_id
566	   566	  return a.at === b.at && a.code === b.code && (a.step ?? null) === (b.step ?? null)
567	   567	}
568	   568	
569	   569	function nextSeq(steps: StepView[]): number {
570	   570	  let max = -1
571	   571	  for (const s of steps) for (const c of s.calls) if (c.seq > max) max = c.seq
572	   572	  return max + 1
573	   573	}
574	   574	
575	   575	/** Fold one event into the view state. Pure: never mutates `state`. */
576	   576	export function reduce(state: ViewState, ev: Event): ViewState {
577	   577	  if (!ev || typeof ev.type !== 'string') return state
578	   578	  const data = asRecord(ev.data)
579	   579	  const step = typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : state.step
580	   580	  const next: ViewState = {
581	   581	    ...state,
582	   582	    runId: state.runId ?? str(ev.run_id),
583	   583	    lastEventId: typeof ev.id === 'number' && ev.id > state.lastEventId ? ev.id : state.lastEventId,
584	   584	    eventCount: state.eventCount + 1,
585	   585	    step: Math.max(state.step, typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : 0),
586	   586	  }
587	   587	
588	   588	  switch (ev.type) {
589	   589	    case 'run.started': {
590	   590	      next.status = 'running'
591	   591	      next.scenarioId = str(data.scenario_id) ?? next.scenarioId
592	   592	      next.model = str(data.model) ?? next.model
593	   593	      next.seed = typeof data.seed === 'number' ? data.seed : next.seed
594	   594	      next.maxSteps = num(data.max_steps, next.maxSteps)
595	   595	      next.anthropicWorkspace = str(data.anthropic_workspace) ?? next.anthropicWorkspace
596	   596	      next.startedAt = state.startedAt ?? str(ev.ts)
597	   597	      return next
598	   598	    }
599	   599	
600	   600	    case 'episode.reset': {
601	   601	      next.episodeId = str(data.episode_id) ?? next.episodeId
602	   602	      next.taskPrompt = str(data.task_prompt) ?? next.taskPrompt
603	   603	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
604	   604	      if (next.status === 'queued') next.status = 'running'
605	   605	      next.scenario = asScenario(data.scenario) ?? next.scenario
606	   606	      const sandboxId = str(data.sandbox_id)
607	   607	      if (sandboxId) {
608	   608	        next.sandboxId = sandboxId
609	   609	        next.sandboxAlive = true
610	   610	      }
611	   611	      if (typeof data.attempt === 'number') next.resetAttempt = data.attempt
612	   612	      return next
613	   613	    }
614	   614	
615	   615	    case 'turn.text': {
616	   616	      const text = str(data.text)
617	   617	      if (!text || text.trim().length === 0) return next
618	   618	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
619	   619	      const target = steps[idx]!
620	   620	      const updated = [...steps]
621	   621	      updated[idx] = { ...target, texts: [...target.texts, text] }
622	   622	      next.steps = updated
623	   623	      return next
624	   624	    }
625	   625	
626	   626	    case 'turn.thinking': {
627	   627	      const text = str(data.text)
628	   628	      if (!text || text.trim().length === 0) return next
629	   629	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
630	   630	      const target = steps[idx]!
631	   631	      const updated = [...steps]
632	   632	      updated[idx] = { ...target, thinking: target.thinking ? `${target.thinking}\n\n${text}` : text }
633	   633	      next.steps = updated
634	   634	      return next
635	   635	    }
636	   636	
637	   637	    case 'llm.call': {
638	   638	      const view: LlmCallView = {
639	   639	        step,
640	   640	        attempt: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(data.attempt, 1))),
641	   641	        model: str(data.model) ?? state.model ?? 'unknown',
642	   642	        stopReason: str(data.stop_reason),
643	   643	        usage: asUsage(data.usage),
644	   644	        durationMs: typeof data.duration_ms === 'number' ? data.duration_ms : null,
645	   645	        requestId: str(data.request_id),
646	   646	        error: str(data.error),
647	   647	      }
648	   648	      // Same (step, attempt) twice means a replayed frame: replace rather than append.
649	   649	      const i = state.llmCalls.findIndex((c) => c.step === view.step && c.attempt === view.attempt)
650	   650	      const llmCalls = i >= 0 ? state.llmCalls.map((c, j) => (j === i ? view : c)) : [...state.llmCalls, view]
651	   651	      next.llmCalls = llmCalls
652	   652	      // `run.finished` (or a terminal record) is the authoritative total; before that, keep a
653	   653	      // running sum so the header moves while the run is in flight.
654	   654	      if (!isTerminal(state.status)) next.usage = sumLlmUsage(llmCalls)
655	   655	      return next
656	   656	    }
657	   657	
658	   658	    case 'tool.call': {
659	   659	      const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
660	   660	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id) ?? `${ev.id}`
661	   661	      const input = asRecord(data.input)
662	   662	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
663	   663	      const target = steps[idx]!
664	   664	      if (target.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) {
665	   665	        next.steps = steps
666	   666	        return next
667	   667	      }
668	   668	      const cls = classify(tool, input)
669	   669	      const call: ToolCallView = {
670	   670	        seq: nextSeq(steps),
671	   671	        step,
672	   672	        toolUseId,
673	   673	        tool,
674	   674	        input,
675	   675	        path: cls.path,
676	   676	        command: cls.command,
677	   677	        mutating: cls.mutating,
678	   678	        read: cls.read,
679	   679	        result: null,
680	   680	        fault: null,
681	   681	        recovered: false,
682	   682	      }
683	   683	      const updated = [...steps]
684	   684	      updated[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, call] }
685	   685	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
686	   686	      return next
687	   687	    }
688	   688	
689	   689	    case 'tool.result': {
690	   690	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id)
691	   691	      if (!toolUseId) return next
692	   692	      const isError = data.is_error === true
693	   693	      const result = parseToolResult(
694	   694	        toolUseId,
695	   695	        str(data.output) ?? '',
696	   696	        isError,
697	   697	        num(data.duration_ms, 0),
698	   698	      )
699	   699	      result.errorClass = isError ? asErrorClass(data.error_class) : null
700	   700	      result.outcome = asToolOutcome(data.outcome)
701	   701	      result.attempts = typeof data.attempts === 'number' ? Math.max(1, Math.trunc(data.attempts)) : null
702	   702	      result.sandbox = asSandboxRef(data.sandbox)
703	   703	      if (result.sandbox) {
704	   704	        next.sandboxId = result.sandbox.id
705	   705	        next.sandboxAlive = result.sandbox.alive
706	   706	      }
707	   707	      const fault = asFault(data.fault)
708	   708	      let found = false
709	   709	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
710	   710	        if (!s.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) return s
711	   711	        found = true
712	   712	        return {
713	   713	          ...s,
714	   714	          calls: s.calls.map((c) =>
715	   715	            c.toolUseId === toolUseId ? { ...c, result, fault: fault ?? c.fault } : c,
716	   716	          ),
717	   717	        }
718	   718	      })
719	   719	      if (!found) {
720	   720	        // A result without its call (resumed mid-run, or events dropped): synthesise the call so
721	   721	        // the timeline still shows the output rather than silently losing it.
722	   722	        const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
723	   723	        const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
724	   724	        const target = steps[idx]!
725	   725	        const cls = classify(tool, {})
726	   726	        const synth: ToolCallView = {
727	   727	          seq: nextSeq(steps),
728	   728	          step,
729	   729	          toolUseId,
730	   730	          tool,
731	   731	          input: {},
732	   732	          path: cls.path,
733	   733	          command: cls.command,
734	   734	          mutating: cls.mutating,
735	   735	          read: cls.read,
736	   736	          result,
737	   737	          fault,
738	   738	          recovered: false,
739	   739	        }
740	   740	        const s2 = [...steps]
741	   741	        s2[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, synth] }
742	   742	        next.steps = applyRecovery(s2)
743	   743	        return next
744	   744	      }
745	   745	      if (fault && !state.faults.some((f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind)) {
746	   746	        next.faults = [...state.faults, fault]
747	   747	      }
748	   748	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
749	   749	      return next
750	   750	    }
751	   751	
752	   752	    case 'fault.fired': {
753	   753	      const fault = asFault(data) ?? asFault(data.fault)
754	   754	      if (!fault) return next
755	   755	      const known = state.faults.some(
756	   756	        (f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind,
757	   757	      )
758	   758	      next.faults = known ? state.faults : [...state.faults, fault]
759	   759	      // Attach to the call at that step that touched the path and has no fault yet.
760	   760	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
761	   761	        if (s.step !== fault.step) return s
762	   762	        let attached = false
763	   763	        const calls = s.calls.map((c) => {
764	   764	          if (attached || c.fault) return c
765	   765	          if (!callTouches(c, fault.path) && c.path !== normalisePath(fault.path)) return c
766	   766	          attached = true
767	   767	          return { ...c, fault }
768	   768	        })
769	   769	        return attached ? { ...s, calls } : s
770	   770	      })
771	   771	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
772	   772	      return next
773	   773	    }
774	   774	
775	   775	    case 'workspace.diff': {
776	   776	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
777	   777	      if (Array.isArray(data.diffs)) next.diffs = data.diffs as FileDiff[]
778	   778	      return next
779	   779	    }
780	   780	
781	   781	    case 'episode.evaluated': {
782	   782	      const checks = Array.isArray(data.checks) ? (data.checks as Check[]) : []
783	   783	      next.evaluation = {
784	   784	        episode_id: str(data.episode_id) ?? state.episodeId ?? '',
785	   785	        score: num(data.score, 0),
786	   786	        passed: data.passed === true,
787	   787	        checks,
788	   788	        tests: asRecord(data.tests) as unknown as EvaluateResponse['tests'],
789	   789	        ledger: Array.isArray(data.ledger) ? (data.ledger as EvaluateResponse['ledger']) : [],
790	   790	      }
791	   791	      return next
792	   792	    }
793	   793	
794	   794	    case 'run.finished': {
795	   795	      const status = str(data.status)
796	   796	      next.status = (status as RunStatus) ?? 'ok'
797	   797	      next.usage = asUsage(data.usage, state.usage)
798	   798	      next.durationMs = typeof data.duration_ms === 'number' ? data.duration_ms : state.durationMs
799	   799	      next.error = str(data.error) ?? state.error
800	   800	      next.finishedAt = str(ev.ts) ?? state.finishedAt
801	   801	      next.errorClass = asErrorClass(data.error_class) ?? state.errorClass
802	   802	      const es = str(data.evaluation_status)
803	   803	      if (es === 'ok' || es === 'failed' || es === 'skipped') next.evaluationStatus = es
804	   804	      next.evaluationError = str(data.evaluation_error) ?? state.evaluationError
805	   805	      return next
806	   806	    }
807	   807	
808	   808	    case 'interruption': {
809	   809	      const it = asInterruption(data, typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : null)
810	   810	      if (!it) return next
811	   811	      const i = state.interruptions.findIndex((x) => sameInterruption(x, it))
812	   812	      next.interruptions = i >= 0 ? state.interruptions.map((x, j) => (j === i ? it : x)) : [...state.interruptions, it]
813	   813	      next.workerGeneration = Math.max(state.workerGeneration, it.worker_generation)
814	   814	      return next
815	   815	    }
816	   816	
817	   817	    case 'run.resumed': {
818	   818	      const gen = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(data.worker_generation, state.workerGeneration + 1)))
819	   819	      const resumed: RunResumedData = {
820	   820	        worker_generation: gen,
821	   821	        resumed_from_event_id: num(data.resumed_from_event_id, state.lastEventId),
822	   822	        dangling_tool_use_id: str(data.dangling_tool_use_id),
823	   823	        resumed_at: str(data.resumed_at) ?? str(ev.ts) ?? '',
824	   824	      }
825	   825	      const known = state.resumes.some((r) => r.worker_generation === gen)
826	   826	      next.resumes = known ? state.resumes : [...state.resumes, resumed]
827	   827	      next.workerGeneration = Math.max(state.workerGeneration, gen)
828	   828	      // The interruption this resume answers (matched by the dangling call) is now `resumed`.
829	   829	      const dangling = resumed.dangling_tool_use_id
830	   830	      if (dangling) {
831	   831	        next.interruptions = state.interruptions.map((x) =>
832	   832	          x.tool_use_id === dangling && !x.resumed ? { ...x, resumed: true } : x,
833	   833	        )
834	   834	      }
835	   835	      if (!isTerminal(state.status)) next.status = 'running'
836	   836	      return next
837	   837	    }
838	   838	
839	   839	    case 'episode.sandbox': {
840	   840	      const sandboxId = str(data.sandbox_id)
841	   841	      const status = str(data.status)
842	   842	      if (!sandboxId || (status !== 'alive' && status !== 'terminated' && status !== 'replaced')) return next
843	   843	      const notice: EpisodeSandboxData = {
844	   844	        sandbox_id: sandboxId,
845	   845	        status,
846	   846	        reason: str(data.reason),
847	   847	        step: typeof data.step === 'number' ? data.step : typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : null,
848	   848	      }
849	   849	      next.sandboxEvents = [...state.sandboxEvents, notice]
850	   850	      next.sandboxId = sandboxId
851	   851	      next.sandboxAlive = status === 'alive'
852	   852	      return next
853	   853	    }
854	   854	
855	   855	    case 'log': {
856	   856	      const line = data as unknown as LogLine
857	   857	      if (typeof line?.ev !== 'string') return next
858	   858	      next.logs = [...state.logs, line].slice(-LOG_BUFFER_LIMIT)
859	   859	      return next
860	   860	    }
861	   861	
862	   862	    default:
863	   863	      return next
864	   864	  }
865	   865	}
866	   866	
867	   867	export function reduceAll(events: Event[], from: ViewState = initialState()): ViewState {
868	   868	  return events.reduce(reduce, from)
869	   869	}
870	   870	
871	   871	/**
872	   872	 * Seed the view from a whole RunRecord (page refresh mid-run, or a bundled demo). Record-level
873	   873	 * fields win over anything the events implied, because the record is the harness's own summary.
874	   874	 */
875	   875	export function fromRunRecord(rec: RunRecord): ViewState {
876	   876	  const folded = reduceAll(rec.events ?? [])
877	   877	  // The record's usage is the harness's own running total and wins whenever it says anything.
878	   878	  // The one exception is an in-flight record whose usage is still the {0,0} default while its
879	   879	  // events already carry `llm.call` sums — showing zeros there would be a regression.
880	   880	  const usage =
881	   881	    rec.usage && (isTerminal(rec.status) || !usageIsEmpty(rec.usage) || usageIsEmpty(folded.usage))
882	   882	      ? rec.usage
883	   883	      : folded.usage
884	   884	  return {
885	   885	    ...folded,
886	   886	    runId: rec.run_id ?? folded.runId,
887	   887	    status: rec.status ?? folded.status,
888	   888	    scenarioId: rec.scenario_id ?? folded.scenarioId,
889	   889	    model: rec.model ?? folded.model,
890	   890	    seed: rec.seed ?? folded.seed,
891	   891	    maxSteps: rec.max_steps ?? folded.maxSteps,
892	   892	    episodeId: rec.episode_id ?? folded.episodeId,
893	   893	    taskPrompt: rec.task_prompt ?? folded.taskPrompt,
894	   894	    startedAt: rec.created_at ?? folded.startedAt,
895	   895	    finishedAt: rec.finished_at ?? folded.finishedAt,
896	   896	    evaluation: rec.evaluation ?? folded.evaluation,
897	   897	    usage,
898	   898	    error: rec.error ?? folded.error,
899	   899	    errorClass: rec.error_class ?? folded.errorClass,
900	   900	    interruptions:
901	   901	      Array.isArray(rec.interruptions) && rec.interruptions.length > 0 ? rec.interruptions : folded.interruptions,
902	   902	    workerGeneration: Math.max(
903	   903	      folded.workerGeneration,
904	   904	      typeof rec.worker_generation === 'number' ? rec.worker_generation : 1,
905	   905	    ),
906	   906	  }
907	   907	}
908	   908	
909	   909	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- selectors
910	   910	
911	   911	export function allCalls(state: ViewState): ToolCallView[] {
912	   912	  return flatten(state.steps)
913	   913	}
914	   914	
915	   915	export function faultCount(state: ViewState): number {
916	   916	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault).length
917	   917	}
918	   918	
919	   919	export function recoveredCount(state: ViewState): number {
920	   920	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
921	   921	}
922	   922	
923	   923	export function elapsedMs(state: ViewState, now: number = Date.now()): number | null {
924	   924	  if (state.durationMs !== null) return state.durationMs
925	   925	  if (!state.startedAt) return null
926	   926	  const t0 = Date.parse(state.startedAt)
927	   927	  if (Number.isNaN(t0)) return null
928	   928	  const end = state.finishedAt ? Date.parse(state.finishedAt) : now
929	   929	  return Math.max(0, (Number.isNaN(end) ? now : end) - t0)
930	   930	}
