Tool reply2026-09-12T23:19:44.435Z
     1	/**
     2	 * The ten-second reliability read of a run, as data.
     3	 *
     4	 * Contract (ARCHITECTURE.md §5.4, docs/error-taxonomy.md): every field here comes from structured
     5	 * event fields — `error_class`, `fault.fired`, `interruption`, `run.resumed`, the evaluate ledger,
     6	 * `episode.evaluated`, `workspace.diff` — never from parsing an error message. Anything the record
     7	 * does not carry is `unknown`, and the raw payloads behind every panel are kept for an expander.
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import type { ErrorClass, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, Interruption, RunStatus } from './types'
    11	
    12	export type PanelStatus = 'ok' | 'warn' | 'fail' | 'unknown' | 'pending'
    13	
    14	export type PanelKey = 'task' | 'fault' | 'execution' | 'recovery' | 'result'
    15	
    16	/** A raw payload shown under "details": the event(s) or record fields a panel was built from. */
    17	export interface RawRef {
    18	  label: string
    19	  json: unknown
    20	}
    21	
    22	export interface Fact {
    23	  label: string
    24	  value: string
    25	  tone?: PanelStatus
    26	  /** Plain-language explanation shown in a tooltip / expander (e.g. what a code means). */
    27	  hint?: string
    28	}
    29	
    30	export interface VerdictPanel {
    31	  key: PanelKey
    32	  status: PanelStatus
    33	  /** The one line a reviewer reads in ten seconds (≤ ~70 chars). */
    34	  headline: string
    35	  /** One or two plain-language sentences. */
    36	  plain: string
    37	  facts: Fact[]
    38	  raw: RawRef[]
    39	}
    40	
    41	/** How a failed/faulted call actually ended, and where that knowledge comes from. */
    42	export type ExecutionOutcome = 'executed' | 'failed' | 'not_executed' | 'unknown'
    43	export type ExecutionSource = 'outcome' | 'error_class' | 'ledger' | 'exit_code' | 'none'
    44	
    45	export interface FollowUp {
    46	  step: number
    47	  tool: string
    48	  toolUseId: string
    49	  kind: 'read' | 'write' | 'other'
    50	  ok: boolean | null
    51	}
    52	
    53	/** One failure the run hit: an injected/staged fault, a real error on a call, or an interruption. */
    54	export interface Incident {
    55	  id: string
    56	  step: number | null
    57	  tool: string | null
    58	  toolUseId: string | null
    59	  path: string | null
    60	  origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown'
    61	  layer: ErrorLayer | null
    62	  code: string | null
    63	  /** ErrorClass.label verbatim when present, else the fixed label for (origin, kind) from the taxonomy, else null. */
    64	  label: string | null
    65	  kind: string | null
    66	  /** interruption.planned — a deliberate real failure (harness_faults). null when not an interruption. */
    67	  planned: boolean | null
    68	  execution: ExecutionOutcome
    69	  executionSource: ExecutionSource
    70	  /** error_class.side_effect_applied, or the ledger's answer once evaluate released it. */
    71	  sideEffectApplied: boolean | null
    72	  outcomeKnown: boolean | null
    73	  /** Later calls touching the same path, in order — the agent's recovery actions. */
    74	  followUps: FollowUp[]
    75	  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
    76	  interruption: Interruption | null
    77	  /** tool.result.data.attempts — harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); null when absent. */
    78	  attempts?: number | null
    79	  /** fault.fired.data.description — one plain sentence from the environment, when present. */
    80	  description?: string | null
    81	  raw: RawRef[]
    82	}
    83	
    84	export interface GradingCheck {
    85	  id: string
    86	  ok: boolean
    87	  weight: number
    88	  /** Plain language for the check id (src/lib/checks.ts), falling back to the id. */
    89	  description: string
    90	  detail: string
    91	}
    92	
    93	export interface Grading {
    94	  /** true once episode.evaluated arrived */
    95	  available: boolean
    96	  /** run.finished.data.evaluation_status when present ("ok" | "failed" | "skipped"). */
    97	  evaluationStatus?: string | null
    98	  score: number | null
    99	  tests: { passed: number; failed: number; errors: number } | null
   100	  checks: GradingCheck[]
   101	  /** Why grading did not happen (status unevaluated / interrupted / error): the ErrorClass label. */
   102	  blockedBy: string | null
   103	}
   104	
   105	export interface SandboxSummary {
   106	  episodeId: string | null
   107	  /** episode.reset.data.sandbox_id / episode.sandbox / tool.result.data.sandbox.id (short form), when present. */
   108	  sandboxId?: string | null
   109	  /** Last known liveness from tool.result.data.sandbox.alive / episode.sandbox.status; null when never reported. */
   110	  alive?: boolean | null
   111	  /** episode.reset.data.attempt — >1 when the harness re-provisioned the workspace. */
   112	  attempt?: number | null
   113	  workerGeneration: number
   114	  resumes: number
   115	  /** true = an interruption/error_class with layer "sandbox" was observed; false = the run was graded inside it; null = cannot say. */
   116	  lost: boolean | null
   117	  lostAtStep: number | null
   118	}

   121	  files: number
   122	  added: number
   123	  removed: number

   125	}

   127	export interface Verdict {
   128	  status: RunStatus | null
   129	  live: boolean
   130	  panels: Record<PanelKey, VerdictPanel>
   131	  incidents: Incident[]
   132	  sandbox: SandboxSummary
   133	  grading: Grading
   134	  diff: DiffSummary | null
   135	}
/**
 * The reliability verdict: five questions about a run, answered from structured fields only.
 *
 *   task       what was the agent asked to do, what was going to go wrong, what is graded
 *   fault      what failed and where — simulated vs real, which layer
 *   execution  did the failed call execute? (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are
 *              DIFFERENT answers, and each one says where the knowledge came from)
 *   recovery   what the agent did next, what the harness did (retries, workers), sandbox fate,
 *              and — once graded — the ledger-based recovery checks
 *   result     the workspace diff and the independent grade (hidden tests + checks)
 *
 * Sources, and nothing else: `tool.result.data.{outcome,error_class,fault,attempts,sandbox}`,
 * `fault.fired` (origin defaults to `injected` per the schema), `interruption`, `run.resumed`,
 * `episode.sandbox`, `episode.reset`, `run.finished.data` (incl. `error_class`,
 * `evaluation_status`), the RunRecord's `error_class / interruptions / worker_generation / status`,
 * `episode.evaluated` (ledger / checks / tests / score), `workspace.diff`, `llm.call.attempt`, and
 * exit codes. Never the text of `output` / `error`. When a field is absent the answer is
 * `unknown`, and the panel says why. The only static tables applied are the taxonomy's own
 * (src/lib/codes.ts) and the check-id dictionary (src/lib/checks.ts).
 */

import { checkText, isRecoveryCheck } from './checks'
import {
  codeForInjectedKind,
  codeText,
  exitCodeText,
  layerText,
  originPlain,
  originText,
  taxonomyLabel,
} from './codes'
import { firstSentence, plural } from './format'
import { allCalls, argvTokens, callTouches, isTerminal, normalisePath, type ToolCallView, type ViewState } from './reducer'
import type {
  DiffSummary,
  ExecutionOutcome,
  ExecutionSource,
  Fact,
  FollowUp,
  Grading,
  GradingCheck,
  Incident,
  PanelStatus,
  RawRef,
  SandboxSummary,
  Verdict,
  VerdictPanel,
} from './reliability.types'
import type { ErrorClass, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, FaultFired, Interruption, LedgerEntry, Scenario } from './types'

export interface VerdictOptions {
  /** Override "is the run still producing events" (default: `!isTerminal(state.status)`). */
  live?: boolean
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- incidents

/** Everything known about one failure before it is flattened into an `Incident`. */
interface Seed {
  call: ToolCallView | null
  fault: FaultFired | null
  interruption: Interruption | null
  /** `tool.result.data.error_class` (per call) or the run-level class for a run-level seed. */
  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
  runLevel: boolean
}

function sameFault(a: FaultFired, b: FaultFired): boolean {
  return a.step === b.step && a.kind === b.kind && normalisePath(a.path) === normalisePath(b.path)
}

function seedOrder(a: Seed, b: Seed): number {
  const sa = a.call?.seq ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  const sb = b.call?.seq ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  if (sa !== sb) return sa - sb
  const ta = a.fault?.step ?? a.interruption?.step ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  const tb = b.fault?.step ?? b.interruption?.step ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  return ta - tb
}

/**
 * Gather the run's failures: every faulted call, every error result, every orphan `fault.fired`,
 * every `interruption`, plus the run-level `error_class` when nothing else carries it. A call is
 * one seed even when the environment reported it three ways (fault.fired + tool.result.fault +
 * tool.result.error_class), and an interruption merges into the call it names.
 */
function collectSeeds(state: ViewState): Seed[] {
  const calls = allCalls(state)
  const seeds: Seed[] = []
  const byToolUse = new Map<string, Seed>()

  for (const c of calls) {
    const isErr = c.result?.isError === true
    if (!isErr && !c.fault) continue
    const seed: Seed = {
      call: c,
      fault: c.fault,
      interruption: null,
      errorClass: c.result?.errorClass ?? null,
      runLevel: false,
    }
    seeds.push(seed)
    byToolUse.set(c.toolUseId, seed)
  }

  for (const f of state.faults) {
    if (calls.some((c) => c.fault && sameFault(c.fault, f))) continue
    const host = seeds.find(
      (s) => s.call && !s.fault && !s.interruption && s.call.result?.isError && callTouches(s.call, f.path),
    )
    if (host) {
      host.fault = f
      continue
    }
    seeds.push({ call: null, fault: f, interruption: null, errorClass: null, runLevel: false })
  }

  for (const it of state.interruptions) {
    const host = it.tool_use_id ? byToolUse.get(it.tool_use_id) : undefined
    if (host) {
      host.interruption = it
      continue
    }
    const call = it.tool_use_id ? (calls.find((c) => c.toolUseId === it.tool_use_id) ?? null) : null
    const seed: Seed = { call, fault: null, interruption: it, errorClass: null, runLevel: false }
    seeds.push(seed)
    if (call) byToolUse.set(call.toolUseId, seed)
  }

  const ec = state.errorClass
  if (ec && (state.status === 'error' || state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted')) {
    const covered = seeds.some(
      (s) =>
        (s.errorClass && s.errorClass.layer === ec.layer && s.errorClass.code === ec.code) ||
        (s.interruption && s.interruption.layer === ec.layer && s.interruption.code === ec.code),
    )
    if (!covered) seeds.push({ call: null, fault: null, interruption: null, errorClass: ec, runLevel: true })
  }

  return seeds.sort(seedOrder)
}

function rowTouches(row: LedgerEntry, path: string | null): boolean {
  if (!path) return true
  if (row.path && normalisePath(row.path) === path) return true
  if (row.command) {
    return argvTokens(row.command).some((t) => {
      const n = normalisePath(t)
      return n === path || n.endsWith(`/${path}`)
    })
  }
  return false
}

/**
 * The evaluate-ledger row for a seed. Injected faults match exactly (the row carries the same
 * FaultFired); interruptions match rows the harness marked `interrupted`; anything else matches
 * the k-th non-ok, non-injected row on the same tool and path, where k is this seed's rank among
 * its peers. Returns null rather than guessing when nothing lines up.
 */
function findLedgerRow(
  ledger: LedgerEntry[],
  seed: Seed,
  tool: string | null,
  path: string | null,
  ordinal: number,
): LedgerEntry | null {
  if (ledger.length === 0 || seed.runLevel) return null
  const f = seed.fault
  if (f) {
    const exact = ledger.find(
      (r) => r.fault && r.fault.kind === f.kind && r.fault.step === f.step && normalisePath(r.fault.path) === normalisePath(f.path),
    )
    if (exact) return exact
    const byStep = ledger.find((r) => r.step === f.step && r.outcome !== 'ok' && rowTouches(r, path))
    if (byStep) return byStep
  }
  if (seed.interruption) {
    const rows = ledger.filter((r) => r.interrupted === true && (!tool || r.tool === tool) && rowTouches(r, path))
    return rows[ordinal] ?? rows[0] ?? null
  }
  if (f) return null
  const rows = ledger.filter((r) => r.outcome !== 'ok' && !r.fault && (!tool || r.tool === tool) && rowTouches(r, path))
  return rows[ordinal] ?? null
}

interface Resolution {
  execution: ExecutionOutcome
  source: ExecutionSource
  sideEffectApplied: boolean | null
  /** One sentence for the panel's `plain`. */
  why: string
}

const FS_CODES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['ENOENT', 'EACCES'])

function fromLedger(row: LedgerEntry): Resolution {
  const where = `ledger row ${row.step}`
  if (row.interrupted) {
    return row.outcome === 'ok'
      ? {
          execution: 'executed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: true,
          why: `${where} shows the call completed although the harness never received the response.`,
        }
      : {
          execution: 'failed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: false,
          why: `${where} shows the call ran and failed; the harness never received the response.`,
        }
  }
  switch (row.outcome) {
    case 'ack_lost':
      return {
        execution: 'executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: true,
        why: `${where} is ack_lost: the operation executed fully and only the response was withheld.`,
      }
    case 'short_circuit':
      return {
        execution: 'not_executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `${where} is short_circuit: sandbox-env answered at the boundary and the sandbox never saw the call.`,
      }
    case 'error': {
      const code = row.error_code ?? null
      if (code && !FS_CODES.has(code)) {
        return {
          execution: 'not_executed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: false,
          why: `${where} is error ${code}: the call was rejected before the sandbox ran it.`,
        }
      }
      return {
        execution: 'failed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `${where} is error${code ? ` ${code}` : ''}: the sandbox ran the call and it failed.`,
      }
    }
    default:
      return {
        execution: 'executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: true,
        why: `${where} is ok: the call completed on the server side.`,
      }
  }
}

/**
 * Execution answer for one seed, in priority order:
 *   1. `tool.result.data.outcome` when the harness stated it (anything but `unknown`)
 *   2. `error_class` when `outcome_known` (side effect true → executed; a filesystem-layer error
 *      means the sandbox really ran it → failed; otherwise → not executed)
 *   3. the evaluate ledger, which resolves the `unknown` cases after the fact
 *   4. `error_class` with `outcome_known: false` and no ledger yet → unknown
 *   5. a non-error result with a non-zero exit code → failed (exit code)
 *   6. otherwise unknown — and the reason is spelled out (predates classification, no ledger…)
 */
function resolveExecution(seed: Seed, row: LedgerEntry | null, graded: boolean, live: boolean): Resolution {
  const r = seed.call?.result ?? null
  const ec = seed.errorClass

  if (r?.outcome && r.outcome !== 'unknown') {
    const sideEffect =
      r.outcome === 'executed' ? true : r.outcome === 'not_executed' ? false : (ec?.side_effect_applied ?? false)
    return {
      execution: r.outcome,
      source: 'outcome',
      sideEffectApplied: sideEffect,
      why: `the harness reported the call as ${r.outcome.replace('_', ' ')}.`,
    }
  }

  if (ec && ec.outcome_known) {
    if (ec.side_effect_applied === true) {
      return { execution: 'executed', source: 'error_class', sideEffectApplied: true, why: 'the error class records that the side effect applied.' }
    }
    if (ec.layer === 'filesystem') {
      return {
        execution: 'failed',
        source: 'error_class',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `a ${ec.origin === 'staged' ? 'staged' : 'real'} OS error: the sandbox ran the call and it failed.`,
      }
    }
    return {
      execution: 'not_executed',
      source: 'error_class',
      sideEffectApplied: ec.side_effect_applied ?? (ec.layer === 'model' || ec.layer === 'gym' ? null : false),
      why:
        ec.origin === 'injected'
          ? 'the call was intercepted at the tool boundary; nothing reached the sandbox.'
          : `the ${layerText(ec.layer)} failed before the call could run.`,
    }
  }

  if (row) return fromLedger(row)

  const pending = live && !graded ? ' The ledger, released at evaluate, will settle it.' : graded ? ' No ledger row matched this call.' : ' The ledger was never released for this run.'

  if (ec) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'error_class',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `the outcome was unknown at the time (${ec.label}).${pending}`,
    }
  }
  if (seed.interruption) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'none',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `the worker was interrupted mid-call and no ledger row resolves it.${pending}`,
    }
  }
  if (r && !r.isError && r.exitCode !== null && r.exitCode !== 0) {
    return {
      execution: 'failed',
      source: 'exit_code',
      sideEffectApplied: false,
      why: `${exitCodeText(r.exitCode)} — from the exit code only; the record carries no error class and no ledger row matched.`,
    }
  }
  if (seed.fault || r?.isError) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'none',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `this record predates error classification (no error_class on the result) and no ledger row matched.`,
    }
  }
  return { execution: 'unknown', source: 'none', sideEffectApplied: null, why: 'nothing in the record says how this call ended.' }
}

function followUpsFor(seed: Seed, path: string | null, calls: ToolCallView[]): FollowUp[] {
  if (!seed.call || !path) return []
  const seq = seed.call.seq
  return calls
    .filter((c) => c.seq > seq && callTouches(c, path))
    .map((c) => ({
      step: c.step,
      tool: c.tool,
      toolUseId: c.toolUseId,
      kind: c.read ? 'read' : c.mutating ? 'write' : 'other',
      ok: c.result ? !c.result.isError : null,
    }))
}

function resultRaw(call: ToolCallView): unknown {
  const r = call.result
  if (!r) return { tool_use_id: call.toolUseId, tool: call.tool, result: null }
  return {
    tool_use_id: call.toolUseId,
    tool: call.tool,
    is_error: r.isError,
    exit_code: r.exitCode,
    error_code: r.errorCode,
    outcome: r.outcome,
    attempts: r.attempts,
    sandbox: r.sandbox,
    fault: call.fault,
    error_class: r.errorClass,
  }
}

interface Internal {
  __why: string
  __mutating: boolean | null
  __read: boolean | null
  __ledgerStep: number | null
}

type RichIncident = Incident & Internal

function internal(i: Incident): Internal {
  return i as unknown as Internal
}

function strip(i: RichIncident): Incident {
  const { __why, __mutating, __read, __ledgerStep, ...rest } = i
  void __why
  void __mutating
  void __read
  void __ledgerStep
  return rest
}

/** Build every `Incident` for the run, execution resolved, follow-ups attached. */
export function collectIncidents(state: ViewState, opts: { live: boolean } = { live: !isTerminal(state.status) }): Incident[] {
  return collectRich(state, opts.live).map(strip)
}

  const calls = allCalls(state)
  const ledger = state.evaluation?.ledger ?? []
  const graded = state.evaluation !== null
  const seeds = collectSeeds(state)
  const ordinals = new Map<string, number>()

    const { call, fault, interruption } = seed
    const ec = seed.errorClass

    const origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' =
      ec?.origin ?? (interruption ? 'real' : fault ? (fault.origin ?? 'injected') : 'unknown')
    const layer: ErrorLayer | null =
      ec?.layer ?? interruption?.layer ?? fault?.layer ?? (fault ? (origin === 'staged' ? 'filesystem' : 'boundary') : null)
    const kind = ec?.kind ?? fault?.kind ?? null
    const code =
      ec?.code ??
      interruption?.code ??
      call?.result?.errorCode ??

    const tool = call?.tool ?? interruption?.tool ?? null
    const path = fault?.path

      : interruption?.path

        : (call?.path ?? null)
    const step = call?.step ?? interruption?.step ?? fault?.step ?? null

    const peerKey = `${interruption ? 'int' : 'err'}:${tool ?? ''}:${path ?? ''}`
    const ordinal = fault ? 0 : (ordinals.get(peerKey) ?? 0)

80	function originTone(origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown'): PanelStatus {
81	  return origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : origin === 'unknown' ? 'unknown' : 'warn'
82	}
83	
84	function where(i: Incident): string {
85	  const parts: string[] = []
86	  if (i.step !== null) parts.push(`step ${i.step}`)
87	  if (i.tool) parts.push(i.tool)
88	  if (i.path) parts.push(i.path)
89	  return parts.join(' ')
90	}
91	
92	function suffix(i: number, n: number): string {
93	  return n > 1 ? ` #${i + 1}` : ''
94	}
95	
96	function verbFor(tool: string | null): string {
97	  switch (tool) {
98	    case 'write_file':
99	      return 'Write'
100	    case 'run_command':
101	      return 'Command'
102	    case 'read_file':
103	      return 'Read'
104	    case 'list_dir':
105	      return 'Listing'
106	    default:
107	      return 'Call'
108	  }
109	}
110	
111	function sourceText(s: ExecutionSource): string {
112	  switch (s) {
113	    case 'outcome':
114	      return 'harness-reported outcome'
115	    case 'error_class':
116	      return 'error class'
117	    case 'ledger':
118	      return 'evaluate ledger'
119	    case 'exit_code':
120	      return 'exit code'
121	    default:
122	      return 'no source'
123	  }
124	}
125	
126	function executionText(e: ExecutionOutcome): string {
127	  switch (e) {
128	    case 'executed':
129	      return 'executed'
130	    case 'failed':
131	      return 'ran and failed'
132	    case 'not_executed':
133	      return 'not executed'
134	    default:
135	      return 'unknown'
136	  }
137	}
138	
139	function executionTone(i: Incident): PanelStatus {
140	  switch (i.execution) {
141	    case 'executed':
142	      return 'ok'
143	    case 'unknown':
144	      return 'unknown'
145	    default:
146	      return i.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'warn'
147	  }
148	}
149	
150	function executionHeadline(i: Incident, live: boolean, graded: boolean): string {
151	  const verb = verbFor(i.tool)
152	  const confirm = i.executionSource === 'ledger' ? 'ledger confirms' : i.executionSource === 'outcome' ? 'harness reports' : null
153	  const withConfirm = (s: string) => (confirm ? `${s} (${confirm})` : s)
154	  const lostAck = i.kind === 'ack_lost' || (i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT' && i.origin === 'injected')
155	  switch (i.execution) {
156	    case 'executed':
157	      if (lostAck) return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, acknowledgement lost`)
158	      if (i.code === 'EHARNESS') return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, worker was interrupted`)
159	      if (i.code === 'ETRANSPORT' || (i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT' && i.origin === 'real')) return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, transport failed`)
160	      return withConfirm(`${verb} landed`)
161	    case 'not_executed': {
162	      const tail = confirm ? `, ${confirm}` : ''
163	      if (i.kind === 'denied_write' || i.code === 'EACCES') return `Nothing was written (denied at the boundary${tail})`
164	      if (i.kind === 'missing_file' && i.origin === 'injected') return `Nothing ran (answered at the boundary; file still on disk${tail})`
165	      if (i.code === 'ESANDBOX') return `Nothing ran (sandbox unavailable${tail})`
166	      if (i.code === 'EINVAL') return `Nothing ran (rejected as invalid${tail})`
167	      return `Nothing ran (${layerText(i.layer)}${tail})`
168	    }
169	    case 'failed':
170	      if (i.executionSource === 'exit_code') return `${verb} ran and failed (from exit code)`
171	      return withConfirm(`${verb} ran and failed (${i.origin === 'staged' ? 'staged OS error' : i.origin === 'real' ? 'real OS error' : 'error'})`)
172	    default: {
173	      const pending = live && !graded ? ' · ledger pending' : ''
174	      if (i.code === 'EHARNESS') return `Unknown: the worker was interrupted mid-call${pending}`
175	      if (i.code === 'ETRANSPORT') return `Unknown: transport failed mid-call${pending}`
176	      if (lostAck || i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') return `Unknown: the acknowledgement was lost${pending}`
177	      if (i.origin === 'unknown') return 'Unknown: this record predates error classification'
178	      return `Unknown: the outcome was not recorded${pending}`
179	    }
180	  }
181	}
182	
183	function countBy<T extends string>(items: Incident[], key: (i: Incident) => T): Map<T, number> {
184	  const m = new Map<T, number>()
185	  for (const i of items) m.set(key(i), (m.get(key(i)) ?? 0) + 1)
186	  return m
187	}
188	
189	function joinNonZero(parts: [number, string][]): string {
190	  return parts
191	    .filter(([n]) => n > 0)
192	    .map(([n, w]) => `${n} ${w}`)
193	    .join(', ')
194	}
195	
196	// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- panels
197	
198	function taskPanel(state: ViewState, scenario: Scenario | null, grading: Grading): VerdictPanel {
199	  const sc = scenario ?? state.scenario
200	  const headline = sc?.title ?? sc?.id ?? state.scenarioId ?? 'Unknown task'
201	  const prompt = state.taskPrompt ?? sc?.task_prompt ?? null
202	
203	  const sentences: string[] = []
204	  if (prompt) sentences.push(firstSentence(prompt))
205	
206	  const kinds = sc?.fault_kinds ?? []
207	  const injectedLabels = kinds.map((k) => taxonomyLabel('injected', k, 'boundary') ?? k)
208	  const harness = sc?.harness_faults ?? []
209	  if (sc) {
210	    const bits: string[] = []
211	    if (injectedLabels.length > 0) bits.push(`the scenario injects ${injectedLabels.join('; ')}`)
212	    if (harness.length > 0) {
213	      bits.push(
214	        `includes a real harness interruption (${harness.map((h) => `${h.kind.replace('_', ' ')} on ${h.tool} ${h.path}`).join('; ')})`,
215	      )
216	    }
217	    if (bits.length === 0) bits.push('no faults are planned for this scenario')
218	    const s = bits.join(' and ')
219	    sentences.push(`${s.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${s.slice(1)}.`)
220	  } else {
221	    sentences.push('The scenario is not on hand, so the planned faults are unknown.')
222	  }
223	
224	  const checkDescriptions =
225	    sc?.checks && sc.checks.length > 0
226	      ? sc.checks.map((c) => c.description)
227	      : grading.available
228	        ? grading.checks.map((c) => c.description)
229	        : []
230	  if (checkDescriptions.length > 0) sentences.push(`Graded on: ${checkDescriptions.join('; ')}.`)
231	
232	  const facts: Fact[] = []
233	  facts.push({ label: 'scenario', value: sc?.id ?? state.scenarioId ?? 'unknown' })
234	  if (state.model) facts.push({ label: 'model', value: state.model })
235	  facts.push({ label: 'step budget', value: String(sc?.max_steps ?? state.maxSteps) })
236	  facts.push({
237	    label: 'faults in play',
238	    value: kinds.length > 0 ? kinds.join(', ') : sc ? 'none injected' : 'unknown',
239	    tone: kinds.length > 0 ? 'warn' : undefined,
240	  })
241	  if (harness.length > 0) {
242	    facts.push({
243	      label: 'real interruption planned',
244	      value: harness.map((h) => `${h.kind} · ${h.tool} ${h.path} (call #${h.nth})`).join('; '),
245	      tone: 'fail',
246	      hint: 'A deliberate real failure the harness inflicts on itself (Scenario.harness_faults).',
247	    })
248	  }
249	  if (checkDescriptions.length > 0) facts.push({ label: 'checks', value: plural(checkDescriptions.length, 'grader check') })
250	
251	  const raw: RawRef[] = []
252	  if (sc) {
253	    raw.push({
254	      label: 'scenario (public fields)',
255	      json: {
256	        id: sc.id,
257	        title: sc.title,
258	        fault_kinds: sc.fault_kinds,
259	        harness_faults: sc.harness_faults ?? [],
260	        checks: sc.checks ?? [],
261	        faults_public: sc.faults_public ?? [],
262	        max_steps: sc.max_steps,
263	      },
264	    })
265	  }
266	  raw.push({ label: 'task_prompt (episode.reset / RunRecord)', json: prompt })
267	
268	  return {
269	    key: 'task',
270	    status: prompt ? 'ok' : 'unknown',
271	    headline,
272	    plain: sentences.join(' '),
273	    facts,
274	    raw,
275	  }
276	}
277	
278	function faultPanel(incidents: Incident[], live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
279	  const n = incidents.length
280	  const byOrigin = countBy(incidents, (i) => i.origin)
281	  const injected = byOrigin.get('injected') ?? 0
282	  const staged = byOrigin.get('staged') ?? 0
283	  const real = byOrigin.get('real') ?? 0
284	  const unknown = byOrigin.get('unknown') ?? 0
285	  const realUnplanned = incidents.filter((i) => i.origin === 'real' && i.planned !== true)
286	  const realPlanned = incidents.filter((i) => i.origin === 'real' && i.planned === true)
287	
288	  let headline: string
289	  if (n === 0) headline = 'No failures'
290	  else if (n === 1) {
291	    const i = incidents[0]!
292	    headline = [i.label ?? i.code ?? 'failure', i.step !== null ? `step ${i.step}` : null, i.path].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')
293	  } else {
294	    headline = `${n} failures: ${joinNonZero([
295	      [injected, 'simulated'],
296	      [staged, 'staged'],
297	      [real, 'real'],
298	      [unknown, 'unknown'],
299	    ])}`
300	  }
301	
302	  const status: PanelStatus = realUnplanned.length > 0 ? 'fail' : unknown > 0 ? 'unknown' : n > 0 ? 'warn' : 'ok'
303	
304	  const sentences: string[] = []
305	  if (n === 0) {
306	    sentences.push(live ? 'No failed call, injected fault or interruption so far.' : 'No failed call, injected fault or interruption was recorded.')
307	  }
308	  for (const i of realUnplanned) {
309	    sentences.push(`A real, unplanned failure hit the ${layerText(i.layer)}${i.label ? ` (${i.label})` : ''}${i.step !== null ? ` at step ${i.step}` : ''}.`)
310	  }
311	  for (const i of realPlanned) {
312	    sentences.push(`The scenario deliberately inflicted a real ${layerText(i.layer)} interruption${i.label ? ` (${i.label})` : ''}${i.step !== null ? ` at step ${i.step}` : ''}.`)
313	  }
314	  if (injected > 0) {
315	    sentences.push(
316	      `${injected === 1 ? 'One simulated fault was' : `${injected} simulated faults were`} intercepted at the tool boundary by the scenario; nothing real broke there.`,
317	    )
318	  }
319	  if (staged > 0) {
320	    sentences.push(`${staged === 1 ? 'One failure was' : `${staged} failures were`} staged at reset: a genuine OS error the scenario set up on purpose.`)
321	  }
322	  if (unknown > 0) {
323	    sentences.push(
324	      `${unknown === 1 ? 'One failure carries' : `${unknown} failures carry`} no error class — this record predates error classification, so the origin cannot be determined.`,
325	    )
326	  }
327	
328	  const facts: Fact[] = []
329	  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
330	    const sfx = suffix(idx, n)
331	    facts.push({ label: `origin${sfx}`, value: originText(i.origin), tone: originTone(i.origin), hint: originPlain(i.origin) })
332	    facts.push({ label: `layer${sfx}`, value: layerText(i.layer) })
333	    if (i.code) facts.push({ label: `code${sfx}`, value: i.code, hint: codeText(i.code)?.plain })
334	    if (i.path) facts.push({ label: `path${sfx}`, value: i.path })
335	    if (i.step !== null || i.tool) facts.push({ label: `step${sfx}`, value: where(i) })
336	    if (i.planned === true) {
337	      facts.push({ label: `planned${sfx}`, value: 'deliberate real failure', tone: 'warn', hint: "Triggered by the scenario's harness_faults; still a real failure of a real component." })
338	    }
339	    if (i.description) facts.push({ label: `environment says${sfx}`, value: i.description })
340	  })
341	
342	  return {
343	    key: 'fault',
344	    status,
345	    headline,
346	    plain: sentences.join(' '),
347	    facts,
348	    raw: incidents.flatMap((i) => i.raw),
349	  }
350	}
351	
352	function executionPanel(incidents: Incident[], live: boolean, graded: boolean): VerdictPanel {
353	  const n = incidents.length
354	  const by = countBy(incidents, (i) => i.execution)
355	  const unknownCount = by.get('unknown') ?? 0
356	  // A real component that made the call fail — or stopped it from running at all — is a failure of
357	  // execution; a simulated refusal is not (that was the experiment).
358	  const realFailed = incidents.some((i) => (i.execution === 'failed' || i.execution === 'not_executed') && i.origin === 'real')
359	
360	  let headline: string
361	  if (n === 0) headline = live ? 'Every call so far was acknowledged' : 'Every call was acknowledged'
362	  else if (n === 1) headline = executionHeadline(incidents[0]!, live, graded)
363	  else {
364	    headline = joinNonZero([
365	      [by.get('executed') ?? 0, 'executed'],
366	      [by.get('not_executed') ?? 0, 'not executed'],
367	      [by.get('failed') ?? 0, 'failed'],
368	      [unknownCount, 'unknown'],
369	    ]).replace(/, /g, ' · ')
370	  }
371	
372	  const status: PanelStatus = realFailed ? 'fail' : unknownCount > 0 ? 'unknown' : 'ok'
373	
374	  const sentences: string[] = []
375	  if (n === 0) sentences.push('No failed call to account for: nothing was refused, lost or interrupted.')
376	  for (const i of incidents) {
377	    sentences.push(`${where(i) || 'run'}: ${internal(i).__why}`)
378	  }
379	  if (unknownCount > 0 && !graded) {
380	    sentences.push(live ? 'Grading releases the ledger, which resolves unknown outcomes.' : 'Without the ledger these outcomes stay unknown.')
381	  }
382	
383	  const facts: Fact[] = []
384	  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
385	    const sfx = suffix(idx, n)
386	    facts.push({
387	      label: `${where(i) || 'run'}${sfx}`,
388	      value: `${executionText(i.execution)} — via ${sourceText(i.executionSource)}`,
389	      tone: executionTone(i),
390	      hint: internal(i).__why,
391	    })
392	    if (i.sideEffectApplied !== null) {
393	      facts.push({ label: `side effect applied${sfx}`, value: i.sideEffectApplied ? 'yes' : 'no' })
394	    }
395	    if (i.outcomeKnown !== null) {
396	      facts.push({
397	        label: `outcome known at the time${sfx}`,
398	        value: i.outcomeKnown ? 'yes' : 'no',
399	        tone: i.outcomeKnown ? undefined : 'warn',
400	        hint: i.outcomeKnown ? undefined : (codeText(i.code)?.verifyHint ?? 'Verify before retrying.'),
401	      })
402	    }
403	    if (i.attempts !== null && i.attempts !== undefined && i.attempts > 1) {
404	      facts.push({ label: `harness retries of this call${sfx}`, value: String(i.attempts - 1), tone: 'warn' })
405	    }
406	  })
407	
408	  return {
409	    key: 'execution',
410	    status,
411	    headline,
412	    plain: sentences.join(' '),
413	    facts,
414	    raw: incidents.flatMap((i) => i.raw),
415	  }
416	}
417	
418	interface ReadBack {
419	  label: string
420	  value: string
421	  tone?: PanelStatus
422	}
423	
424	/** Live hint from call order only: after a write incident, did a read precede the next write? */
425	function readBackFor(i: Incident): ReadBack | null {
426	  const int = internal(i)
427	  if (!i.path) return null
428	  const fu = i.followUps
429	  if (int.__mutating) {
430	    const firstWrite = fu.findIndex((f) => f.kind === 'write')
431	    const before = firstWrite === -1 ? fu : fu.slice(0, firstWrite)
432	    const readOk = before.some((f) => f.kind === 'read' && f.ok !== false)
433	    if (firstWrite === -1) {
434	      return readOk
435	        ? { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'yes — read back, never rewrote', tone: 'ok' }
436	        : { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'n.a. — never touched again', tone: 'unknown' }
437	    }
438	    return readOk
439	      ? { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'yes', tone: 'ok' }
440	      : { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'no — rewrote without reading back', tone: 'fail' }
441	  }
442	  if (int.__read) {
443	    const retried = fu.some((f) => f.kind === 'read' && f.ok === true)
444	    return retried
445	      ? { label: 'retried the read', value: 'yes', tone: 'ok' }
446	      : { label: 'retried the read', value: fu.length === 0 ? 'no — never touched again' : 'no successful re-read', tone: 'fail' }
447	  }
448	  return null
449	}
450	
451	function recoveryPanel(state: ViewState, incidents: Incident[], grading: Grading, sandbox: SandboxSummary, live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
452	  const n = incidents.length
453	  const recChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => isRecoveryCheck(c.id))
454	  const failed = recChecks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
455	  const maxAttempt = state.llmCalls.reduce((m, c) => Math.max(m, c.attempt), 0)
456	
457	  const facts: Fact[] = []
458	  const hints: string[] = []
459	  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
460	    const rb = readBackFor(i)
461	    if (!rb) return
462	    hints.push(rb.value.split(' — ')[0]!)
463	    facts.push({
464	      label: `${rb.label}${suffix(idx, n)} (live hint)`,
465	      value: rb.value,
466	      tone: rb.tone,
467	      hint: "Derived from call order only; the grader's ledger checks are authoritative.",
468	    })
469	    const reads = i.followUps.filter((f) => f.kind === 'read').length
470	    const writes = i.followUps.filter((f) => f.kind === 'write').length
471	    if (i.followUps.length > 0) {
472	      facts.push({ label: `follow-ups on ${i.path}${suffix(idx, n)}`, value: `${plural(reads, 'read')}, ${plural(writes, 'write')}` })
473	    }
474	  })
475	  facts.push({
476	    label: 'harness attempts',
477	    value: maxAttempt === 0 ? 'unknown (no llm.call events)' : maxAttempt === 1 ? '1 (no retries)' : `${maxAttempt} (${plural(maxAttempt - 1, 'retried request')})`,
478	    tone: maxAttempt > 1 ? 'warn' : undefined,
479	    hint: 'Highest llm.call attempt: model requests retried by the harness after a 429/5xx.',
480	  })
481	  facts.push({
482	    label: 'workers',
483	    value: `${sandbox.workerGeneration} (${plural(sandbox.resumes, 'resume')})`,
484	    tone: sandbox.resumes > 0 ? 'warn' : undefined,
485	    hint: 'worker_generation = 1 + the number of times a fresh worker resumed the run.',
486	  })
487	  facts.push({
488	    label: 'sandbox',
489	    value:
490	      sandbox.lost === true
491	        ? sandbox.lostAtStep !== null
492	          ? `lost at step ${sandbox.lostAtStep}`
493	          : 'lost'
494	        : sandbox.lost === false
495	          ? 'survived'
496	          : 'unknown',
497	    tone: sandbox.lost === true ? 'fail' : sandbox.lost === false ? 'ok' : 'unknown',
498	    hint:
499	      sandbox.lost === true
500	        ? grading.available
501	          ? 'A sandbox-layer failure was observed; grading still ran afterwards.'
502	          : 'A sandbox-layer failure was observed and the run was not graded inside it.'
503	        : sandbox.lost === false
504	          ? 'Grading ran inside the sandbox, so it was alive at the end.'
505	          : 'No grading and no sandbox-layer failure recorded: cannot say.',
506	  })
507	  for (const c of recChecks) {
508	    const t = checkText(c.id)
509	    facts.push({
510	      label: c.id,
511	      value: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'failed',
512	      tone: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail',
513	      hint: c.ok ? t.description : `${t.whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : ''}`,
514	    })
515	  }
516	
517	  let status: PanelStatus
518	  let headline: string
519	  let plain: string
520	  const hintText = hints.length > 0 ? hints.join(' / ') : 'n.a.'
521	
522	  if (grading.available && recChecks.length > 0) {
523	    if (failed.length > 0) {
524	      status = 'fail'
525	      headline = `Recovery check failed: ${failed[0]!.id}${failed.length > 1 ? ` +${failed.length - 1}` : ''}`
526	      plain = failed.map((c) => `${checkText(c.id).whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` (${c.detail})` : ''}.`).join(' ')
527	    } else {
528	      status = 'ok'
529	      headline = `Recovery verified by the grader (${recChecks.length}/${recChecks.length} checks)`
530	      plain = `The grader's ledger-based checks confirm the agent handled the failure correctly: ${recChecks.map((c) => checkText(c.id).description).join('; ')}.`
531	    }
532	  } else if (n === 0) {
533	    status = live ? 'pending' : 'ok'
534	    headline = live ? 'No failures to recover from yet' : 'Nothing to recover from'
535	    plain = live
536	      ? 'No fault, real error or interruption so far.'
537	      : 'No fault, real error or interruption was recorded, so there was nothing to recover from.'
538	  } else if (live && !grading.available) {
539	    status = 'pending'
540	    headline = `Awaiting grading · read-back so far: ${hintText}`
541	    plain = 'The recovery checks are computed from the ledger at evaluate time. Until then the facts here are hints from call order only.'
542	  } else if (grading.available) {
543	    status = 'unknown'
544	    headline = 'Grader did not score recovery for this scenario'
545	    plain = `The evaluation carries no recovery check, so recovery is unverified. Live hint from call order: read-back ${hintText}.`
546	  } else {
547	    status = 'unknown'
548	    headline = `Not graded${grading.blockedBy ? `: ${grading.blockedBy}` : ''}`
549	    plain = `The run was never evaluated, so recovery is unverified. Live hint from call order: read-back ${hintText}.`
550	  }
551	
552	  const raw: RawRef[] = []
553	  if (grading.available) raw.push({ label: 'episode.evaluated.checks (recovery)', json: recChecks })
554	  for (const i of incidents) if (i.followUps.length > 0) raw.push({ label: `follow-ups · ${i.path}`, json: i.followUps })
555	  raw.push({ label: 'llm.call attempts', json: state.llmCalls.map((c) => ({ step: c.step, attempt: c.attempt, error: c.error })) })
556	  if (state.resumes.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'run.resumed', json: state.resumes })
557	  if (state.interruptions.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'interruption', json: state.interruptions })
558	  if (state.sandboxEvents.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'episode.sandbox', json: state.sandboxEvents })
559	
560	  return { key: 'recovery', status, headline, plain, facts, raw }
561	}
562	
563	function resultPanel(state: ViewState, grading: Grading, diff: DiffSummary | null, live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
564	  const blocked = state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted' || state.status === 'error'
565	  const okChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => c.ok).length
566	  const failedChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
567	  const tests = grading.tests
568	  const testsTotal = tests ? tests.passed + tests.failed + tests.errors : 0
569	  const filesText = diff ? `${plural(diff.files, 'file')} changed` : null
570	
571	  let status: PanelStatus
572	  let headline: string
573	  let plain: string
574	
575	  if (grading.available) {
576	    const passed = state.evaluation?.passed === true
577	    status = passed && failedChecks.length === 0 ? 'ok' : 'warn'
578	    headline = [
579	      `Score ${Math.round(grading.score ?? 0)}`,
580	      tests ? `tests ${tests.passed}/${testsTotal}` : null,
581	      `checks ${okChecks}/${grading.checks.length}`,
582	      filesText,
583	    ]
584	      .filter(Boolean)
585	      .join(' · ')
586	    const bits = [
587	      `Hidden tests ${passed ? 'passed' : 'did not pass'}${tests ? ` (${tests.passed}/${testsTotal})` : ''}`,
588	      `${okChecks}/${grading.checks.length} grader checks passed${failedChecks.length > 0 ? ` — failed: ${failedChecks.map((c) => c.id).join(', ')}` : ''}`,
589	    ]
590	    plain = `${bits.join('; ')}.${diff ? ` ${plural(diff.files, 'file')} changed (+${diff.added} / −${diff.removed}).` : ''}`
591	  } else if (blocked) {
592	    status = state.errorClass?.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'unknown'
593	    headline = grading.blockedBy ? `Not graded: ${grading.blockedBy}` : `Not graded (${state.status}; no error class recorded)`
594	    const why =
595	      state.status === 'unevaluated'
596	        ? "The loop finished but evaluate() failed — not the agent's fault."
597	        : state.status === 'interrupted'
598	          ? 'A real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it.'
599	          : 'The harness could not run the episode.'
600	    plain = `Grading did not run: ${grading.blockedBy ?? 'no reason recorded'}. ${why}${diff ? ` ${plural(diff.files, 'file')} had changed.` : ''}`
601	  } else if (live) {
602	    status = 'pending'
603	    headline = `Awaiting grading${filesText ? ` · ${filesText} so far` : ''}`
604	    plain = 'Grading runs when the agent submits or the step budget ends; hidden tests are only uploaded then.'
605	  } else {
606	    status = 'unknown'
607	    headline = `Not graded${filesText ? ` · ${filesText}` : ''}`
608	    plain = `The run ended with status ${state.status} but no evaluation was recorded.`
609	  }
610	
611	  const facts: Fact[] = []
612	  if (grading.available) {
613	    facts.push({ label: 'score', value: String(Math.round(grading.score ?? 0)), tone: status })
614	    if (tests) {
615	      facts.push({
616	        label: 'tests',
617	        value: `${tests.passed} passed, ${tests.failed} failed, ${tests.errors} errors`,
618	        tone: tests.failed + tests.errors === 0 && tests.passed > 0 ? 'ok' : 'fail',
619	      })
620	    }
621	    for (const c of grading.checks) {
622	      facts.push({
623	        label: c.id,
624	        value: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'failed',
625	        tone: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail',
626	        hint: c.ok ? c.description : `${checkText(c.id).whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : ''}`,
627	      })
628	    }
629	  }
630	  if (grading.evaluationStatus) facts.push({ label: 'evaluation', value: grading.evaluationStatus })
631	  if (grading.blockedBy) facts.push({ label: 'blocked by', value: grading.blockedBy, tone: state.errorClass?.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'unknown' })
632	  if (diff) {
633	    facts.push({ label: 'files changed', value: diff.files > 0 ? diff.paths.join(', ') : 'none' })
634	    facts.push({ label: 'lines', value: `+${diff.added} / −${diff.removed}` })
635	  }
636	
637	  const raw: RawRef[] = []
638	  if (state.evaluation) {
639	    raw.push({
640	      label: 'episode.evaluated',
641	      json: {
642	        score: state.evaluation.score,
643	        passed: state.evaluation.passed,
644	        tests: state.evaluation.tests,
645	        checks: state.evaluation.checks,
646	      },
647	    })
648	  }
649	  if (state.diffs.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'workspace.diff (last)', json: state.diffs.map((d) => d.path) })
650	  if (state.errorClass) raw.push({ label: 'run-level error_class', json: state.errorClass })
651	  if (state.evaluationStatus || state.evaluationError) {
652	    raw.push({ label: 'run.finished evaluation', json: { evaluation_status: state.evaluationStatus, evaluation_error: state.evaluationError } })
653	  }
654	
655	  return { key: 'result', status, headline, plain, facts, raw }
656	}
657	
658	// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- summaries
659	
660	/** `+`/`-` lines of the last workspace.diff, excluding the `+++`/`---` file headers. */
661	export function summariseDiff(diffs: ViewState['diffs']): DiffSummary | null {
662	  if (diffs.length === 0) return null
663	  let added = 0
664	  let removed = 0
665	  for (const d of diffs) {
666	    for (const line of d.unified.split('\n')) {
667	      if (line.startsWith('+++') || line.startsWith('---')) continue
668	      if (line.startsWith('+')) added += 1
669	      else if (line.startsWith('-')) removed += 1
670	    }
671	  }
672	  return { files: diffs.length, added, removed, paths: diffs.map((d) => d.path) }
673	}
674	
675	function buildGrading(state: ViewState): Grading {
676	  const ev = state.evaluation
677	  const blocked = state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted' || state.status === 'error'
678	  const checks: GradingCheck[] = (ev?.checks ?? []).map((c) => ({
679	    id: c.id,
680	    ok: c.ok === true,
681	    weight: typeof c.weight === 'number' ? c.weight : 1,
682	    description: checkText(c.id).description,
683	    detail: c.detail ?? '',
684	  }))
685	  const tests = ev?.tests
686	  return {
687	    available: ev !== null,
688	    evaluationStatus: state.evaluationStatus,
689	    score: ev ? ev.score : null,
690	    tests: tests ? { passed: tests.passed ?? 0, failed: tests.failed ?? 0, errors: tests.errors ?? 0 } : null,
691	    checks,
692	    blockedBy: blocked ? (state.errorClass?.label ?? state.evaluationError ?? null) : null,
693	  }
694	}
695	
696	function buildSandbox(state: ViewState, incidents: Incident[]): SandboxSummary {
697	  const sandboxIncident = incidents.find((i) => i.layer === 'sandbox') ?? null
698	  const notice = state.sandboxEvents.find((e) => e.status === 'terminated' || e.status === 'replaced') ?? null
699	  const lostByRecord = state.errorClass?.layer === 'sandbox'
700	  const lost = sandboxIncident || notice || lostByRecord ? true : state.evaluation ? false : null
701	  const resumes = Math.max(state.resumes.length, state.workerGeneration - 1)
702	  return {
703	    episodeId: state.episodeId,
704	    sandboxId: state.sandboxId,
705	    alive: lost === true ? false : state.sandboxAlive,
706	    attempt: state.resetAttempt,
707	    workerGeneration: Math.max(state.workerGeneration, resumes + 1),
708	    resumes,
709	    lost,
710	    lostAtStep: sandboxIncident?.step ?? notice?.step ?? null,
711	  }
712	}
713	
714	// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- entry point
715	
716	/** Pure: the same state and scenario always give the same verdict. */
717	export function buildVerdict(state: ViewState, scenario: Scenario | null, opts: VerdictOptions = {}): Verdict {
718	  const live = opts.live ?? !isTerminal(state.status)
719	  const graded = state.evaluation !== null
720	  const rich = collectRich(state, live)
721	  const grading = buildGrading(state)
722	  const sandbox = buildSandbox(state, rich)
723	  const diff = summariseDiff(state.diffs)
724	
725	  const panels = {
726	    task: taskPanel(state, scenario, grading),
727	    fault: faultPanel(rich, live),
728	    execution: executionPanel(rich, live, graded),
729	    recovery: recoveryPanel(state, rich, grading, sandbox, live),
730	    result: resultPanel(state, grading, diff, live),
731	  }
732	
733	  return {
734	    status: state.status ?? null,
735	    live,
736	    panels,
737	    incidents: rich.map(strip),
738	    sandbox,
739	    grading,
740	    diff,
741	  }
742	}

     1	/** Small presentation helpers. No React, no side effects — safe to unit test. */
     2	
     3	export function fmtDuration(ms: number | null | undefined): string {
     4	  if (ms === null || ms === undefined || !Number.isFinite(ms)) return '—'
     5	  if (ms < 1000) return `${Math.round(ms)} ms`
     6	  const s = ms / 1000
     7	  if (s < 60) return `${s.toFixed(s < 10 ? 1 : 0)} s`
     8	  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
     9	  const rem = Math.round(s % 60)
    10	  return `${m}m ${String(rem).padStart(2, '0')}s`
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function fmtBytes(n: number | null | undefined): string {
    14	  if (n === null || n === undefined || !Number.isFinite(n)) return '—'
    15	  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`
    16	  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
    17	  return `${(n / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`
    18	}
    19	
    20	export function fmtTokens(n: number): string {
    21	  if (n < 1000) return String(n)
    22	  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`
    23	}
    24	
    25	export function fmtTime(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
    26	  if (!iso) return '—'
    27	  const t = Date.parse(iso)
    28	  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return '—'
    29	  return new Date(t).toISOString().slice(11, 23) + 'Z'
    30	}
    31	
    32	/** One-line preview of a command or path for a collapsed card header. */
    33	export function oneLine(s: string, max = 120): string {
    34	  const flat = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
    35	  return flat.length <= max ? flat : `${flat.slice(0, max - 1)}…`
    36	}

    39	  missing_file: 'missing_file',
    40	  denied_write: 'denied_write',
    41	  ack_lost: 'ack_lost',
    42	}

    44	export const FAULT_BLURB: Record<string, string> = {
    45	  missing_file: 'A read was answered with ENOENT — the file is gone, or the filesystem is flaky.',
    46	  denied_write: 'A write was refused with EACCES and nothing was written.',
    47	  ack_lost: 'The write landed, but the response timed out — the agent cannot know whether it applied.',
    48	}

    50	/** First 7 hex chars of a sha256, for a file-list column. */
    51	export function shortSha(sha: string | null | undefined): string {
    52	  return sha ? sha.slice(0, 7) : ''
    53	}
    54	
    55	/**
    56	 * The first sentence of a prompt, for a one-line summary. Breaks on `.`, `!` or `?` followed by
    57	 * whitespace (optionally after a closing quote/bracket/backtick), so "0.2.0" and "README.md:" do
    58	 * not end the sentence. Capped at `max` chars with an ellipsis.
    59	 */
    60	export function firstSentence(s: string | null | undefined, max = 240): string {
    61	  if (!s) return ''
    62	  const flat = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
    63	  const m = /[.!?][`'")\]]*(?=\s)/.exec(flat)
    64	  const sentence = m ? flat.slice(0, m.index + m[0].length) : flat
    65	  return sentence.length <= max ? sentence : `${sentence.slice(0, max - 1)}…`
    66	}
    67	
    68	/** `plural(1, 'file')` → "1 file", `plural(2, 'file')` → "2 files", `plural(2, 'retry', 'retries')`. */
    69	export function plural(n: number, word: string, words = `${word}s`): string {
    70	  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? word : words}`
    71	}

    28	function summarise(call: ToolCallView): string {
    29	  if (call.command) return call.command
    30	  if (call.path) return call.path
    31	  const keys = Object.keys(call.input)
    32	  if (keys.length === 0) return '(no input)'
    33	  return oneLine(JSON.stringify(call.input), 100)
    34	}

    36	function ResultBody({ result }: { result: ToolResultView }) {
    37	  const parts: { label: string; body: string }[] = []
    38	  if (result.errorText) parts.push({ label: result.errorCode ?? 'error', body: result.errorText })
    39	  if (result.stdout) parts.push({ label: 'stdout', body: result.stdout })
    40	  if (result.stderr) parts.push({ label: 'stderr', body: result.stderr })
    41	  if (result.content !== null) parts.push({ label: 'content', body: result.content })
    42	  if (result.entries) {
    43	    parts.push({
    44	      label: 'entries',
    45	      body: result.entries.map((e) => `${e.type === 'dir' ? 'd' : '-'} ${e.name}`).join('\n'),
    46	    })
    47	  }
    48	  if (result.bytesWritten !== null) {
    49	    parts.push({ label: 'write', body: `${result.bytesWritten} bytes written` })
    50	  }
    51	  if (parts.length === 0 && result.output) parts.push({ label: 'output', body: result.output })
    52	
    53	  return (
    54	    <div className="space-y-1.5">
    55	      {parts.map((p) => (
    56	        <div key={p.label}>
    57	          <div className="mb-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
    58	            {p.label}
    59	          </div>
    60	          <Mono>{p.body.length > 4000 ? `${p.body.slice(0, 4000)}\n…[truncated in view]` : p.body}</Mono>
    61	        </div>
    62	      ))}
    63	      {result.truncated ? (
    64	        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">Output was truncated by the environment.</p>
    65	      ) : null}
    66	    </div>
    67	  )
    68	}

    70	function CallCard({ call }: { call: ToolCallView }) {
    71	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
    72	  const Icon = TOOL_ICON[call.tool] ?? Terminal
    73	  const result = call.result
    74	  const failed = result?.isError === true
    75	  const tone = failed
    76	    ? 'border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/5'
    77	    : call.fault
    78	      ? 'border-amber-500/30'
    79	      : 'border-border'
    80	
    81	  return (
    82	    <div className={`rounded-lg border ${tone} bg-card/60`}>
    83	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2">
    84	        <Icon className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden />
    85	        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-medium">{call.tool}</span>
    86	        <code className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
    87	          {oneLine(summarise(call), 160)}
    88	        </code>
    89	        {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge kind={call.fault.kind} /> : null}
    90	        {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
    91	        {result ? (
    92	          <Badge
    93	            variant="outline"
    94	            className={`font-mono ${failed ? 'border-destructive/40 text-destructive' : 'border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300'}`}
    95	          >
    96	            {result.exitCode !== null ? `exit ${result.exitCode}` : failed ? 'error' : 'ok'}
    97	          </Badge>
    98	        ) : (
    99	          <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">
   100	            running…
   101	          </Badge>
   102	        )}
   103	        {result ? (

   105	            {fmtDuration(result.durationMs)}
   106	          </span>
   107	        ) : null}
   108	        <Button

   110	          size="sm"
   111	          className="h-6 px-1.5"
   112	          aria-expanded={open}
   113	          aria-label={open ? 'Collapse tool call' : 'Expand tool call'}
   114	          onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
   115	        >
   116	          {open ? <ChevronDown aria-hidden /> : <ChevronRight aria-hidden />}
   117	        </Button>
   118	      </div>
   119	
   120	      {open ? (
   121	        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-border/60 px-3 py-2">
   122	          <div>
   123	            <div className="mb-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
   124	              input
   125	            </div>
   126	            <Mono>{JSON.stringify(call.input, null, 2)}</Mono>
   127	          </div>
   128	          {result ? <ResultBody result={result} /> : null}
   129	        </div>
   130	      ) : result ? (
   131	        <div className="border-t border-border/60 px-3 py-2">
   132	          <ResultBody result={result} />
   133	        </div>
   134	      ) : null}
   135	    </div>
   136	  )
   137	}
   138	
   139	function StepBlock({ step, maxSteps }: { step: StepView; maxSteps: number }) {
   140	  return (
   141	    <section aria-label={`Step ${step.step}`} className="relative pl-8">
   142	      <div className="absolute top-1 left-0 flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-border bg-card font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">
   143	        {step.step}
   144	      </div>
   145	      <div className="space-y-2 pb-5">
   146	        {step.texts.map((t, i) => (
   147	          <p
   148	            key={`${step.step}-t-${i}`}
   149	            className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-foreground/90"
   150	          >
   151	            {t}
   152	          </p>
   153	        ))}
   154	        {step.calls.map((c) => (
   155	          <CallCard key={c.toolUseId} call={c} />
   156	        ))}
   157	        {step.texts.length === 0 && step.calls.length === 0 ? (
   158	          <p className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
   159	            step {step.step} of {maxSteps}
   160	          </p>
   161	        ) : null}
   162	      </div>
   163	    </section>
   164	  )
   165	}
   166	
   167	export function Timeline({ state, live }: { state: ViewState; live: boolean }) {
   168	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
   169	  const stick = useRef(true)
   170	
   171	  // Auto-scroll while running, but yield the moment the reader scrolls up.
   172	  useEffect(() => {
   173	    const el = ref.current
   174	    if (!el) return
   175	    const onScroll = () => {
   176	      stick.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 80
   177	    }
   178	    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
   179	    return () => el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
   180	  }, [])
   181	
   182	  useEffect(() => {
   183	    const el = ref.current
   184	    if (!el || !live || !stick.current) return
   185	    el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight
   186	  }, [state.eventCount, live])
   187	
   188	  if (state.steps.length === 0) {
   189	    return (
   190	      <div ref={ref} className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-6">
   191	        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   192	          {live ? 'Waiting for the first step…' : 'No steps recorded.'}
   193	        </p>
   194	      </div>
   195	    )
   196	  }
   197	
   198	  return (
   199	    <div ref={ref} className="h-full overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Run timeline">
   200	      {state.taskPrompt ? (
   201	        <div className="mb-5 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card/40 p-3">
   202	          <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
   203	            <CornerDownRight className="size-3" aria-hidden /> task
   204	          </div>
   205	          <p className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-foreground/80">
   206	            {state.taskPrompt}
   207	          </p>
   208	        </div>
   209	      ) : null}
   210	      <div className="relative">
   211	        <div className="absolute top-2 bottom-2 left-3 w-px bg-border" aria-hidden />
   212	        {state.steps.map((s) => (
   213	          <StepBlock key={s.step} step={s} maxSteps={state.maxSteps} />
   214	        ))}
   215	      </div>
   216	    </div>
   217	  )
   218	}

    11	const WIDE_QUERY = '(min-width: 1280px)'
    12	
    13	function subscribeWide(cb: () => void): () => void {
    14	  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return () => undefined
    15	  const mql = window.matchMedia(WIDE_QUERY)
    16	  mql.addEventListener('change', cb)
    17	  return () => mql.removeEventListener('change', cb)
    18	}
    19	
    20	function isWide(): boolean {
    21	  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function'
    22	    ? window.matchMedia(WIDE_QUERY).matches
    23	    : true
    24	}
    25	
    26	/** True at ≥1280 px, where the workspace is a side column rather than an overlay sheet. */
    27	export function useIsWide(): boolean {
    28	  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribeWide, isWide, () => true)
    29	}

    12	function storageOrUndefined(): LayoutStorage | undefined {
    13	  try {
    14	    const s = window.localStorage
    15	    // A blocked storage throws on access; probe once.

    23	/**
    24	 * Transcript column + workspace panel.
    25	 *
    26	 * ≥1280 px: shadcn Resizable panels — drag the handle, double-click it to reset, collapse the
    27	 * workspace to zero; the split is remembered per browser (localStorage). Below that: the
    28	 * transcript alone, with the workspace as an overlay sheet (the Sheet is portaled, so it is not
    29	 * rendered at all on wide screens).
    30	 */
    31	export function RunLayout({
    32	  children,
    33	  state,
    34	  live,
    35	  open,
    36	  onOpenChange,
    37	}: {
    38	  children: ReactNode
    39	  state: ViewState | null
    40	  live: boolean
    41	  open: boolean
    42	  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
    43	}) {
    44	  const wide = useIsWide()
    45	  const panelRef = usePanelRef()
    46	  const storage = useMemo(() => storageOrUndefined(), [])
    47	  const { defaultLayout, onLayoutChanged } = useDefaultLayout({
    48	    id: LAYOUT_ID,
    49	    panelIds: PANEL_IDS,
    50	    storage,
    51	    onlySaveAfterUserInteractions: true,
    52	  })
    53	
    54	  // Keep the imperative panel in step with the `open` state (top-bar toggle, breakpoint changes).
    55	  useEffect(() => {
    56	    if (!wide) return
    57	    const p = panelRef.current
    58	    if (!p) return
    59	    if (open && p.isCollapsed()) p.expand()
    60	    else if (!open && !p.isCollapsed()) p.collapse()
    61	  }, [open, wide, panelRef])
    62	
    63	  if (!wide) {
    64	    return (
    65	      <>
    66	        <div className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">{children}</div>
    67	        <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
    68	          <SheetContent side="right" className="flex w-[92vw] flex-col gap-0 p-0 sm:max-w-[520px]">
    69	            <SheetHeader className="border-b border-border px-4 py-3">
    70	              <SheetTitle>Workspace</SheetTitle>
    71	              <SheetDescription>Files, diffs, raw timeline and logs for this run.</SheetDescription>
    72	            </SheetHeader>
    73	            <WorkspaceTabs state={state} live={live} />
    74	          </SheetContent>
    75	        </Sheet>
    76	      </>
    77	    )
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  return (
    81	    <ResizablePanelGroup
    82	      orientation="horizontal"
    83	      defaultLayout={defaultLayout}
    84	      onLayoutChanged={onLayoutChanged}
    85	      className="h-auto min-h-0 flex-1"
    86	    >
    87	      <ResizablePanel id="transcript" defaultSize="64" minSize={420}>
    88	        <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-col">{children}</div>
    89	      </ResizablePanel>
    90	      <ResizableHandle
    91	        withHandle
    92	        aria-label="Resize the workspace panel"
    93	        className="w-1.5 bg-transparent hover:bg-border/60 data-[resize-handle-active]:bg-brand/40"
    94	      />
    95	      <ResizablePanel
    96	        id="workspace"
    97	        defaultSize="36"
    98	        minSize={300}
    99	        maxSize="65"
   100	        collapsible
   101	        collapsedSize={0}
   102	        panelRef={panelRef}
   103	        onResize={() => {
   104	          // Dragging the handle to zero collapses the panel; reflect that in the toggle state.
   105	          const collapsed = panelRef.current?.isCollapsed() ?? false
   106	          if (collapsed === open) onOpenChange(!collapsed)
   107	        }}
   108	      >
   109	        <aside className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col border-l border-border bg-card/30" aria-label="Workspace">

   111	        </aside>
   112	      </ResizablePanel>

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual'

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { subscribeLogs } from '@/lib/log'
import type { ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
import type { LogLevel, LogLine } from '@/lib/types'

const LEVELS: (LogLevel | 'all')[] = ['all', 'debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error']
const RANK: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }

const LVL_TONE: Record<LogLevel, string> = {
  debug: 'text-muted-foreground',
  info: 'text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300',
  warn: 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300',
  error: 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
}

const ROW_ESTIMATE_PX = 22

function renderExtras(line: LogLine): string {
  const skip = new Set(['ts', 'svc', 'lvl', 'ev', 'msg', '_seq'])
  const parts: string[] = []
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(line)) {
    if (skip.has(k) || v === null || v === undefined) continue
    parts.push(`${k}=${typeof v === 'string' ? v : JSON.stringify(v)}`)
  }
  return parts.join(' ')
}

/**
 * Unified log lines from the run's event stream (harness + sandbox-env) merged with the browser's
 * own log ring buffer. Rows are virtualized (@tanstack/react-virtual) so a long run's thousands of
 * lines stay cheap; each row is measured after render because lines wrap.
 */
export function LogsPanel({ state, live = false }: { state: ViewState; live?: boolean }) {
  const [level, setLevel] = useState<LogLevel | 'all'>('all')
  const [raw, setRaw] = useState(false)
  const [follow, setFollow] = useState(true)
  const [webLines, setWebLines] = useState<LogLine[]>([])
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => subscribeLogs(setWebLines), [])

  const lines = useMemo(() => {
    const merged = [...state.logs, ...webLines].sort((a, b) =>
      String(a.ts ?? '').localeCompare(String(b.ts ?? '')),
    )
    if (level === 'all') return merged
    const min = RANK[level]
    return merged.filter((l) => (RANK[(l.lvl as LogLevel) ?? 'info'] ?? 20) >= min)
  }, [state.logs, webLines, level])

  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: lines.length,
    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
    estimateSize: () => ROW_ESTIMATE_PX,
    overscan: 12,
    getItemKey: (i) => `${lines[i]?.ts ?? ''}-${i}`,

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:20:51.897Z
     1	/**
     2	 * ToolCallChips — adapted from Beautiful UI `ToolChips` (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine).
     3	 *
     4	 * One row per tool call: tool icon that swaps to a chevron on hover, a label, the command / path
     5	 * in a mono chip, then status. Expanding a row reveals the call input, every result part the
     6	 * harness returned (error + code, stdout, stderr, content, entries, bytes written, truncation),
     7	 * and — for a mutating call whose path has a `FileDiff` — the unified diff.
     8	 *
     9	 * The original's hover-portal diff preview was replaced by inline expansion so it behaves inside
    10	 * a scrolling column; the demo timers are gone (rows appear as the reducer adds them).
    11	 */
    12	
    13	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
    14	import {
    15	  Check,
    16	  ChevronDown,
    17	  FileText,
    18	  FolderTree,
    19	  LoaderCircle,
    20	  PenLine,
    21	  Send,
    22	  Terminal,
    23	  X,
    24	  type LucideIcon,
    25	} from 'lucide-react'
    26	
    27	import { FaultFiredBadge, RecoveredBadge } from '@/components/FaultBadge'
    28	import { fmtDuration, oneLine } from '@/lib/format'
    29	import { callTouches, normalisePath, type ToolCallView, type ToolResultView } from '@/lib/reducer'
    30	import type { FileDiff } from '@/lib/types'
    31	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
    32	
    33	import { CodeBlock, parseUnifiedDiff } from './CodeBlock'
    34	
    35	export interface ToolCallChipsProps {
    36	  calls: ToolCallView[]
    37	  /** Workspace diffs; a mutating call whose path matches gets its diff inline. */
    38	  diffs?: FileDiff[]
    39	  /** A call without a result renders as running (instead of "no result"). */
    40	  live?: boolean
    41	}
    42	
    43	export const TOOL_ICON: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
    44	  run_command: Terminal,
    45	  read_file: FileText,
    46	  write_file: PenLine,
    47	  list_dir: FolderTree,
    48	  submit: Send,
    49	}
    50	
    51	export const TOOL_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
    52	  run_command: 'Run',
    53	  read_file: 'Read',
    54	  write_file: 'Write',
    55	  list_dir: 'List',
    56	  submit: 'Submit',
    57	}
    58	
    59	export function toolLabel(tool: string): string {
    60	  return TOOL_LABEL[tool] ?? tool
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** One-line description of the call: the command, the path, or the raw input. */
    64	export function summariseCall(call: ToolCallView): string {
    65	  if (call.command) return call.command
    66	  if (call.path) return call.path
    67	  if (call.tool === 'submit' && typeof call.input.summary === 'string') return call.input.summary
    68	  if (Object.keys(call.input).length === 0) return '(no input)'
    69	  return JSON.stringify(call.input)
    70	}
    71	
    72	/** The workspace diff this call produced, if it is mutating and a diff exists for its path. */
    73	export function diffForCall(call: ToolCallView, diffs: readonly FileDiff[]): FileDiff | null {
    74	  if (!call.mutating) return null
    75	  return (
    76	    diffs.find((d) => (call.path ? normalisePath(d.path) === call.path : callTouches(call, d.path))) ??
    77	    null
    78	  )
    79	}
    80	
    81	export interface ResultPart {
    82	  label: string
    83	  body: string
    84	  tone: 'error' | 'plain'
    85	  /** Render as a numbered listing (file content) rather than a log block. */
    86	  code?: { filename: string | null }
    87	}
    88	
    89	/** Same parts the timeline card shows, in the same order. */
    90	export function resultParts(result: ToolResultView, path: string | null): ResultPart[] {
    91	  const parts: ResultPart[] = []
    92	  if (result.errorText) parts.push({ label: result.errorCode ?? 'error', body: result.errorText, tone: 'error' })
    93	  if (result.stdout) parts.push({ label: 'stdout', body: result.stdout, tone: 'plain' })
    94	  if (result.stderr) parts.push({ label: 'stderr', body: result.stderr, tone: 'plain' })
    95	  if (result.content !== null) {
    96	    parts.push({ label: 'content', body: result.content, tone: 'plain', code: { filename: path } })
    97	  }
    98	  if (result.entries) {
    99	    parts.push({
   100	      label: 'entries',
   101	      body: result.entries.map((e) => `${e.type === 'dir' ? 'd' : '-'} ${e.name}`).join('\n'),
   102	      tone: 'plain',
   103	    })
   104	  }
   105	  if (result.bytesWritten !== null) {
   106	    parts.push({ label: 'write', body: `${result.bytesWritten} bytes written`, tone: 'plain' })
   107	  }
   108	  if (parts.length === 0 && result.output) parts.push({ label: 'output', body: result.output, tone: 'plain' })
   109	  return parts
   110	}
   111	
   112	const CLIP = 4000
   113	function clip(s: string): string {
   114	  return s.length > CLIP ? `${s.slice(0, CLIP)}\n…[truncated in view]` : s
   115	}
   116	
   117	function Section({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
   118	  return (
   119	    <div>
   120	      <div className="mb-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ink-3 uppercase">{label}</div>
   121	      {children}
   122	    </div>
   123	  )
   124	}
   125	
   126	function Mono({ children, tone = 'plain' }: { children: ReactNode; tone?: 'error' | 'plain' }) {
   127	  return (
   128	    <pre
   129	      className={cn(
   130	        'overflow-x-auto rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap',
  const removed = counts?.filter((l) => l.kind === 'del').length ?? 0

  return (
    <div
      className="bui-fade-up"
      style={{ animationDelay: `${live ? 0 : Math.min(index, 8) * 50}ms` }}
      data-slot="tool-call"
      data-tool={call.tool}
    >
      <div className="flex min-h-7 w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={open}
          aria-controls={panelId}
          onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
          className="group/row flex h-7 min-w-0 flex-1 basis-48 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1 text-left transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/40 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
        >
          <span className="relative flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center text-ink-3">
            <Icon
              className={cn(
                'size-[13px] transition-opacity duration-100 group-hover/row:opacity-0',
                open && 'opacity-0',
              )}
              aria-hidden
            />
            <ChevronDown
              className={cn(
                'absolute size-3 transition-[opacity,transform] duration-150 group-hover/row:opacity-100',
                open ? 'rotate-0 opacity-100' : '-rotate-90 opacity-0',
              )}
              aria-hidden
            />
          </span>
          <span className="shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{toolLabel(call.tool)}</span>
          <span className="inline-flex h-5.5 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center rounded-md bg-muted/50 px-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-2 ring-1 ring-line ring-inset">
            <span className="truncate">{oneLine(summary, 160)}</span>
          </span>
        </button>

        {diff ? (
          <span
            className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums"
            aria-label={`${added} lines added, ${removed} lines removed`}
          >
            <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300">+{added}</span>{' '}
            <span className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300">−{removed}</span>
          </span>
        ) : null}
        {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge kind={call.fault.kind} /> : null}
        {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
        <StatusPill call={call} live={live} />
        {result ? (
          <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
            {fmtDuration(result.durationMs)}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div
        id={panelId}
        aria-hidden={!open}
        inert={!open}
        className={cn(
          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-300 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
          open ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
        )}
      >
        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="mt-0.5 mb-1 ml-3 flex flex-col gap-2 border-l border-line py-1 pl-3.5">
            <Section label="input">
              <InputBlock call={call} />
            </Section>
            {result ? (
              <ResultBlock call={call} />
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">{live ? 'Waiting for the result…' : 'No result recorded.'}</p>
            )}
            {diff ? (
              <Section label="diff">
                <CodeBlock diff={diff.unified} filename={diff.path} maxHeight={320} />
              </Section>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function ToolCallChips({ calls, diffs = [], live = false }: ToolCallChipsProps) {
  if (calls.length === 0) {
    return <p className="px-1 text-[12px] text-ink-3">No tool calls.</p>
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex w-full min-w-0 flex-col gap-1" data-slot="tool-call-chips">
      {calls.map((c, i) => (
        <Row key={c.toolUseId} call={c} diff={diffForCall(c, diffs)} live={live} index={i} />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}
