function StatusPill({ call, live }: { call: ToolCallView; live: boolean }) {
  const r = call.result
  const base = 'inline-flex h-5 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 font-mono text-[11px] ring-1 ring-inset'
  if (!r) {
    return live ? (
      <span className={cn(base, 'bg-muted/50 text-ink-2 ring-line')} data-status="running">
        <LoaderCircle className="bui-spin size-3" aria-hidden />
        running
      </span>
    ) : (
      <span className={cn(base, 'text-ink-3 ring-line')} data-status="pending">
        no result
      </span>
    )
  }
  const failed = r.isError || (r.exitCode !== null && r.exitCode !== 0)
  const text = r.exitCode !== null ? `exit ${r.exitCode}` : r.isError ? 'error' : 'ok'
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        base,
        failed
          ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 ring-rose-500/30'
          : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 ring-emerald-500/30',
      )}
      data-status={failed ? 'error' : 'ok'}
    >
      {failed ? <X className="size-3" aria-hidden /> : <Check className="size-3" aria-hidden />}
      {text}
    </span>
  )
}

function InputBlock({ call }: { call: ToolCallView }) {
  if (call.command) return <Mono>$ {call.command}</Mono>
  if (call.tool === 'write_file' && typeof call.input.content === 'string') {
    const { content, ...rest } = call.input
    return (
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <CodeBlock code={content} filename={call.path ?? 'content'} maxHeight={240} />
        {Object.keys(rest).length > 0 ? <Mono>{JSON.stringify(rest)}</Mono> : null}
      </div>
    )
  }
  return <Mono>{JSON.stringify(call.input, null, 2)}</Mono>
}

function ResultBlock({ call }: { call: ToolCallView }) {
  const result = call.result!
  const parts = resultParts(result, call.path)
  return (
    <>
      {parts.map((p) => (
        <Section key={p.label} label={p.label}>
          {p.code ? (
            <CodeBlock code={clip(p.body)} filename={p.code.filename ?? undefined} maxHeight={280} />
          ) : (
            <Mono tone={p.tone}>{clip(p.body)}</Mono>
          )}
        </Section>
      ))}
      {result.truncated ? (
        <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-3">Output was truncated by the environment.</p>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
}

  const counts = diff ? parseUnifiedDiff(diff.unified) : null
  const added = counts?.filter((l) => l.kind === 'add').length ?? 0
  const removed = counts?.filter((l) => l.kind === 'del').length ?? 0

export function ThinkingTrace({
  step,
  maxSteps,
  calls,
  thinking,
  active,
  done,
  defaultOpen,
  children,
}: ThinkingTraceProps) {
  const [manual, setManual] = useState<boolean | null>(null)
  const expanded = manual ?? defaultOpen ?? active
  const panelId = useId()

  const n = calls.length
  const faults = calls.filter((c) => c.fault).length
  const recovered = calls.filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
  const errors = calls.filter((c) => c.result?.isError).length
  const title = maxSteps ? `Step ${step} of ${maxSteps}` : `Step ${step}`

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col" data-slot="thinking-trace" data-step={step}>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={expanded}
        aria-controls={panelId}
        onClick={() => setManual(!expanded)}
        className="-mx-1.5 flex w-fit max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1.5 py-1 text-left transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/40 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
      >
        <Sparkles
          className={cn('size-4 shrink-0 transition-colors duration-200', active ? 'text-ink-2' : 'text-ink-3')}
          fill="currentColor"
          strokeWidth={0}
          aria-hidden
        />
        <span className="text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink">{title}</span>
        <span role="status" className="contents">
          {active ? (
            <span className="bui-shimmer text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
              {statusText(active, done, n)}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="bui-fade-in text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
              {statusText(active, done, n)}
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
        {faults > 0 ? (
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 tabular-nums">{plural(faults, 'fault')}</span>
        ) : null}
        {recovered > 0 ? (
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 tabular-nums">{recovered} recovered</span>
        ) : null}
        {errors > 0 && errors !== faults ? (
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 tabular-nums">{plural(errors, 'error')}</span>
        ) : null}
        <ChevronDown
          className={cn('size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3 transition-transform duration-300', expanded && 'rotate-180')}
          aria-hidden
        />
      </button>

      <div
        id={panelId}
        aria-hidden={!expanded}
        inert={!expanded}
        className={cn(
          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-400 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
          expanded ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
        )}
      >
        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="relative mt-1 ml-[7px] pl-4">
            <span aria-hidden className="absolute top-0 bottom-1 left-0 w-px bg-line" />
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 py-1">
              {thinking ? (
                <p className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2">
                  {thinking}
                </p>
              ) : null}

              {calls.map((call, i) => {
                const Icon = TOOL_ICON[call.tool] ?? Terminal
                return (
                  <div
                    key={call.toolUseId}
                    className="bui-fade-up flex min-h-7 w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5"
                    style={{ animationDelay: `${active ? 0 : Math.min(i, 8) * 60}ms` }}
                    data-slot="trace-row"
                  >
                    <Glyph call={call} active={active} />

                    <span className="shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{toolLabel(call.tool)}</span>

                    </span>
                    {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge kind={call.fault.kind} /> : null}
                    {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
                  </div>
                )
              })}

              {active && n === 0 ? (
                <div className="flex min-h-7 items-center gap-2 px-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-3">
                  <span className="bui-spin size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-ink-2" />
                  waiting for the model…
                </div>
              ) : null}

              {children ? <div className="mt-1 min-w-0">{children}</div> : null}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:21:11.225Z
     1	/**
     2	 * Source-independent transcript view model.
     3	 *
     4	 * The UI renders one shape whether the run is streaming (ViewState from the reducer), being
     5	 * replayed from a bundled RunRecord (also ViewState), or read back from the harness Store as
     6	 * Anthropic-shaped messages (ConversationDetail.messages). The persisted path re-uses the
     7	 * reducer's own classification, result parsing and recovery derivation, so a fault badge or a
     8	 * "recovered" tick means the same thing on every path — and the same thing the grader means.
     9	 */
    10	
    11	import {
    12	  applyRecovery,
    13	  classifyCall,
    14	  isTerminal,
    15	  parseToolResult,
    16	  type StepView,
    17	  type ToolCallView,
    18	  type ViewState,
    19	} from './reducer'
    20	import type { Block, EvaluateResponse, Message, RunStatus, RunSummary } from './types'
    21	
    22	export interface Turn {
    23	  step: number
    24	  thinking: string | null
    25	  texts: string[]
    26	  calls: ToolCallView[]
    27	}
    28	
    29	export interface Transcript {
    30	  runId: string | null
    31	  status: RunStatus
    32	  scenarioId: string | null
    33	  model: string | null
    34	  taskPrompt: string | null
    35	  turns: Turn[]
    36	  evaluation: EvaluateResponse | null
    37	  score: number | null
    38	  /** The run's error text (`run.finished.error` / `RunRecord.error`); RunSummary carries none. */
    39	  error: string | null
    40	  /** True while more turns may still arrive. */
    41	  live: boolean
    42	}

    44	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- from the reducer

    46	export function transcriptFromViewState(state: ViewState, live: boolean): Transcript {
    47	  return {
    48	    runId: state.runId,
    49	    status: state.status,
    50	    scenarioId: state.scenarioId,
    51	    model: state.model,
    52	    taskPrompt: state.taskPrompt,
    53	    turns: state.steps.map((s) => ({ step: s.step, thinking: s.thinking, texts: s.texts, calls: s.calls })),
    54	    evaluation: state.evaluation,
    55	    score: state.evaluation?.score ?? null,
    56	    error: state.error,
    57	    live,
    58	  }
    59	}
    60	
    61	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- from persisted messages
    62	
    63	function sortedBlocks(m: Message): Block[] {
    64	  return [...(m.blocks ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
    65	}
    66	
    67	function upsertTurn(turns: StepView[], step: number): StepView {
    68	  const found = turns.find((t) => t.step === step)
    69	  if (found) return found
    70	  const fresh: StepView = { step, thinking: null, texts: [], calls: [] }
    71	  turns.push(fresh)
    72	  turns.sort((a, b) => a.step - b.step)
    73	  return fresh
    74	}
    75	
    76	function highestStep(turns: StepView[]): number {
    77	  return turns.reduce((m, t) => Math.max(m, t.step), 0)
    78	}
    79	
    80	function nextSeq(turns: StepView[]): number {
    81	  let max = -1
    82	  for (const t of turns) for (const c of t.calls) if (c.seq > max) max = c.seq
    83	  return max + 1
    84	}
    85	
    86	function makeCall(turns: StepView[], step: number, toolUseId: string, tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): ToolCallView {
    87	  const cls = classifyCall(tool, input)
    88	  return {
    89	    seq: nextSeq(turns),
    90	    step,
    91	    toolUseId,
    92	    tool,
    93	    input,
    94	    path: cls.path,
    95	    command: cls.command,
    96	    mutating: cls.mutating,
    97	    read: cls.read,
    98	    result: null,
    99	    fault: null,
   100	    recovered: false,
   101	  }
   102	}
   103	
   104	function findCall(turns: StepView[], toolUseId: string): { turn: StepView; idx: number } | null {
   105	  for (const turn of turns) {
   106	    const idx = turn.calls.findIndex((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)
   107	    if (idx >= 0) return { turn, idx }
   108	  }
   109	  return null
   110	}
   111	
   112	function attachResult(turns: StepView[], msg: Message, b: Block): void {
   113	  const toolUseId = b.tool_use_id
   114	  if (!toolUseId) return
   115	  const result = parseToolResult(toolUseId, b.text ?? '', b.is_error === true, b.duration_ms ?? 0)
   116	  if (result.exitCode === null && typeof b.exit_code === 'number') result.exitCode = b.exit_code
   117	  if (b.truncated) result.truncated = true
   118	  const fault = b.fault ?? null
   119	
   120	  const hit = findCall(turns, toolUseId)
   121	  if (hit) {
   122	    const prev = hit.turn.calls[hit.idx]!
   123	    hit.turn.calls[hit.idx] = { ...prev, result, fault: fault ?? prev.fault }
   124	    return
   125	  }
   126	  // A result whose tool_use did not survive: synthesise the call so the output still renders
   127	  // (mirrors the reducer's behaviour for a resumed stream).
   128	  const step = typeof msg.step === 'number' ? msg.step : highestStep(turns)
   129	  const turn = upsertTurn(turns, step)
   130	  const synth = makeCall(turns, step, toolUseId, b.tool_name ?? 'unknown', {})
   131	  turn.calls.push({ ...synth, result, fault })
   132	}
   133	
   134	/**
   135	 * Build the transcript of one run from a conversation's persisted messages.
   136	 *
   137	 * Grouping follows ARCHITECTURE.md §4.5: the first user message's text block is the task prompt;
   138	 * each assistant message is one turn (thinking / text / tool_use blocks in seq order); the user
   139	 * message that follows carries the tool_result blocks, matched back by tool_use_id.
   140	 */
   141	export function transcriptFromMessages(messages: Message[], run: RunSummary): Transcript {
   142	  const mine = messages.filter((m) => m.run_id === run.id).sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
   143	
   144	  let taskPrompt: string | null = null
   145	  const turns: StepView[] = []
   146	
   147	  for (const m of mine) {
   148	    const blocks = sortedBlocks(m)
   149	
   150	    if (m.role === 'user') {
   151	      for (const b of blocks) {
   152	        if (b.type === 'tool_result') {
   153	          attachResult(turns, m, b)
   154	        } else if (b.type === 'text' && taskPrompt === null && typeof b.text === 'string' && b.text.length > 0) {
   155	          taskPrompt = b.text
   156	        }
   157	      }
   158	      continue
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    // Assistant. A message without a step (the submit summary) joins the current last turn, as
   162	    // the reducer does for an event without one.
   163	    const step = typeof m.step === 'number' ? m.step : highestStep(turns)
   164	    const turn = upsertTurn(turns, step)
   165	    for (const b of blocks) {
   166	      switch (b.type) {
   167	        case 'thinking': {
   168	          const text = b.text ?? ''
   169	          if (text.trim().length === 0) break
   170	          turn.thinking = turn.thinking ? `${turn.thinking}\n\n${text}` : text
   171	          break
   172	        }
   173	        case 'text': {
   174	          const text = b.text ?? ''
   175	          if (text.trim().length === 0) break
   176	          turn.texts.push(text)
   177	          break
   178	        }
   179	        case 'tool_use': {
   180	          const toolUseId = b.tool_use_id ?? b.id
   181	          if (turn.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) break
   182	          const input = b.input && typeof b.input === 'object' ? b.input : {}
   183	          turn.calls.push(makeCall(turns, step, toolUseId, b.tool_name ?? 'unknown', input))
   184	          break
   185	        }
   186	        default:
   187	          break
   188	      }
   189	    }
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  const recovered = applyRecovery(turns)
   193	
   194	  return {
   195	    runId: run.id,
   196	    status: run.status,
   197	    scenarioId: run.scenario_id,
   198	    model: run.model,
   199	    taskPrompt,
   200	    turns: recovered.map((t) => ({ step: t.step, thinking: t.thinking, texts: t.texts, calls: t.calls })),
   201	    evaluation: null,
   202	    score: run.score ?? null,
   203	    error: null,
   204	    live: !isTerminal(run.status),
   205	  }
   206	}
     1	import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
     2	import { AlertTriangle, CornerDownRight, OctagonAlert } from 'lucide-react'
     3	
     4	import { AssistantText, LoadingState, ScoreRows, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips } from '@/components/bui'

    11	function UserBubble({ text }: { text: string }) {
    12	  return (
    13	    <div className="flex justify-end" aria-label="Task prompt">
    14	      <div className="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl rounded-br-md border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
    15	        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ink-3 uppercase">
    16	          <CornerDownRight className="size-3" aria-hidden /> task
    17	        </div>
    18	        <p className="text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink">{text}</p>
    19	      </div>
    20	    </div>

    23	
    24	function Callout({
    25	  tone,
    26	  title,
    27	  body,
    28	}: {
    29	  tone: 'error' | 'warn'
    30	  title: string
    31	  body?: string | null
    32	}) {
    33	  const cls =
    34	    tone === 'error'
    35	      ? 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200'
    36	      : 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200'
    37	  const Icon = tone === 'error' ? OctagonAlert : AlertTriangle
    38	  return (
    39	    <div role="status" className={`flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] ${cls}`}>
    40	      <Icon className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
    41	      <div className="min-w-0">
    42	        <div className="font-medium">{title}</div>
    43	        {body ? <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-words opacity-80">{body}</div> : null}
    44	      </div>
    45	    </div>
    46	  )
    47	}
    48	
    49	function TurnBlock({
    50	  turn,
    51	  isLast,
    52	  live,
    53	  maxSteps,
    54	  diffs,
    55	}: {
    56	  turn: Turn
    57	  isLast: boolean
    58	  live: boolean
    59	  maxSteps: number
    60	  diffs: FileDiff[]
    61	}) {
    62	  const pending = turn.calls.some((c) => c.result === null)
    63	  const active = live && isLast && pending
    64	  const done = !live || !isLast || (!pending && turn.calls.length > 0)
    65	  return (
    66	    <section aria-label={`Step ${turn.step}`} className="space-y-3">
    67	      {turn.texts.map((t, i) => (
    68	        <AssistantText
    69	          key={`${turn.step}-t-${i}`}
    70	          text={t}
    71	          streaming={live && isLast && i === turn.texts.length - 1 && turn.calls.length === 0}
    72	        />
    73	      ))}
    74	      {turn.calls.length > 0 || turn.thinking ? (
    75	        <ThinkingTrace
    76	          step={turn.step}
    77	          maxSteps={maxSteps}
    78	          calls={turn.calls}
    79	          thinking={turn.thinking}
    80	          active={active}
    81	          done={done}
    82	          defaultOpen={isLast}
    83	        >
    84	          <ToolCallChips calls={turn.calls} diffs={diffs} live={live && isLast} />
    85	        </ThinkingTrace>
    86	      ) : null}
    87	    </section>
    88	  )
    89	}
    90	
    91	export function TranscriptView({
    92	  transcript,
    93	  diffs,
    94	  maxSteps,
    95	  loading,
    96	  loadingLabel,
    97	  error,
    98	  transport,
    99	  verdict,
   100	  footer,
   101	}: {
   102	  transcript: Transcript
   103	  diffs: FileDiff[]
   104	  maxSteps: number
   105	  loading?: boolean
   106	  loadingLabel?: string
   107	  error?: string | null
   108	  transport?: TransportState | null
   109	  /** The ten-second reliability read (src/lib/reliability.ts), shown above the task bubble. */
   110	  verdict?: Verdict | null
   111	  /** Rendered below the transcript inside the same scroll container (run switcher, composer spacer). */
   112	  footer?: React.ReactNode
   113	}) {
   114	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
   115	  const stick = useRef(true)
   116	  const live = transcript.live
   117	
   118	  useEffect(() => {
   119	    const el = ref.current
   120	    if (!el) return
   121	    const onScroll = () => {
   122	      stick.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 96
   123	    }
   124	    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
   125	    return () => el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
   126	  }, [])
   127	
   128	  const turnCount = transcript.turns.length
   129	  const callCount = transcript.turns.reduce((n, t) => n + t.calls.length, 0)
   130	  useEffect(() => {
   131	    const el = ref.current
   132	    if (!el || !live || !stick.current) return
   133	    el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight
   134	  }, [turnCount, callCount, live, transcript.evaluation])
   135	
   136	  const last = transcript.turns[transcript.turns.length - 1]
   137	  const waitingOnModel =
   138	    live && (!last || (last.calls.length > 0 && last.calls.every((c) => c.result !== null)))
   139	
   140	  return (
   141	    <div ref={ref} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Transcript">
   142	      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-6">
   143	        {loading ? (
   144	          <LoadingState label={loadingLabel ?? 'Loading…'} />
   145	        ) : null}
   146	
   147	        {error && turnCount === 0 && !loading ? (
   148	          <Callout tone="warn" title="Could not load this run" body={error} />
   149	        ) : null}
   150	
   151	        {verdict && !(loading && turnCount === 0) ? (
   152	          <VerdictStrip verdict={verdict} taskPrompt={transcript.taskPrompt} />
   153	        ) : null}
   154	
   155	        {transcript.taskPrompt ? <UserBubble text={transcript.taskPrompt} /> : null}
   156	
   157	        {transcript.turns.map((t, i) => (
   158	          <TurnBlock
   159	            key={t.step}
   160	            turn={t}
   161	            isLast={i === transcript.turns.length - 1}
   162	            live={live}
   163	            maxSteps={maxSteps}
   164	            diffs={diffs}
   165	          />
   166	        ))}
   167	
   168	        {live && turnCount === 0 && !loading && !error ? (
   169	          <LoadingState
   170	            label={transcript.status === 'queued' ? 'Queued — provisioning the sandbox…' : 'Provisioning the sandbox…'}
   171	            sublabel="creating a Modal Sandbox, copying the fixture repo, applying the fault plan"
   172	          />
   173	        ) : null}
   174	        {waitingOnModel && turnCount > 0 ? (
   175	          <LoadingState label="Waiting on the model…" sublabel={transcript.model ?? undefined} />
   176	        ) : null}
   177	
   178	        {transcript.evaluation ? <ScoreRows evaluation={transcript.evaluation} /> : null}
   179	
   180	        {transcript.status === 'error' ? (
   181	          <Callout tone="error" title="The run ended with an error" body={transcript.error ?? null} />
   182	        ) : transcript.error && !transcript.live ? (
   183	          <Callout
   184	            tone="warn"
   185	            title={transcript.evaluation ? 'The run reported an error' : 'The run finished but could not be graded'}
   186	            body={transcript.error}
   187	          />
   188	        ) : null}
   189	        {transcript.status === 'truncated' ? (
   190	          <Callout
   191	            tone="warn"
   192	            title={`Step budget exhausted (${maxSteps} steps)`}
   193	            body="The agent did not submit in time; whatever it left on disk was still graded."
   194	          />
   195	        ) : null}
   196	        {transport?.mode === 'poll' ? (
   197	          <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
   198	            live stream unavailable — polling the run record every 1.5 s
   199	          </p>
   200	        ) : null}
   201	        {footer}
   202	      </div>
   203	    </div>
   204	  )
   205	}

/**
 * VerdictStrip — the ten-second reliability read at the top of a run.
 *
 * Renders a `Verdict` (src/lib/reliability.types.ts) and nothing else: five panels — task, fault,
 * execution, recovery, result — each a status glyph, one headline, one plain sentence and a few
 * fact chips. Every panel has a "details" expander that lists the structured evidence behind it
 * (incidents with their taxonomy label, the execution outcome and where that answer came from,
 * follow-up calls, grader checks) and a "raw" block with the payloads the model was built from.
 *
 * Nothing here derives an origin or an outcome from message text. A panel whose status is
 * `unknown` says so; the reason is the model's `plain` sentence. Codes are explained by the
 * dictionary in src/lib/codes.ts, never by parsing an error string.
 *
 * The expanders use `aria-expanded` / `aria-controls` on a plain button (the ThinkingTrace /
 * ScoreRows pattern) rather than the Collapsible primitive: the open details render full-width
 * under the five-column row, because a 150 px column cannot hold an incident table.
 */

import { Fragment, useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Box, Check, ChevronDown, CircleQuestionMark, TriangleAlert, X } from 'lucide-react'

import { CodeBlock, toolLabel } from '@/components/bui'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { codeText, layerText, originText } from '@/lib/codes'

  ExecutionOutcome,
  ExecutionSource,

  FollowUp,

  PanelStatus,
  RawRef,
  SandboxSummary,
  Verdict,
  VerdictPanel,
} from '@/lib/reliability.types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

function safeJson(value: unknown): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) ?? 'undefined'
  } catch {
    return String(value)
  }
}

function shortId(id: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!id) return '—'
  return id.length > 14 ? `${id.slice(0, 12)}…` : id
}

/** "step 4 · Write · CHANGELOG.md" — where an incident happened, from its structured fields only. */
function where(incident: Incident): string {
  const parts: string[] = []
  if (incident.step !== null) parts.push(`step ${incident.step}`)
  if (incident.tool) parts.push(toolLabel(incident.tool))

# Bundled replays

Each `*.json` in this directory is a **`RunRecord`** — exactly the shape
`GET /runs/{id}` returns (`faultline_common.schemas.RunRecord`, mirrored in
`apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`). The UI folds it through the same reducer a live
SSE run goes through, so a replay renders identically to the real thing.

Why they exist: the demo must survive a cold harness, a missing provider key, or
a rate limit at review time. `Replay demo` on the home page needs no backend and
no network beyond fetching this file.

## Playback

`src/lib/replay.ts` emits the record's `events` one at a time, `400 ms` apart
(`DEMO_STEP_MS`). Register a new file by adding it to `DEMOS` in that module:

```ts
export const DEMOS: DemoMeta[] = [
  { id: 'lost-ack', label: 'lost-ack', file: '/demo/lost-ack.json' },
]
```

The `id` is also the `/replay/<id>` route, so `/replay/lost-ack` deep-links a replay.

## Producing one from a real run

`scripts/export_demo.py` (repo root) reads a run captured under
`runs/<ts>_<run_id>/run.json` and writes a demo file here:

```bash
# after scripts/run_episode_cli.py has written runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc/run.json
python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc
# -> apps/web/public/demo/lost-ack.json   (named after the run's scenario_id)

python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc --out-name gauntlet --pretty
python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc --check   # validate only
```

It validates the record (ids contiguous from 0, `run_id` consistent, required
fields present), strips nothing but obvious secrets (`anthropic_workspace` is
masked), and refuses to write a record with no `episode.evaluated` event unless
`--allow-unevaluated` is passed — an unfinished run makes a poor demo.

## What is shipped today

- **`lost-ack.json`** — a real capture: `claude-haiku-4-5` on the deployed stack
  (run `r_e9bc5c8c6739`, 2026-09-12, 48 events, score 100/100), exported with
  `scripts/export_demo.py` from `runs/20260912T215923Z_lost-ack_live/run.json`.
  The story: read `CHANGELOG.md` → write it → the ack is lost (`504`, `ack_lost`
  fault at step 4) → the agent **reads back instead of retrying** → the section is
  already there, so no duplicate write → bump `version.py` → `pytest` green →
  `submit` → graded 100/100.

  `src/lib/demo.test.ts` folds this file through the reducer on every `pnpm test`
  run and asserts that story still holds, so the replay cannot rot silently.
  Re-export whenever a fresher run is worth shipping.

## 5. HTTP contracts

### 5.1 Harness API (`faultline-harness/api`, CORS `*`, all routes below `/scenarios` and `/health` require `X-Faultline-User`)

| Route | Body → Response |
|---|---|
| `GET /health` | `Health{svc:"harness", ok, model_default, sandbox_env_url, has_provider_key:false, detail:{store:{ok,last_checkpoint_at}}}` |
| `GET /scenarios` | proxied from sandbox-env (public fields only) |
| `GET /me` | upsert → `{user_id, conversations: n}` |
| `GET /conversations` | `[{id, title, scenario_id, created_at, updated_at, last_run:{id,status,score}|null}]` newest first, archived excluded |
| `POST /conversations` | `{scenario_id, title?}` → `Conversation` |
| `GET /conversations/{id}` | `{conversation, runs:[RunSummary], messages:[Message{blocks:[Block]}]}` |
| `PATCH /conversations/{id}` | `{title?, archived?}` → `Conversation` |
| `DELETE /conversations/{id}` | soft archive → `{archived:true}` |
| `POST /conversations/{id}/runs` | `{model?, seed?, max_steps?, task_prompt?}` → `{run_id}` (202) |
| `POST /runs` | `{scenario_id, model?, seed?, max_steps?}` → creates a conversation then a run → `{run_id, conversation_id}` |
| `GET /runs/{id}` | `RunRecord` (record + all events + evaluation + usage) |
| `GET /runs/{id}/events` | SSE; `id:` = `seq`; honours `Last-Event-ID`; server closes at ~110 s |

Ownership: every `/conversations/*` and `/runs/*` route resolves the resource, compares `user_id` with
the header, and returns 404 on mismatch (not 403 — no existence oracle).

Wire shapes (JSON; the pydantic models in `faultline_common.schemas` and `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`
mirror these field for field; DB columns `input_json` / `fault_json` / `evaluation_json` are parsed
into `input` / `fault` / `evaluation` on the wire):

```ts
type User                = { id: string; created_at: string; last_seen_at: string }
type Conversation        = { id: string; user_id: string; scenario_id: string; title: string;
                             created_at: string; updated_at: string; archived_at: string | null }
type ConversationSummary = { id: string; title: string; scenario_id: string; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
                             last_run: { run_id: string; status: RunStatus; score: number | null } | null }
type Block               = { id: string; seq: number; type: 'text' | 'thinking' | 'tool_use' | 'tool_result';
                             text?: string | null; tool_name?: string | null; tool_use_id?: string | null;
                             input?: Record<string, unknown> | null; is_error?: boolean | null; exit_code?: number | null;
                             duration_ms?: number | null; fault?: FaultFired | null; truncated: boolean }
type Message             = { id: string; conversation_id: string; run_id: string; seq: number;
                             role: 'user' | 'assistant'; step?: number | null; created_at: string; blocks: Block[] }
type LlmCall             = { id: string; run_id: string; step: number; attempt: number; model: string;
                             request_id?: string | null; stop_reason?: string | null;
                             input_tokens?: number | null; output_tokens?: number | null;
                             cache_read_tokens?: number | null; cache_write_tokens?: number | null;
                             started_at: string; duration_ms?: number | null; error?: string | null }
type RunSummary          = { run_id: string; status: RunStatus; scenario_id: string; model: string;
                             created_at: string; finished_at: string | null; events: number; score: number | null }
// RunRecord gains three optional fields: conversation_id?, user_id?, task_prompt?
// Event.data for the two new types:
//   'llm.call'      { attempt, model, stop_reason, usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens,
//                     cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens? }, request_id?, duration_ms }
//   'turn.thinking' { text }
// Responses:
//   GET  /me                        → { user_id: string; conversations: number }
//   GET  /conversations             → ConversationSummary[]
//   POST /conversations             → Conversation
//   GET  /conversations/{id}        → { conversation: Conversation; runs: RunSummary[]; messages: Message[] }
//   PATCH /conversations/{id}       → Conversation          DELETE → { archived: true }
//   POST /conversations/{id}/runs   → { run_id: string; conversation_id: string }
//   POST /runs                      → { run_id: string; conversation_id?: string }   (conversation_id once Store lands)
```

Until the Store lands, the web app sends `X-Faultline-User` anyway and treats 404 on `/me` and
`/conversations` as "history unavailable" (rail hidden, runs still work through `POST /runs`).

### 5.2 Sandbox-env (gym + MCP)

Unchanged from `PLAN.md` §2.2: REST `POST /episodes` (reset), `GET /episodes/{id}` (observe),
`POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate`, `DELETE /episodes/{id}`, `GET /scenarios`, `GET /health`; MCP tools
`run_command`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `list_dir` at `/mcp`, episode selected by the
`X-Faultline-Episode` header so the model can never name another episode.

### 5.3 Event stream

Event types and payloads are defined in `faultline_common.schemas.Event` (`run.started`, `episode.reset`,
`turn.thinking`, `turn.text`, `tool.call`, `tool.result`, `llm.call`, `fault.fired`, `workspace.diff`,
`episode.evaluated`, `run.finished`, `log`). `Event.id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and doubles
as the SSE id, the `events.seq` primary key, and the resume cursor. Events are produced by exactly one
writer per run (`run_episode`) and are never edited.

### 5.4 Reliability event contract (confirmed with the harness lead 2026-09-12 ~22:45 EDT)

Canonical: `packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py` (the `Event` docstring lists every data
shape), labels in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`, injector behaviour in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
The UI is a ten-second reliability read — *task → fault (simulated or real, which layer) → did the
command execute (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are four different answers) → recovery
(agent actions vs harness retries; did the sandbox survive) → diff + independent checks* — and it
**never derives an outcome from error text**: anything absent renders as "unknown" with the raw
payload expandable. Fields (all additive):

| Where | Field | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `tool.result.data` | `outcome: executed \| failed \| not_executed \| unknown` | executed = ran and succeeded; failed = ran and reported failure; not_executed = refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it; unknown = may or may not have run (ack lost, transport, worker interrupted) |
| `tool.result.data` | `error_class` (iff `is_error`): `{origin: injected\|staged\|real, layer: boundary\|filesystem\|sandbox\|transport\|harness\|model\|gym, code, kind?, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied?, detail?}` | provenance and layer; `label` is a fixed string rendered verbatim; `error_code` kept for compatibility; raw text stays in `output` |
| `tool.result.data` | `attempts`, `sandbox: {id, alive}` | harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); identity/liveness of the workspace that answered |
| `fault.fired.data` | `origin` (injected \| staged, never real), `layer` (boundary for injected, filesystem for staged), `description` | one plain sentence per fault |
| `episode.reset.data` | `sandbox_id` (short), `attempt` | workspace identity; >1 when re-provisioned |
| `episode.sandbox` (new) | `{sandbox_id, status: alive\|terminated\|replaced, reason, step}` | the environment noticed the worker changed |
| `interruption` (new) | `Interruption {step?, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail?}` | always real; `planned: true` when a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it |
| `run.resumed` (new) | `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` | a fresh worker picked the run up |
| `run.finished.data` | `evaluation_status: ok\|failed\|skipped`, `evaluation_error?`, `steps`, `score?` | a run that could not be graded is `unevaluated`, never "ok" |
| `RunStatus` | `+ unevaluated`, `+ interrupted` | "ok" now implies a score |
| `RunRecord` | `error_class?`, `interruptions[]`, `worker_generation` | why the run ended as it did |
| `GET /scenarios` | `checks: [{id, description, weight}]`, `faults_public: [{kind, origin, layer, description}]`, `harness_faults: [{kind: worker_crash\|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}]` | intended outcome and fault classes known before the run; the agent never sees them |
| codes (final) | `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` | `ETIMEDOUT` is ack-lost *or* a real client timeout — origin/layer tell them apart |

Older records (e.g. `r_e9bc5c8c6739`) predate these fields; the UI resolves what it can from the
evaluate ledger (`outcome: ack_lost` ⇒ the write landed, `short_circuit` ⇒ nothing ran) and says
"unknown" otherwise. Web-side model: `apps/web/src/lib/reliability.ts` (`buildVerdict`), rendered by
`src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx`.

## 6. Identity

- **Minting.** `src/lib/identity.ts`: read `localStorage["faultline.user_id"]`, else the `faultline_uid`
  cookie, else mint `"u_" + crypto.randomUUID()`; write back to both. Cookie attributes:
  `SameSite=Lax; Secure; Max-Age=31536000; Path=/`. Not `HttpOnly` (JS writes it). All storage access is
  wrapped in try/catch; a browser that blocks storage still works for the session with an in-memory id.
- **Transport.** The harness API lives on a different origin from the SPA, so cookies are never sent to
  it. The id travels as `X-Faultline-User` on every request; the harness CORS config allows that header.
- **Why both stores.** localStorage is the primary (no size/attribute quirks, not sent anywhere);
  the cookie only exists so the id survives a "clear site data → local storage" that leaves cookies.
- **Threat model.** This is scoping, not authentication (explicit non-goal). Ids are 122-bit random and
  never listed, so they are unguessable in practice, but anyone holding one can read that browser's
  transcripts. Nothing sensitive is stored: scenarios are bundled, the workspace is a fixture, and the
  provider key never leaves `run_episode`. `POST …/runs` is rate-limited per id.

## 7. Web application

### 7.1 Stack

Vite 8 + React 19 + TypeScript 6, Tailwind v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`), shadcn v4 CLI (style `base-nova`
on Base UI, `neutral` base colour, CSS variables, the `cn` helper package, lucide icons) for primitives,
Beautiful UI for the AI-native composites, vitest (jsdom). No client router: the URL carries
`?c=<conversation_id>`, `?run=<run_id>` or `?demo=<scenario_id>`. **Node ≥ 22.12 is required** (Vite 8
`^20.19 || >=22.12`, vitest 5 `^22.12 || ^24`; `.nvmrc` pins 22 and the Modal image installs Node 22).
Built into `dist/` inside the Modal image and served by a Modal Server (`python -m http.server`, or
`@modal.web_server` as the fallback), with `VITE_HARNESS_URL` baked in and `/config.json` as a runtime
override (precedence: injected `window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__` → `/config.json` → build env → default) so
the harness URL can change without a rebuild.

### 7.2 Beautiful UI: what it is and how it is integrated

`https://www.beautifului.dev` is an MIT-licensed gallery (© 2026 Shane Levine) of 21 React + Tailwind
components "for AI-native interfaces". It is **not** an npm package and **not** a shadcn registry: each
component has a *Copy code* button, and the TSX source is embedded in the page. Integration is therefore
copy-paste into `src/components/bui/` with a LICENSE file, plus three adaptations that the source makes
necessary:

| Adaptation | Reason |
|---|---|
| icons → `lucide-react` | `SidebarNav` imports twelve icons from `@central-icons-react/*`, a paid set |
| unpublished atoms → shadcn | components import `@/components/atoms/{Button,StreamText,Shimmer,ValuePill,EntityChip}` and `@/components/primitives/GlideMenu`, which the gallery does not publish; shadcn `Button`, `Skeleton`, `Badge`, `DropdownMenu` stand in |
| drop `glimm` | `PromptBar` imports a shader/sound helper; the branch is deleted |
| `accent*` classes → `brand*` | shadcn already owns `accent` / `accent-foreground` (its subtle hover surface); Beautiful UI uses `accent` as the brand colour. Renaming on copy keeps both palettes intact |

Components used and their role: `SidebarNav` (conversation rail), `PromptBar` (composer with scenario
`/` command and model picker), `StreamingText` (assistant prose), `ThinkingState` (per-step trace),
`ToolChips` (tool calls with output/exit code/duration/fault badge), `CodeBlock` (outputs and unified
diffs), `TaskRows` (score card), `LoadingState`, `DiffTable` (workspace file status). No animation library
is required; the components use CSS transitions and `useLayoutEffect`.

Theme bridge: Beautiful UI is written against its own Tailwind tokens (`ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-3`, `canvas`,
`surface`, `line`, `line-strong`, and `accent`, `accent-tint`, `accent-ink` → renamed `brand*`). They are
defined once in the existing `@theme inline` block of `src/index.css` as aliases of shadcn's CSS
variables (`--foreground`, `--muted-foreground`, `--background`, `--card`, `--border`, `--input`,
`--primary`, `--primary-foreground`), so both libraries follow the same light/dark palette (exact block
in `PLAN.md` §2.10.2).

### 7.3 Layout, routes, state

```
┌──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┐
│ SidebarNav   │ header: title · scenario · model · status │ Workspace (Tabs)     │
│  + New run   │           step n/max · tokens · elapsed   │  Files (DiffTable)   │
│  search      │ ─────────────────────────────────────────│  Diffs (CodeBlock)   │
│  today       │ [user] task prompt                        │  Ledger              │
│   · lost-ack │ [assistant] StreamingText                 │                      │
│   · locked…  │    ThinkingState ▸ ToolChips (CodeBlock)  │                      │
│  yesterday   │      fault ⚡ · recovered ✓               │                      │
│   · …        │ [assistant] summary + TaskRows score card │                      │
│              │ ─────────────────────────────────────────│                      │
│ u_1a2b… ·    │ PromptBar  /scenario  model ▾  seed  Run  │                      │
│ Replay demo  │                                           │                      │
└──────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────┘
```

- Landing (`/`): five bullets explaining the app, a centered composer (`/` picks a scenario, model and
  seed chips), and below it a wide shadcn data table of scenarios (TanStack Table v9, fixed layout)
  with separate columns for title, description, max turns, failure badges, Live run and Replay;
  every injected-fault scenario ships a real recorded replay; harness reachability is a composer hint
  and a line in the identity menu, not a card.
- URL state (no router, as scaffolded): no params = the landing page;
  `?c=<conversation_id>` = a persisted transcript; `?c=…&run=<run_id>` = that run inside its
  conversation (live tail if unfinished); `?demo=<scenario_id>` = bundled replay.
- One pure reducer (`src/lib/reducer.ts`, already present) builds the view model for live SSE, for
  `GET /runs/{id}` restore, and for replay of `public/demo/*.json`. Persisted transcripts from
  `GET /conversations/{id}` are mapped to the same view model, so every path renders through the same
  components.
- `src/lib/api.ts` (already present) reconnects SSE with `Last-Event-ID` and, after two failed opens,
  falls back to polling `GET /runs/{id}`; identity is added to it as a default header.
- Theme: light / dark / system, chosen from a radio group of icon buttons in the user menu (bottom-left
  sidebar footer) and stored
  per browser (`faultline.theme`); "system" follows `prefers-color-scheme` live; a one-off
  `?theme=light|dark|system` in the URL sets and persists the choice (for sharing a link in a given
  look, and for screenshot tooling). The choice is applied
  by toggling the `dark` class on `<html>` (shadcn's dark variant) both from an inline pre-paint script
  in `index.html` and from `src/lib/theme.ts`, so the first paint is already right. Semantic colour
  tones are written as light/dark pairs so status badges keep contrast on both backgrounds.
- Workspace panel: shadcn Resizable panels (`react-resizable-panels` 4.x) on ≥1280 px — draggable
  handle, collapsible to zero, split persisted in localStorage, the top-bar toggle bound to the
  panel's imperative handle; an overlay sheet below that width. The Logs tab virtualizes its rows
  (`@tanstack/react-virtual`, rows measured after render because lines wrap) and follows the tail
  while a run is live.
- Verdict strip (`src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx`, model in `src/lib/reliability.ts`): the
  five-panel ten-second read described in §5.4, rendered above every transcript (live, persisted,
  replay); details and raw JSON expand per panel; the sandbox chip in the top bar shows workspace
  identity, worker generation and survival.
- Unified logging mirrors the server's JSON shape to the console with `svc:"web"`.

## 8. Deployment and configuration

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Workspace / environment | `appliedlabsai` / `local` → web functions at `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run`; the static site is a Modal **Server**, whose URL is `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` (read it back with `modal.Server.from_name("faultline-web", "site").get_url()`; the `.modal.run` shape answers 404 for Servers) |
| Deploy order | sandbox-env → harness (creates Volume `faultline-db` on first deploy) → web. Live URLs (2026-09-12): harness `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run`, sandbox-env `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run`, web `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` |
| Secret | `anthropic-secret` (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`; name overridable via `FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET`), attached only to `run_episode` |
| Volume | `faultline-db` (v1), mounted at `/data` on `Store` only |
| Env | `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5`), `SANDBOX_ENV_URL`, `HARNESS_URL`, `LOG_LEVEL` (see `.env.example`) |
| Warm containers | web Server, harness `api`, harness `Store` (`min_containers=1`) |
| Web image | Node 22 + pnpm; `pnpm i --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build`; `config.json` written from `HARNESS_URL` at container start |
| Sandbox guardrails | `timeout=1800`, `idle_timeout=600`, `block_network=True`, delete in `finally`, `reap` helper |
| Export | `modal run … ::checkpoint_now` then `modal volume get faultline-db faultline.sqlite3 runs/` |

## 9. Decision log

| # | Decision | Alternatives considered | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Three Modal apps with a strict secret boundary | one app | harness swappable, environment gradeable without trusting the agent, static UI; the provider key exists in exactly one function |
| 2 | Faults injected at the tool boundary, not inside the shell | `chmod`, deleting files live, network faults | deterministic, seedable, invisible to the agent, gradeable from a ledger; ack-lost is only expressible at the boundary |
| 3 | `spawn` + event log + reconnecting SSE | run the loop inside the request | Modal web requests cap at 150 s |
| 4 | Persistence = SQLite on a Modal Volume | Modal Dict only; hosted Postgres; Modal Dict as hot path + SQLite archive | user asked for it; zero external services; a single file is trivial to export as evidence |
| 5 | Single-writer `Store` actor (`max_containers=1`) | every function opens the DB on the Volume; per-run DB files | Volumes have no distributed locking and last-write-wins; one process owning the file removes every consistency question |
| 6 | Hot DB on local disk, `VACUUM INTO` snapshots renamed atomically onto the Volume | run SQLite directly on the FUSE mount | WAL/locking on FUSE is undocumented, and background commits are not multi-file snapshots; Modal's own example copies-then-commits |
| 7 | Volume v1 | v2 | v2 needs an explicit flag and is marked experimental in the 1.5.5 CLI; no benefit for one file |
| 8 | `events` as source of truth + `messages`/`blocks`/`llm_calls` projections | events only; projections only | replay/SSE need the log; the UI and "what did the model see" need the message shape; projections are a cheap same-transaction write and a listed scope cut |
| 9 | Identity via `X-Faultline-User` header, minted in the browser, mirrored to a cookie | cookie-only; server-minted id | the API is cross-origin so cookies never reach it; localStorage is the reliable store; the cookie is a backup |
| 10 | Beautiful UI by copy-paste with lucide/shadcn substitutions | Vercel AI Elements (a real registry); shadcn only | matches the requested look; MIT; the private deps are small and swappable; scope cut #4 if it drags |
| 11 | Haiku 4.5 default, Sonnet/Opus 5 allowlisted | Sonnet default | cost and latency for a demo; the ack-lost scenario is more interesting when a cheaper model sometimes fails it |
| 12 | No client router; conversation/run/demo in query params | `react-router-dom` | the scaffold already does this and it is enough for three screens; one less dependency in a static SPA |
| 13 | Node 22 pinned (`.nvmrc`, `engines`, Modal image) | stay on the machine default 21.6 | Vite 8 and vitest 5 refuse to start on 21.6 (`styleText` missing from `node:util`) |

    11	checkout:
    12	
    13	  * **prebuilt** (default when `apps/web/dist/index.html` exists, or `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT=1`):
    14	    `pnpm build` already ran locally, so the image just copies `dist/` to `/site`. No node in the
    15	    image, build takes seconds.
    16	  * **in-image**: no local `dist/`, so the image installs node 22 + pnpm and runs
    17	    `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build` itself. Slower and it re-builds the image on
    18	    every source change, but it needs nothing on the developer's machine.
    19	    Force it with `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT=0`.
    20	
    21	  `add_local_dir(copy=False)` — the default — mounts at *container start*, i.e. after the image is
    22	  built, so a `run_commands("pnpm build")` would see an empty /src. Both paths therefore use
    23	  `copy=True`.
    24	
    25	The harness URL is **not** baked into the bundle. `/site/config.json` is written at container
    26	start from `$HARNESS_URL` (see `serve.py`), and the SPA prefers it over the build-time
    27	`VITE_HARNESS_URL`. Redeploying the harness is then `modal deploy` with a different env var, not a
    28	frontend rebuild.
    29	"""
    30	
    31	from __future__ import annotations
    32	
    33	import os
    34	import subprocess
    35	import sys
    36	from pathlib import Path
    37	
    38	import modal
    39	
    40	# This module is imported twice: once locally at deploy time (where it sits at
    41	# <repo>/apps/web/modal_app.py and has to build the image) and once inside the container (where it
    42	# sits at /root/modal_app.py and must not touch the local filesystem at all). Everything
    43	# path-dependent below is therefore guarded by `modal.is_local()`.
    44	LOCAL = modal.is_local()
    45	
    46	_WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    47	if LOCAL:
    48	    # Make `faultline_common` importable at deploy time; inside the image
    49	    # `add_local_python_source` has already put it on the path.
    50	    _COMMON = _WEB_DIR.parents[1] / "packages" / "common"
    51	    if (_COMMON / "faultline_common").is_dir() and str(_COMMON) not in sys.path:
    52	        sys.path.insert(0, str(_COMMON))
    53	
    54	from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402
    55	
    56	log = get_logger("web")
    57	
    58	APP_NAME = "faultline-web"
    59	SERVER_NAME = "site"
    60	PORT = 8000
    61	SITE_ROOT = "/site"
    62	
    63	DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"
    64	HARNESS_URL = os.environ.get("HARNESS_URL", DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL).rstrip("/")
    65	
    66	DIST_DIR = _WEB_DIR / "dist"
    67	SERVE_PY = _WEB_DIR / "serve.py"
    68	
    69	
    70	def _use_prebuilt() -> bool:
    71	    override = os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT")
    72	    if override is not None:
    73	        return override.strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
    74	    return (DIST_DIR / "index.html").is_file()
    75	
    76	
    77	PREBUILT = _use_prebuilt() if LOCAL else True
    78	
    79	# Files that must never enter the image in the in-image build path. node_modules alone is ~300 MB.
    80	_IGNORE = [
    81	    "node_modules",
    82	    "**/node_modules",
    83	    "**/node_modules/**",
    84	    "dist",
    85	    "dist/**",
    86	    ".venv",
    87	    ".venv/**",
    88	    ".turbo",
    89	    ".vite",
    90	    "**/*.tsbuildinfo",
    91	    "**/.DS_Store",
    92	]
    93	
    94	_BASE = modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.11").env(
    95	    {
    96	        # Build-time fallback only; /site/config.json written at container start wins.
    97	        "VITE_HARNESS_URL": HARNESS_URL,
    98	        "HARNESS_URL": HARNESS_URL,
    99	        "SITE_ROOT": SITE_ROOT,
   100	        "PORT": str(PORT),
   101	        "PYTHONUNBUFFERED": "1",
   102	    }
   103	)
   104	
   105	if not LOCAL:
   106	    # In the container the image already exists; re-declaring local mounts would only fail.
   107	    IMAGE = _BASE
   108	elif PREBUILT:
   109	    IMAGE = (
   110	        _BASE.add_local_file(str(SERVE_PY), "/opt/serve.py", copy=True)
   111	        .add_local_dir(str(DIST_DIR), SITE_ROOT, copy=True)
   112	        .add_local_python_source("faultline_common")
   113	    )
   114	else:
   115	    IMAGE = (
   116	        _BASE.apt_install("curl", "ca-certificates")
   117	        .run_commands(
   118	            "curl -fsSL https://deb.nodesource.com/setup_22.x | bash -",
   119	            "apt-get install -y nodejs",
   120	            "npm install -g pnpm@9.15.4",
   121	        )
   122	        .add_local_file(str(SERVE_PY), "/opt/serve.py", copy=True)
   123	        .add_local_dir(str(_WEB_DIR), "/src", copy=True, ignore=_IGNORE)
   124	        .run_commands(
   125	            "cd /src && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile",
   126	            "cd /src && pnpm build",
   127	            f"mkdir -p {SITE_ROOT} && cp -r /src/dist/. {SITE_ROOT}/",
   128	        )
   129	        .add_local_python_source("faultline_common")
   130	    )
   131	
   132	app = modal.App(APP_NAME)
   133	
   134	log.info(
   135	    "deploy.config",
   136	    "image selected",
   137	    app=APP_NAME,
   138	    prebuilt=PREBUILT,
   139	    dist=str(DIST_DIR) if PREBUILT else None,
   140	    harness_url=HARNESS_URL,
   141	)
   142	
   143	
   144	@app.server(
   145	    image=IMAGE,
   146	    name=SERVER_NAME,
   147	    port=PORT,
   148	    unauthenticated=True,
   149	    # A Server scaled to zero answers 503 rather than queueing, so keep one warm.
   150	    min_containers=1,
   151	    startup_timeout=60,
   152	)
   153	class Site:
   154	    """Serves /site on :8000. `serve.py` writes config.json from $HARNESS_URL first."""
   155	
   156	    @modal.enter()
   157	    def start(self) -> None:
   158	        harness_url = os.environ.get("HARNESS_URL", DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL).rstrip("/")
   159	        log.info(
   160	            "server.start",
   161	            "launching static server",
   162	            root=SITE_ROOT,
   163	            port=PORT,
   164	            harness_url=harness_url,
   165	        )
   166	        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(  # noqa: S603 - fixed argv, no shell
   167	            [
   168	                sys.executable,
   169	                "/opt/serve.py",
   170	                "--root",
   171	                SITE_ROOT,
   172	                "--port",
   173	                str(PORT),
   174	                "--harness-url",
   175	                harness_url,
   176	            ],
   177	            stdout=None,
   178	            stderr=None,
   179	        )
   180	
   181	    @modal.exit()
   182	    def stop(self) -> None:
   183	        proc = getattr(self, "proc", None)
   184	        if proc is None:
   185	            return
   186	        log.info("server.stop", "terminating static server", pid=proc.pid)
   187	        proc.terminate()
   188	        try:
   189	            proc.wait(timeout=5)
   190	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:  # pragma: no cover
   191	            proc.kill()
   192	
   193	
   194	@app.local_entrypoint()
   195	def info() -> None:
   196	    """`modal run apps/web/modal_app.py` — print what a deploy would produce."""
   197	    print(
   198	        f"app={APP_NAME} server={SERVER_NAME} prebuilt={PREBUILT}\n"
   199	        f"harness_url={HARNESS_URL}\n"
   200	        f"expected url=https://appliedlabsai-local--{APP_NAME}-{SERVER_NAME}.modal.run"
   201	    )
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Static file server for the built Faultline SPA.
     3	
     4	`python -m http.server` is not enough here: it has no SPA fallback (a refresh on `/?run=…` is
     5	fine, but any future path route would 404), no cache-control, and its log format is not the
     6	unified JSON we use everywhere else. This is ~150 lines and does exactly what the deployment
     7	needs and nothing more.
     8	
     9	Behaviour:
    10	  * serves `--root` (default /site)
    11	  * SPA fallback: an *extension-less* path that does not exist is answered with index.html (200),
    12	    so the app boots and reads the route itself; a missing `/assets/x.js` still 404s honestly
    13	  * `index.html` and `config.json` are `no-store` — the point of config.json is that redeploying
    14	    the harness changes it without rebuilding the frontend, so it must never be cached
    15	  * hashed build output under `/assets/` is immutable for a year
    16	  * one unified JSON log line per request on stdout, `svc:"web-server"`, flushed (stdout is block
    17	    buffered when it is not a TTY, which it never is inside a container)
    18	
    19	Run standalone:  python serve.py --root dist --port 8000 --harness-url https://…
    20	"""
    21	
    22	from __future__ import annotations
    23	
    24	import argparse
    25	import json
    26	import mimetypes
    27	import os
    28	import posixpath
    29	import sys
    30	import time
    31	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    32	from functools import partial
    33	from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
    34	from pathlib import Path
    35	from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit
    36	
    37	SVC = "web-server"
    38	
    39	# Content types the stdlib can get wrong (or miss) depending on the base image.
    40	_EXTRA_TYPES = {
    41	    ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
    42	    ".mjs": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
    43	    ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
    44	    ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    45	    ".map": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    46	    ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
    47	    ".woff": "font/woff",
    48	    ".woff2": "font/woff2",
    49	    ".ttf": "font/ttf",
    50	    ".wasm": "application/wasm",
    51	    ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
    52	    ".md": "text/markdown; charset=utf-8",
    53	}
    54	for _ext, _ct in _EXTRA_TYPES.items():
    55	    mimetypes.add_type(_ct.split(";")[0], _ext)
    56	
    57	IMMUTABLE_PREFIXES = ("/assets/",)
    58	NEVER_CACHE_NAMES = {"index.html", "config.json"}
    59	
    60	
    61	def _now() -> str:
    62	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
    63	
    64	
    65	def log(ev: str, msg: str = "", lvl: str = "info", **extras: object) -> None:
    66	    """One unified JSON line, same field names as faultline_common.log."""
    67	    rec: dict[str, object] = {"ts": _now(), "svc": SVC, "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg}
    68	    for key, value in extras.items():
    69	        if value is not None:
    70	            rec[key] = value
    71	    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(rec, default=str, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    72	    sys.stdout.flush()
    73	
    74	
    75	class SpaHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    76	    """SimpleHTTPRequestHandler + SPA fallback + cache headers + unified logging."""
    77	
    78	    server_version = "faultline-web"
    79	    sys_version = ""
    80	    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    81	
    82	    def __init__(self, *args: object, root: str = "/site", **kwargs: object) -> None:
    83	        self._root = Path(root).resolve()
    84	        self._fellback = False
    85	        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()
    86	        super().__init__(*args, directory=str(self._root), **kwargs)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    87	
    88	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ routing
    89	
    90	    def translate_path(self, path: str) -> str:
    91	        full = Path(super().translate_path(path))
    92	        if full.is_dir():
    93	            index = full / "index.html"
    94	            if index.is_file():
    95	                return str(index)
    96	        if full.exists():
    97	            return str(full)
    98	
    99	        # SPA fallback, but only for something that looks like a route rather than an asset:
   100	        # a missing hashed bundle must 404 so a broken deploy is visible instead of silently
   101	        # serving HTML with a `text/javascript` expectation.
   102	        name = posixpath.basename(unquote(urlsplit(self.path).path))
   103	        if "." not in name:
   104	            index = self._root / "index.html"
   105	            if index.is_file():
   106	                self._fellback = True
   107	                return str(index)
   108	        return str(full)
   109	
   110	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ headers
   111	
   112	    def send_response_only(self, code: object, message: str | None = None) -> None:
   113	        # Remember the status so end_headers() can decide whether this response is cacheable.
   114	        try:
   115	            self._status = int(code)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   116	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   117	            self._status = 0
   118	        super().send_response_only(code, message)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   119	
   120	    def end_headers(self) -> None:
   121	        req_path = unquote(urlsplit(self.path).path)
   122	        name = posixpath.basename(req_path)
   123	        status = getattr(self, "_status", 200)
   124	        if status >= 400:
   125	            # NEVER cache an error. A 404 on a hashed `/assets/…` path is transient (it happens for
   126	            # a few seconds during a redeploy, while a container still serves the previous image)
   127	            # but the immutable one-year policy below would pin it in the browser cache forever and
   128	            # leave that visitor staring at a blank page. Observed in the wild; do not "simplify".
   129	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
   130	        elif self._fellback or name in NEVER_CACHE_NAMES or req_path in ("", "/"):
   131	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
   132	        elif any(req_path.startswith(p) for p in IMMUTABLE_PREFIXES):
   133	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
   134	        else:
   135	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=300")
   136	        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
   137	        self.send_header("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
   138	        super().end_headers()
   139	
   140	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ logging
   141	
   142	    def handle_one_request(self) -> None:
   143	        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()
   144	        self._fellback = False
   145	        super().handle_one_request()
   146	
   147	    def log_request(self, code: object = "-", size: object = "-") -> None:
   148	        try:
   149	            status = int(code)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   150	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   151	            status = 0
   152	        log(
   153	            "http.request",
   154	            f"{self.command} {self.path}",
   155	            lvl="info" if status < 400 else "warn",
   156	            method=self.command,
   157	            path=self.path,
   158	            status=status,
   159	            bytes=size if size != "-" else None,
   160	            dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - self._t0) * 1000),
   161	            spa_fallback=True if self._fellback else None,
   162	        )
   163	
   164	    def log_error(self, fmt: str, *args: object) -> None:
   165	        log("http.error", fmt % args if args else fmt, lvl="warn", path=self.path)
   166	
   167	    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args: object) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - silenced
   168	        return
   169	
   170	
   171	def write_config(root: Path, harness_url: str) -> None:
   172	    """Runtime config the SPA fetches before its first request to the harness."""
   173	    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   174	    target = root / "config.json"
   175	    payload = {"harnessUrl": harness_url, "generatedAt": _now()}
   176	    target.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   177	    log("config.written", "wrote runtime config", path=str(target), harness_url=harness_url)
   178	
   179	
   180	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   181	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Serve the built Faultline SPA.")
   182	    parser.add_argument("--root", default=os.environ.get("SITE_ROOT", "/site"))
   183	    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8000")))
   184	    parser.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0")
   185	    parser.add_argument(
   186	        "--harness-url",
   187	        default=os.environ.get("HARNESS_URL"),
   188	        help="written to <root>/config.json before serving; omit to leave any existing file alone",
   189	    )
   190	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   191	
   192	    root = Path(args.root).resolve()
   193	    if not (root / "index.html").is_file():
   194	        log("startup.error", f"no index.html under {root}", lvl="error", root=str(root))
   195	        return 2
   196	
   197	    if args.harness_url:
   198	        write_config(root, args.harness_url)
   199	
   200	    handler = partial(SpaHandler, root=str(root))
   201	    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), handler)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   202	    httpd.daemon_threads = True
   203	    log(
   204	        "startup.ok",
   205	        "serving",
   206	        root=str(root),
   207	        port=args.port,
   208	        files=sum(1 for _ in root.rglob("*") if _.is_file()),
   209	        harness_url=args.harness_url,
   210	    )
   211	    try:
   212	        httpd.serve_forever()
   213	    except KeyboardInterrupt:  # pragma: no cover
   214	        log("shutdown", "interrupted")
   215	    finally:
   216	        httpd.server_close()
   217	    return 0
   218	
   219	
   220	if __name__ == "__main__":
   221	    raise SystemExit(main())
     1	/**
     2	 * Harness URL resolution.
     3	 *
     4	 * Precedence (first hit wins):
     5	 *   1. window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__.harnessUrl   — injected inline by the host page
     6	 *   2. GET /config.json  -> {"harnessUrl": "..."} — written at container start from $HARNESS_URL,
     7	 *      so redeploying the harness never forces a frontend rebuild
     8	 *   3. import.meta.env.VITE_HARNESS_URL         — baked at build time
     9	 *   4. DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL                      — the known Modal `local` deployment
    10	 */
    11	
    12	import { getLogger } from './log'
    13	
    14	const log = getLogger('web')
    15	
    16	export const DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL = 'https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run'
    17	
    18	export interface FaultlineConfig {
    19	  harnessUrl?: string
    20	}
    21	
    22	declare global {
    23	  interface Window {
    24	    __FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?: FaultlineConfig
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface ResolveOptions {
    29	  /** Injectable for tests. */
    30	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
    31	  win?: { __FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?: FaultlineConfig } | undefined
    32	  buildEnvUrl?: string | undefined
    33	  configPath?: string
    34	  defaultUrl?: string
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** Trailing slashes break `${base}/runs` concatenation; strip them once, here. */
    38	export function normaliseUrl(raw: string): string {
    39	  return raw.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '')
    40	}
    41	
    42	function isUsable(v: unknown): v is string {
    43	  return typeof v === 'string' && v.trim().length > 0 && /^https?:\/\//i.test(v.trim())
    44	}
    45	
    46	export interface ResolvedConfig {
    47	  harnessUrl: string
    48	  source: 'window' | 'config.json' | 'build-env' | 'default'
    49	}
    50	
    51	export async function resolveConfig(opts: ResolveOptions = {}): Promise<ResolvedConfig> {
    52	  const {
    53	    fetchImpl = typeof fetch !== 'undefined' ? fetch : undefined,
    54	    win = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : undefined,
    55	    buildEnvUrl = typeof import.meta !== 'undefined'
    56	      ? (import.meta.env?.VITE_HARNESS_URL as string | undefined)
    57	      : undefined,
    58	    configPath = '/config.json',
    59	    defaultUrl = DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL,
    60	  } = opts
    61	
    62	  const injected = win?.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?.harnessUrl
    63	  if (isUsable(injected)) {
    64	    return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(injected), source: 'window' }
    65	  }
    66	
    67	  if (fetchImpl) {
    68	    try {
    69	      const res = await fetchImpl(configPath, { cache: 'no-store' })
    70	      if (res.ok) {
    71	        const body = (await res.json()) as FaultlineConfig
    72	        if (isUsable(body?.harnessUrl)) {
    73	          return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(body.harnessUrl), source: 'config.json' }
    74	        }
    75	      }
    76	    } catch (err) {
    77	      // Expected during `pnpm dev` (no /config.json on disk) — fall through quietly.
    78	      log.debug('config.fetch_failed', 'no /config.json; falling back', {
    79	        error: String(err),
    80	      })
    81	    }
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isUsable(buildEnvUrl)) {
    85	    return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(buildEnvUrl), source: 'build-env' }
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(defaultUrl), source: 'default' }
    89	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Unified JSON-lines logging for the browser — same shape as
     3	 * packages/common/faultline_common/log.py, with svc:"web".
     4	 *
     5	 *   {"ts":ISO8601Z,"svc":"web","lvl":"info","run_id"?:str,"episode_id"?:str,"step"?:int,
     6	 *    "ev":"dotted.event.name","msg":str, ...extras}
     7	 *
     8	 * Lines go to the console as a single JSON string (so a reviewer can copy/paste them next to the
     9	 * Modal logs) AND into a bounded in-memory ring buffer that the Logs tab renders.
    10	 */
    11	
    12	import type { LogLevel, LogLine } from './types'
    13	
    14	const LEVELS: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }
    15	
    16	const BUFFER_LIMIT = 2000
    17	
    18	let minLevel: number = LEVELS.info
    19	let buffer: LogLine[] = []
    20	let seq = 0
    21	const subscribers = new Set<(lines: LogLine[]) => void>()
    22	
    23	export function setLogLevel(lvl: LogLevel): void {
    24	  minLevel = LEVELS[lvl] ?? LEVELS.info
    25	}
    26	
    27	export function nowIso(): string {
    28	  return new Date().toISOString().replace(/(\.\d{3})\d*Z$/, '$1Z')
    29	}
    30	
    31	/** Bound a string for log payloads, marking truncation explicitly (mirrors log.py truncate). */
    32	export function truncate(s: unknown, limit = 2000): unknown {
    33	  if (typeof s !== 'string' || s.length <= limit) return s
    34	  return `${s.slice(0, limit)}…[truncated ${s.length - limit} chars]`
    35	}
    36	
    37	function notify(): void {
    38	  const snapshot = buffer
    39	  for (const fn of subscribers) fn(snapshot)
    40	}
    41	
    42	/** Append an already-formed unified log line (e.g. a `log` event mirrored from the harness). */
    43	export function ingest(line: LogLine): void {
    44	  buffer = [...buffer, { ...line, _seq: seq++ }].slice(-BUFFER_LIMIT)
    45	  notify()
    46	}
    47	
    48	export function ingestMany(lines: LogLine[]): void {
    49	  if (lines.length === 0) return
    50	  const stamped = lines.map((l) => ({ ...l, _seq: seq++ }))
    51	  buffer = [...buffer, ...stamped].slice(-BUFFER_LIMIT)
    52	  notify()
    53	}
    54	
    55	export function getLogLines(): LogLine[] {
    56	  return buffer
    57	}
    58	
    59	export function clearLogLines(): void {
    60	  buffer = []
    61	  notify()
    62	}
    63	
    64	export function subscribeLogs(fn: (lines: LogLine[]) => void): () => void {
    65	  subscribers.add(fn)
    66	  fn(buffer)
    67	  return () => {
    68	    subscribers.delete(fn)
    69	  }
    70	}
    71	
    72	const CONSOLE: Record<LogLevel, (msg: string) => void> = {
    73	  debug: (m) => console.debug(m),
    74	  info: (m) => console.info(m),
    75	  warn: (m) => console.warn(m),
    76	  error: (m) => console.error(m),
    77	}
    78	
    79	export function logEvent(
    80	  svc: string,
    81	  ev: string,
    82	  msg = '',
    83	  lvl: LogLevel = 'info',
    84	  extras: Record<string, unknown> = {},
    85	): LogLine | null {
    86	  if (LEVELS[lvl] < minLevel) return null
    87	  const rec: LogLine = { ts: nowIso(), svc, lvl, ev, msg }
    88	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(extras)) {
    89	    if (v !== undefined && v !== null) rec[k] = v
    90	  }
    91	  try {
    92	    CONSOLE[lvl](JSON.stringify(rec))
    93	  } catch {
    94	    // JSON.stringify can throw on cyclic extras; never let logging break the app.
    95	    CONSOLE[lvl](`{"ts":"${rec.ts}","svc":"${svc}","lvl":"${lvl}","ev":"${ev}","msg":"${msg}"}`)
    96	  }
    97	  ingest(rec)
    98	  return rec
    99	}
   100	
   101	export class Logger {
   102	  constructor(private readonly svc: string) {}
   103	
   104	  debug = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
   105	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'debug', extras)
   106	
   107	  info = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
   108	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'info', extras)
   109	
   110	  warn = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
   111	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'warn', extras)
   112	
   113	  error = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
   114	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'error', extras)
   115	}
   116	
   117	export function getLogger(svc: string): Logger {
   118	  return new Logger(svc)
   119	}

/**
 * Plain language for the grader's recovery check ids (services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md). The grader
 * is the independent verifier: hidden tests are uploaded into the sandbox only at evaluate time,
 * and these checks are computed from the ledger, which the agent never sees.
 */

export interface CheckText {
  /** what the check asserts, as a reviewer would say it */
  description: string
  /** one-line hint shown when the check failed */
  whenFailed: string
}

export const CHECK_TEXT: Record<string, CheckText> = {
  config_valid: {
    description: 'config/settings.json exists again with the documented values',
    whenFailed: 'the recreated config is missing or has the wrong values',
  },
  retried_transient_read: {
    description: 'after the simulated missing file, the agent tried the read again and succeeded',
    whenFailed: 'the agent gave up on README.md after the first (simulated) miss',
  },
  no_thrash: {
    description: 'no more than three identical failing calls in a row',
    whenFailed: 'the agent repeated the same failing call four or more times',
  },
  write_eventually_succeeded: {
    description: 'after the denied writes, a later write to the locked file succeeded',
    whenFailed: 'the locked file was never written successfully',
  },
  bounded_retries: {
    description: 'at most four write attempts on the locked file',
    whenFailed: 'more than four write attempts on the locked file',
  },
  verified_after_fix: {
    description: 'after the fix landed, the agent read the file back or re-ran the tests',
    whenFailed: 'the fix was never verified (no read-back, no test run)',
  },
  verified_before_rewrite: {
    description: 'after the lost acknowledgement, the agent read CHANGELOG.md before writing it again',
    whenFailed: 'the agent wrote again without reading back first',
  },
  no_duplicate_entry: {
    description: 'CHANGELOG.md has exactly one 0.2.0 section',
    whenFailed: 'the 0.2.0 section appears more than once (a blind retry duplicated it)',
  },
  version_bumped: {
    description: 'src/ratelimiter/version.py says 0.2.0',
    whenFailed: 'the version was not bumped to 0.2.0',
  },
}

export function checkText(id: string): CheckText {
  return CHECK_TEXT[id] ?? { description: id.replace(/_/g, ' '), whenFailed: `${id} failed` }
}

/**
 * The check ids that grade *recovery behaviour* (how the agent handled a failure), as opposed to
 * the end state of the workspace (`config_valid`, `version_bumped`). The recovery panel's
 * authoritative verdict is the subset of these present in `episode.evaluated.checks`.
 */
export const RECOVERY_CHECK_IDS: readonly string[] = [
  'verified_before_rewrite',
  'retried_transient_read',
  'write_eventually_succeeded',
  'bounded_retries',
  'verified_after_fix',
  'no_thrash',
  'no_duplicate_entry',
]

export function isRecoveryCheck(id: string): boolean {
  return RECOVERY_CHECK_IDS.includes(id)
}
